  app.querySelectorAll('[data-area]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.alocSel=b.dataset.area;render();topAloc()});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-alq]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{NAV.length=0;openAlocEq(b.dataset.alq)});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-alocar]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{NAV.length=0;formAlocar()});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-aback]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const to=b.dataset.aback;S.alocSite=to;S.alocSel='';render();topAloc()});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-eq]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openEq(b.dataset.eq));
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-pend]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{NAV.length=0;openPend(b.dataset.pend)});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-pv]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const k=b.dataset.pv;S.pv[k]=!S.pv[k];render()});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-cron]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{NAV.length=0;openPend(b.dataset.cron,true)});app.querySelectorAll('[data-sif]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{NAV.length=0;openEq(b.dataset.sif)});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-tr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openTroca(b.dataset.tr));
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-hist]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{NAV.length=0;openHistEq(b.dataset.hist)});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-cartadd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const [k,id]=b.dataset.cartadd.split(':');
    if(k==='st'){const x=stById(id);addCart([cartFromSt(x)])}
    else{const e=eqById(id);const its=(e.itens||[]).filter(it=>{const c=itemStatus(e,it).cls;return c==='bad'||c==='warn'});addCart(its.map(it=>cartFromIt(it,e)))}});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-pntab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.pnTab=b.dataset.pntab;render()});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-mov]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formMov(b.dataset.mov));
  const esq=$('#esq');if(esq){
    const run=()=>{S.esQ=esq.value;const toks=esNorm(esq.value.trim()).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);let tot=0;
      /* sem busca, a listagem completa so aparece pelo botao Relatorio */
      const fil=!!S.esFil,blks=[...app.querySelectorAll('.es-blk')].filter(b=>b.classList.contains('es-res')===fil);
      if(fil)app.querySelectorAll('.es-blk:not(.es-res)').forEach(b=>b.hidden=true);
      if(!toks.length&&!S.esAll&&!fil){blks.forEach(blk=>blk.hidden=true);const z=$('#esq-0');if(z)z.hidden=true;return}
      blks.forEach(blk=>{let n=0,first=null;blk.querySelectorAll('[data-q]').forEach(r=>{const t=r.dataset.q,w=t.split(/[\s(),·–]+/),c=t.replace(/[\s.\-\/]/g,'');
        /* palavra comecando pelo termo; codigos com numeros tambem valem sem traco/ponto (cb05 = CB-05) */
        const ok=toks.every(k=>w.some(x=>x.startsWith(k))||(/\d/.test(k)&&c.includes(k.replace(/[.\-\/]/g,''))));r.hidden=!ok;r.classList.remove('es-first');if(ok){n++;if(!first){first=r;r.classList.add('es-first')}}});
        blk.hidden=!n;const em=blk.querySelector('.es-g em');if(em)em.textContent=n;tot+=n});
      const z=$('#esq-0');if(z)z.hidden=!!tot||(fil&&!blks.length);const en=$('#es-n');if(en&&fil)en.textContent=`${tot} ${tot===1?'item encontrado':'itens encontrados'}`};
    esq.oninput=run;const ec=$('#es-cam');if(ec)ec.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item){NAV.length=0;formMov(v.item.id)}else if(v.text){esq.value=v.text;run()}}});app.querySelectorAll('[data-estab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(b.dataset.estab==='rel'){S.esAll=!S.esAll;S.esFil=false;render();return}
      if(!S.esFil){S.esFil=true;S.esAll=false;render()}NAV.length=0;esFilSheet()});
    const ef=$('#es-fil');if(ef){ef.addEventListener('click',ev=>{if(ev.target.closest('#ef-clr')){S.esSel=esSelDef();render();return}
      const x=ev.target.closest('[data-tgx]');if(!x)return;const [k,v]=x.dataset.tgx.split(':'),E=S.esSel;
      if(k==='tag')E.tag='';else if(k==='sit')E.sit=['at'];else if(k==='ord')E.ord='az';else{const i=E[k].indexOf(v);if(i>=0)E[k].splice(i,1)}render()})}esq.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter')esq.blur()};
    esq.onfocus=()=>{S.esFoc=true};esq.onblur=()=>setTimeout(()=>{if(esq.isConnected)S.esFoc=false},0);
    if(S.esQ){esq.value=S.esQ;run()}
    if(S.esFoc){esq.focus({preventScroll:true});const n=esq.value.length;try{esq.setSelectionRange(n,n)}catch(e){}}}
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-a="up"]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    /* volta so uma tela: dentro da Alocacao sobe um nivel; senao volta ao inicio */
    if(S.focus==='aloc'&&S.alocSel){S.alocSel='';render();SCR().scrollTo(0,0);return}
    if(S.focus==='aloc'&&S.alocSite){S.alocSite='';render();SCR().scrollTo(0,0);return}
    /* dentro da Frota: volta para a Frota */
    if(FROTA_KIDS.includes(S.focus)){abreCard('frota');return}
    goHome()});
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-fr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>abreCard(b.dataset.fr));
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-a="home"]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.focus='';S.esQ='';S.esFoc=false;S.esAll=false;S.esFil=false;S.alocSite='';S.alocSel='';S.mf.equip='';S.showAllSched=false;S.showAllHist=false;S.pv={};try{localStorage.setItem('frota.focus','')}catch(e){}render();SCR().scrollTo(0,0)});
  const on=(k,fn)=>app.querySelectorAll(`[data-a="${k}"]`).forEach(b=>b.onclick=fn);
  on('new-tr',()=>{NAV.length=0;formRegistrar(S.mf.equip)});
  on('clear-ex',clearExamples);
  on('more-sched',()=>{S.showAllSched=!S.showAllSched;render()});on('more-hist',()=>{S.showAllHist=!S.showAllHist;render()});
  const pq=$('#pq');if(pq){const box=$('#pq-r');
    const norm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
    pq.oninput=()=>{const q=norm(pq.value.trim());if(!q){box.hidden=true;box.innerHTML='';return}
      const qs=q.replace(/[\s-]/g,'');
      const hits=S.eqCtl.filter(e=>{const k=norm(e.codigo).replace(/[\s-]/g,'');return k.includes(qs)||norm([e.tipo,e.marca,e.modelo].join(' ')).includes(q)}).sort((a,b)=>{const sa=norm(a.codigo).replace(/[\s-]/g,'').startsWith(qs)?0:1,sb=norm(b.codigo).replace(/[\s-]/g,'').startsWith(qs)?0:1;return sa-sb||byCode(a,b)}).slice(0,8);
      box.hidden=false;box.innerHTML=hits.length?hits.map(e=>`<button type="button" class="tq" data-pqid="${esc(e.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc(e.tipo)}${e.modelo?` · ${esc([e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' '))}`:''}</span>${I.chev}</button>`).join(''):'<div class="empty" style="border-radius:0">Nenhum equipamento encontrado.</div>';
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-pqid]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const id=b.dataset.pqid;pq.value='';box.hidden=true;box.innerHTML='';pq.blur();NAV.length=0;const tem=S.trocas.some(t=>t.equipId===id&&t.tipo!=='reprog'&&t.tipo!=='hmtroca');tem?openEq(id):openSemPrev(id)})};}
}

let sheetOpen=false,lastFocus=null;
/* texto longo num quadro de dados (observacoes, descricao) vai para baixo do rotulo, alinhado a esquerda */
function stackLong(root){requestAnimationFrame(()=>root.querySelectorAll('.spec .r:not(.ls):not(.stk)>b').forEach(v=>{if(v.querySelector('small,div,.cp,.sit,.pill'))return;if(v.getBoundingClientRect().height>26)v.parentElement.classList.add('stk')}))}
function openSheet({title,sub='',body,foot='',mount}){
  if(!sheetOpen)lastFocus=document.activeElement;
  $('#sheet').innerHTML=`<div class="sh-top"><div class="grab"></div><div class="sh-head"><button class="backbtn" data-back aria-label="Voltar" title="Voltar">${I.back}</button><div class="sh-tt"><h2>${/<[a-z]/i.test(title)?title:`<span class="tt-x">${title}</span>`}</h2>${sub?`<div class="sub">${sub}</div>`:''}</div></div></div>
    <div class="sh-body">${body}</div>${foot?`<div class="sh-foot">${foot}</div>`:''}`;
  $('#scrim').hidden=false;sheetOpen=true;document.documentElement.style.overflow='hidden';
  $('#sheet').querySelectorAll('[data-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=closeSheet);
  $('#sheet').querySelectorAll('[data-back],[data-k="back"]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=goBack);
  mount&&mount($('#sheet'));$('#sheet').querySelectorAll('select[data-eqs]').forEach(eqSearch);$('#sheet [data-back]').focus({preventScroll:true});
  stackLong($('#sheet'));
}
const NAV=[];
function nav(key,fn){const i=NAV.findIndex(n=>n.key===key);if(i>=0)NAV.length=i;NAV.push({key,fn})}
function goBack(){dropPending();NAV.pop();const prev=NAV[NAV.length-1];if(prev)prev.fn();else closeSheet()}
function after(fn){NAV.pop();fn()}
function finishReg(eqId){const i=NAV.findIndex(n=>n.key.startsWith('eq:'));const j=NAV.findIndex(n=>n.key==='reg');const cut=j>=0?j:NAV.length-1;
  if(i>=0&&i<cut){NAV.length=i+1;NAV[i].fn()}else if(cut>0){NAV.length=cut;NAV[cut-1].fn()}else closeSheet()}
let rsT;function refreshSheet(){clearTimeout(rsT);rsT=setTimeout(()=>{const top=NAV[NAV.length-1];if(!sheetOpen||!top||!/^(eq|tr|lst|mv|pd|cr|cart|listas|alq|sp|avs|av):/.test(top.key))return;const ae=document.activeElement;if(ae&&ae.closest&&ae.closest('#sheet')&&/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(ae.tagName)&&ae.value)return;
  const b=$('#sheet .sh-body'),y=b?b.scrollTop:0;top.fn();const nb=$('#sheet .sh-body');if(nb)nb.scrollTop=y},60)}
function closeSheet(){dropPending();NAV.length=0;$('#scrim').hidden=true;$('#sheet').innerHTML='';sheetOpen=false;document.documentElement.style.overflow='';render();lastFocus&&lastFocus.focus&&lastFocus.focus({preventScroll:true})}
$('#scrim').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='scrim')closeSheet()});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&sheetOpen)goBack()});
/* botao/gesto voltar do celular: volta uma tela dentro do app; na tela inicial pede um segundo toque para sair */
(function(){let armed=false,exitT=0;
  const arm=()=>{if(armed)return;try{history.pushState({frota:1},'');armed=true}catch(e){}};
  const inner=()=>{if(pickPhoto.close&&document.querySelector('.pk-w')){pickPhoto.close();return true}if(document.querySelector('.rdr')){rdClose();return true}const dv=document.querySelector('.docv');
    if(dv){if(dv.classList.contains('zoom'))dv.classList.remove('zoom');else{const x=dv.querySelector('[data-x]');x?x.click():dv.remove()}return true}
    if(sheetOpen){goBack();return true}
    if(S.focus){const up=document.querySelector('#app [data-a="up"]');if(up)up.click();else goHome();return true}
    return false};
  window.addEventListener('popstate',()=>{armed=false;
    if(inner()){arm();return}
    /* ja na tela inicial: o proximo voltar fecha; se nao vier em 2,5 s, volta a proteger */
    toast('Toque voltar de novo para sair');clearTimeout(exitT);exitT=setTimeout(arm,2500)});
  try{history.replaceState({frota:0},'')}catch(e){}arm();
  /* garante a protecao depois do primeiro toque, se o navegador ignorou o pushState inicial */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',arm,{passive:true});
})();
function confirmRemovals(box,removed,proceed){
  if(!removed.length){proceed();return}
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="confirm"><span>Voce esta excluindo: <b>${removed.map(esc).join(', ')}</b>. Confirma e salva?</span><div class="b"><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-no>Revisar</button><button type="button" class="btn sm danger" data-yes>Excluir e salvar</button></div></div>`;
  box.querySelector('[data-no]').onclick=()=>box.innerHTML='';box.querySelector('[data-yes]').onclick=()=>{box.innerHTML='';proceed()};
  box.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});
}
function askConfirm(box,label,text,fn){
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="confirm"><span>${text}</span><div class="b"><button class="btn sm" data-no>Cancelar</button><button class="btn sm pri" data-yes>${label}</button></div></div>`;
  box.querySelector('[data-no]').onclick=()=>box.innerHTML='';box.querySelector('[data-yes]').onclick=fn;
  box.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});
}
function askDelete(box,text,fn){
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="confirm"><span>${text}</span><div class="b"><button class="btn sm" data-no>Cancelar</button><button class="btn sm danger" data-yes>Excluir</button></div></div>`;
  box.querySelector('[data-no]').onclick=()=>box.innerHTML='';box.querySelector('[data-yes]').onclick=fn;
  box.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});
}
async function safe(fn,okMsg){
  try{await fn();okMsg&&toast(okMsg);return true}
  catch(e){toast(e&&e.code==='invalid_argument'?'Voce nao tem permissao para editar esta frota.':e&&e.code==='quota_exceeded'?'Limite de registros atingido. Apague itens antigos.':'Nao foi possivel salvar. Tente de novo.');console.error(e);return false}
}

const ICON_TAG='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 12V4h8l10 10-8 8z"/><circle cx="7.5" cy="8.5" r="1.3"/></svg>';
const ICON_FLAG='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 21V4M5 4h11l-2 4 2 4H5"/></svg>';
const LISTAS={cats:{t:'Categorias de itens',one:'categoria',ph:'Ex.: Correia',campo:'tipo',ic:ICON_TAG,est:1,f:1},donos:{t:'Proprietarios',one:'proprietario',ph:'Nome do proprietario',campo:'locador',ic:ICON_USER},
  tipos:{t:'Tipos de equipamento',one:'tipo',ph:'Ex.: Caminhao munck',campo:'tipo',ic:ICON_TAG},
  locais:{t:'Locais de alocacao',one:'local',ph:'Ex.: PQ',campo:'alocacao',ic:null},
  areas:{t:'Areas do DM1',one:'area',ph:'Ex.: Planta',campo:'area',ic:null},
  frentes:{t:'Frentes de trabalho',one:'frente',ph:'Ex.: Britador',campo:'frente',ic:ICON_FLAG}};
function openMenu(mode){
  nav('menu'+(mode?':'+mode:''),()=>openMenu(mode));
  const mi=(a,ic,t)=>`<button class="mi mi1" data-m="${a}"><span class="ic">${ic}</span><span class="tx"><b>${t}</b></span>${I.chev}</button>`;
  const JOIN_IC='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 4v5a4 4 0 0 0 4 4h6"/><path d="M19 4v5a4 4 0 0 1-4 4"/><path d="M12 13v7"/><path d="M9 17l3 3 3-3"/></svg>';
  const CART_IC=document.querySelector('#cartb svg').outerHTML;
  const MV_IN='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 4v11"/><path d="M7 10l5 5 5-5"/><path d="M4 20h16"/></svg>',MV_OUT='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 15V4"/><path d="M7 9l5-5 5 5"/><path d="M4 20h16"/></svg>';
  const DEL='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M6 7l1 13h10l1-13"/></svg>';
  const EDI='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 20h4L19 9l-4-4L4 16z"/><path d="M13.5 6.5l4 4"/></svg>';
  const DB_IC='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><ellipse cx="12" cy="5.5" rx="7" ry="2.5"/><path d="M5 5.5v6c0 1.4 3.1 2.5 7 2.5s7-1.1 7-2.5v-6"/><path d="M5 11.5v6c0 1.4 3.1 2.5 7 2.5s7-1.1 7-2.5v-6"/></svg>';
  const BK_IC='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 4v11M7 10l5 5 5-5M4 20h16"/></svg>';
  const g=t=>`<div class="mgrp">${t}</div>`;
  /* + abre por area: Frota · Estoque · Combustivel · Ajustes do app (sugestao A) */
  const T={
    '':{title:'Menu',sub:'Escolha a area',body:mi('frota',I.equip,'Frota','Preventivas, equipamentos e horimetro')+mi('est',I.estoque,'Estoque','Entrada, saida, filtros, oleos, kits e compras')+mi('comb',I.comb,'Combustivel','Abastecimentos')+mi('aj',DB_IC,'Ajustes do app','Listas do app e copia de seguranca')},
    frota:{title:'Frota',sub:'Preventivas, equipamentos e horimetro',body:mi('x:eq',I.equip,'Equipamentos','Novo, editar ou excluir')+mi('reg',I.manut,'Preventiva','Troca de oleos e filtros, ou filtros de ar')+mi('thm',I.manut,'Troca de horimetro','O relogio de horas foi substituido')
      +g('Correcoes')+mi('thm-x',I.back,'Desfazer troca de horimetro','Volta a contagem do relogio antigo')},
    est:{title:'Estoque',sub:'Movimento e cadastro de itens',body:mi('mv-entrada',MV_IN,'Entrada no estoque','Itens que chegaram ao almoxarifado')+mi('mv-saida',MV_OUT,'Saida do estoque','Retirada avulsa, fora das preventivas')
      +mi('x:st',I.estoque,'Filtros e oleos','Novo, editar ou excluir')+mi('x:kit',KIT_IC,'Kits','Novo, editar ou excluir')+mi('cart',CART_IC,'Listas de compra','Listas concluidas: compartilhar, excluir ou mesclar')},
    comb:{title:'Combustivel',sub:'Abastecimentos',body:mi('abast',I.comb,'Abastecimento','Litros e horimetro para o consumo por hora')+g('Correcoes')+mi('dl-ab',DEL,'Excluir abastecimento','Apaga o ultimo abastecimento de um equipamento')},
    aj:{title:'Ajustes do app',sub:'Listas e copia de seguranca',body:g('Listas')+mi('l:donos',ICON_USER,'Proprietarios','Donos dos equipamentos da frota')+mi('l:tipos',ICON_TAG,'Tipos de equipamento','Categorias da frota')+mi('l:cats',ICON_TAG,'Categorias de itens','Filtro, oleo, graxa, correia e outros do estoque')+mi('l:locais',I.aloc,'Locais de alocacao','Ex.: DM1, PQ')+mi('l:areas',I.aloc,'Areas do DM1','Ex.: Planta, Mina')+mi('l:frentes',ICON_FLAG,'Frentes de trabalho','Ex.: Britador, Geral')
      +g('Dados')+mi('backup',BK_IC,'Copia de seguranca','Baixa um arquivo com todos os dados do app')}};
  /* tela limpa: Novo · Editar · Excluir */
  const X=(t,k,n)=>({title:t,sub:'O que voce quer fazer?',body:mi('eq'===k?'eq':k==='st'?'st':'kit',I.plus,'Novo','Cadastrar '+n)+mi('ed-'+k,EDI,'Editar','Alterar um cadastro existente')+mi('dl-'+k,DEL,'Excluir','Apagar um cadastro')});
  T['x:eq']=X('Equipamentos','eq','equipamento');T['x:st']=X('Filtros e oleos','st','item do estoque');T['x:kit']=X('Kits','kit','kit');
  const v=T[mode||'']||T[''];
  openSheet({title:v.title,sub:v.sub,body:`<div class="menu">${v.body}</div>`,
    mount:sh=>sh.querySelectorAll('[data-m]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const m=b.dataset.m;
      if(T[m])return openMenu(m);
      if(m.startsWith('l:'))return formLista(m.slice(2),'tudo');
      if(m.startsWith('g:'))return formGerir(m.slice(2));
      if(m.startsWith('mv-')){formMovEst.done=null;formMovEst.d&&(formMovEst.d.cur=null,formMovEst.d.q='');return formMovEst(m.slice(3))}
      const A={backup:()=>backupAll(b),abast:formAbast,cart:openListas,'dl-ab':openDelAbast,reg:()=>formRegistrar('',true),eq:()=>formEq(),thm:()=>formTrocaHm(),'thm-x':openDesfazHm,'ed-tr':formPickTr,st:()=>formStock(),kit:()=>formKit(),
        'ed-eq':()=>formPickEdit('eq'),'ed-st':()=>formPickEdit('st'),'ed-kit':()=>formPickEdit('kit'),'dl-eq':()=>formPickDel('eq'),'dl-tr':()=>formPickDel('tr'),'dl-st':()=>formPickDel('st'),'dl-kit':()=>formPickDel('kit')};
      A[m]&&A[m]()})});
}
/* ---------- + › Excluir cadastro ---------- */
function formPickDel(kind){
  nav('pkd:'+kind,()=>formPickDel(kind));
  const norm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase();
  let all,row,title,ph,match;
  if(kind==='eq'){all=S.equipsAll.slice().sort(byCode);title='Excluir equipamento';ph='TAG, tipo ou modelo';
    row=x=>`<button type="button" class="tq" data-pd="${esc(x.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(x.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc([x.marca,x.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'—')}${x.status==='inativo'?' · inativo':''}</span>${I.chev}</button>`;
    match=(x,t)=>norm([x.codigo,x.tipo,x.marca,x.modelo].join(' ')).includes(t)||norm(x.codigo).replace(/[\s-]/g,'').includes(t)}
  else if(kind==='st'||kind==='kit'){const K=kind==='kit';all=S.estoqueAll.filter(x=>isKit(x)===K).sort(byCode);title=K?'Excluir kit':'Excluir filtros e oleos';ph=K?'Codigo ou descricao':'Codigo, referencia ou descricao';
    row=x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-pd="${esc(x.id)}"`});
    match=(x,t)=>norm([x.codigo,x.ref,x.desc].join(' ')).includes(t)}
  else{all=S.trocas.filter(t=>t.tipo!=='hmtroca').sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||''));title='Excluir preventiva';ph='TAG do equipamento';
    row=x=>{const e=eqById(x.equipId);return `<button type="button" class="tq" data-pd="${esc(x.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e?e.codigo:'—')}</span><span class="tq-m">${tTit(x)}</span><span class="tq-d">${fdate(x.data)}</span>${I.chev}</button>`};
    match=(x,t)=>{const e=eqById(x.equipId),c=norm(e?e.codigo:'');return c.includes(t)||c.replace(/[\s-]/g,'').includes(t)}}
  const draw=l=>kind==='eq'?porTipo(l,row):l.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${l.map(row).join('')}</div>`:'';
  openSheet({title,sub:'Toque no item para excluir',
  body:`<div class="search">${I.search}<input id="pd-q" type="search" placeholder="${ph}" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Buscar"></div>${kind==='st'||kind==='kit'?rdBtn('pd-cam'):''}
    <div id="pd-l" style="margin-top:8px">${draw(all)||'<div class="empty">Nada cadastrado.</div>'}</div>`,
  mount:sh=>{const l=sh.querySelector('#pd-l'),q=sh.querySelector('#pd-q');
    const bind=()=>l.querySelectorAll('[data-pd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openDelete(kind,b.dataset.pd));
    q.oninput=()=>{const t=norm(q.value.trim()),tc=t.replace(/[\s-]/g,'');l.innerHTML=draw(all.filter(x=>!t||match(x,t)||match(x,tc)))||'<div class="empty">Nada encontrado.</div>';bind()};bind();
    const pc=sh.querySelector('#pd-cam');if(pc)pc.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item&&all.some(x=>x.id===v.item.id))openDelete(kind,v.item.id);else{q.value=v.item?stCod(v.item):v.text||'';q.oninput()}}})}});
}
/* o que prende um cadastro: com qualquer registro ligado, ele nao pode ser excluido (so desativado) */
async function vinculos(kind,id){const v=[],pl=(n,a,b)=>n+' '+(n===1?a:b);
  if(kind==='eq'){const e=eqById(id)||{},tr=S.trocas.filter(t=>t.equipId===id),n=f=>tr.filter(f).length;
    const pv=n(t=>!['reprog','hmtroca','filtro_ar'].includes(t.tipo)),fa=n(t=>t.tipo==='filtro_ar'),rp=n(t=>t.tipo==='reprog'),hm=n(t=>t.tipo==='hmtroca');
    if(pv)v.push(pl(pv,'preventiva','preventivas'));if(fa)v.push(pl(fa,'troca de filtro de ar','trocas de filtro de ar'));if(rp)v.push(pl(rp,'reprogramacao','reprogramacoes'));if(hm)v.push(pl(hm,'troca de horimetro','trocas de horimetro'));
    if(e.combBase||e.combUltData)v.push('abastecimentos');if(e.horimetroData)v.push('leitura de horimetro');if(e.alocacao)v.push('alocacao');
    try{const q=await S.db.collection('movs').where('equipId','==',id).get();if(q.size)v.push(pl(q.size,'movimento de estoque','movimentos de estoque'))}catch(x){}
    if((S.avisos||[]).some(a=>JSON.stringify(a).includes('"'+id+'"')))v.push('avisos');
    const li=(e.itens||[]).filter(i=>i.estoqueId).length;if(li)v.push(pl(li,'item do plano ligado ao estoque','itens do plano ligados ao estoque'))}
  else{const s=stById(id)||{};const u=S.trocas.filter(t=>(t.itens||[]).some(i=>i.estoqueId===id)).length;if(u)v.push('uso em '+pl(u,'preventiva','preventivas'));
    try{const q=await S.db.collection('movs').where('estoqueId','==',id).get();if(q.size)v.push(pl(q.size,'movimento','movimentos'))}catch(x){}
    const pq=S.equipsAll.filter(e=>(e.itens||[]).some(i=>i.estoqueId===id)).length;if(pq)v.push('plano de '+pl(pq,'equipamento','equipamentos'));
    const kt=S.estoqueAll.filter(k=>isKit(k)&&(k.comps||[]).some(c=>c.id===id)).length;if(kt)v.push(pl(kt,'kit','kits'));
    const en=(s.encaixe||[]).length;if(en)v.push('encaixe em '+pl(en,'equipamento','equipamentos'));if((Number(s.qtd)||0)!==0)v.push('saldo em estoque')}
  return v}
/* ativo / inativo: botao no cadastro (nunca caixa de marcar) */
const ehInativo=(kind,o)=>kind==='eq'?o.status==='inativo':!!o.inativo;
const atNome=k=>k==='eq'?'equipamento':k==='kit'?'kit':'item';
function atBox(kind,o){if(!o||!o.id)return '';const ina=ehInativo(kind,o),nome=atNome(kind),onde=kind==='eq'?' e do cronograma':' e dos alertas de estoque';
  return `<div class="fset at-f"><h3>Cadastro</h3><p class="note" style="margin:0 0 10px">${ina?`Este ${nome} esta <b>inativo</b>: fora das listas, buscas${onde}. O historico continua.`:`Este ${nome} esta <b>ativo</b>. Desativado, sai das listas, buscas${onde}; o historico continua.`}</p>
    <button type="button" class="btn ${ina?'pri':''} block" data-at="${ina?'on':'off'}">${ina?'Reativar':'Desativar'} ${nome}</button><div class="at-ask"></div></div>`}
function atBind(sh,kind,id){const b=sh.querySelector('[data-at]');if(!b)return;b.onclick=()=>{const on=b.dataset.at==='on',o=kind==='eq'?eqById(id):stById(id);if(!o)return;const nome=kind==='eq'?o.codigo:stCod(o),onde=kind==='eq'?' e do cronograma':' e dos alertas de estoque';
  const bx=sh.querySelector('.at-ask');bx.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask" style="margin-top:10px"><b>${on?'Reativar':'Desativar'} ${esc(nome)}?</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">${on?'Volta para as listas, buscas'+onde.replace(' e do',' e o').replace(' e dos',' e os')+'.':'Sai das listas, buscas'+onde+'. Nada do historico e apagado.'}</p><div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Cancelar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>${on?'Reativar':'Desativar'}</button></div></div>`;
  bx.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=()=>bx.innerHTML='';bx.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});
  bx.querySelector('[data-go]').onclick=async()=>{const upd=kind==='eq'?{status:on?'ativo':'inativo'}:{inativo:!on};
    if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc((kind==='eq'?'equipamentos/':'estoque/')+id).update(upd),on?'Reativado':'Desativado'))goBack()}}}
function openDelete(kind,id){
  nav('del:'+kind+':'+id,()=>openDelete(kind,id));
  let title,info,warn,run;
  if(kind==='eq'){const e=eqById(id);if(!e){goBack();return}const n=S.trocas.filter(t=>t.equipId===id).length;
    title=`<span class="code" style="font-size:16px">${esc(e.codigo)}</span>${esc(e.tipo)}`;
    info=`<div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Marca / modelo</span><b>${esc([e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'—')}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Alocado em</span><b>${esc(alocTxt(e)||'—')}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Oleos e filtros no plano</span><b>${(e.itens||[]).length}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Preventivas registradas</span><b>${n}</b></div></div>`;
    warn=`Excluir ${esc(e.codigo)}? ${n?`As ${n} preventivas registradas dele tambem serao apagadas. `:''}Nao da para desfazer.`;
    run=async()=>{for(const t of S.trocas.filter(t=>t.equipId===id))await S.db.doc('trocas/'+t.id).delete();await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+id).delete();dropAsset(e.foto);DOCS.forEach(([k])=>{const d=e[k];if(d&&d.id)[d.id,...(d.pages||[])].filter((x,i,a)=>a.indexOf(x)===i).forEach(dropAsset)})}}
  else if(kind==='st'||kind==='kit'){const s=stById(id);if(!s){goBack();return}const used=S.equips.filter(e=>(e.itens||[]).some(i=>i.estoqueId===id)).length;
    title=`<span class="code" style="font-size:16px">${esc(stCod(s))}</span>${esc(s.desc)}`;
    info=`<div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Referencia</span><b>${esc(s.ref||'—')}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Saldo</span><b>${nf(stQtd(s),1)} ${esc(stUn(s))}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Ligado a planos</span><b>${used} equip.</b></div></div>`;
    const kits=S.estoque.filter(k=>isKit(k)&&(k.comps||[]).some(c=>c.id===id));
    if(isKit(s))info=`<div class="spec">${specList('Itens no kit',kitComps(s).map(x=>[x.s.desc,stCod(x.s),nf(x.c.qtd,1)+' '+(x.s.un||'')]))}<div class="r"><span>Ligado a planos</span><b>${used} equip.</b></div></div>`;
    else if(kits.length)info=info.replace(/<\/div>$/,`<div class="r"><span>Faz parte dos kits</span><b>${kits.map(k=>esc(k.codigo)).join(', ')}</b></div></div>`);
    warn=isKit(s)?`Excluir este kit? Os itens dele continuam no estoque.${used?' Os planos ligados ao kit ficam sem baixa automatica.':''} Nao da para desfazer.`
      :`Excluir este item?${kits.length?` Ele sai tambem ${kits.length===1?'do kit':'dos kits'} ${kits.map(k=>k.codigo).join(', ')}.`:''}${used?' Os planos ligados a ele ficam sem baixa automatica.':''} Nao da para desfazer.`;
    run=async()=>{for(const k of kits)await S.db.doc('estoque/'+k.id).update({comps:(k.comps||[]).filter(c=>c.id!==id)});
      /* os planos que usavam o item ficam sem vinculo; ele sai tambem do carrinho */
      for(const e of S.equips.filter(e=>(e.itens||[]).some(i=>i.estoqueId===id)))await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({itens:e.itens.map(i=>i.estoqueId===id?{...i,estoqueId:''}:i)});
      if(S.cart.some(c=>c.estoqueId===id))await saveCart(S.cart.filter(c=>c.estoqueId!==id));
      await S.db.doc('estoque/'+id).delete();(s.fotos||[]).forEach(dropAsset)}}
  else{const t=S.trocas.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!t){goBack();return}const e=eqById(t.equipId);
    title=t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?`Filtro de ar · ${esc(t.sub)}`:t.tipo==='reprog'?`Reprogramacao de ${fdate(t.data)}`:t.tipo==='hmtroca'?`Troca de horimetro de ${fdate(t.data)}`:`Preventiva de ${fdate(t.data)}`;
    info=`<div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Equipamento</span><b>${esc(e?e.codigo:'—')}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Data</span><b>${fdate(t.data)}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Horimetro</span><b>${nf(t.horimetro)} h</b></div>${specList('Itens trocados',(t.itens||[]).map(i=>[itNome(i),itCod(i),i.qtd?nf(i.qtd,1)+' '+(i.un||''):'']))}</div>`;
    warn=t.tipo==='reprog'?'Excluir esta reprogramacao? O item volta ao vencimento anterior. Nao da para desfazer.':'Excluir este registro? O plano do equipamento e o estoque nao voltam sozinhos. Nao da para desfazer.';
    run=async()=>{if(t.tipo==='reprog'&&e){const m={};(t.itens||[]).forEach(i=>m[i.pid]=i);
        const its=e.itens.map(i=>{const r=m[i.pid];return r&&Number(i.proxH)===Number(r.para)?{...i,proxH:r.proxAnt??'',reprog:''}:i});
        await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({itens:its})}
      await S.db.doc('trocas/'+id).delete()}}
  openSheet({title,sub:'Confira antes de excluir',body:info+'<div id="delbox"></div>',
    foot:`<button type="button" class="btn danger" id="dl-go" style="flex:1">Excluir</button>`,
    mount:sh=>{sh.querySelector('#dl-go').onclick=async()=>{const bx=sh.querySelector('#delbox');
      if(kind!=='tr'){const v=await vinculos(kind,id);if(v.length){const o=kind==='eq'?eqById(id):stById(id),nome=kind==='eq'?o.codigo:stCod(o),ina=ehInativo(kind,o);
        bx.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask"><b>Nao pode ser excluido</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">${esc(nome)} tem ${esc(v.join(', '))}. Cadastros com registros ligados nao podem ser apagados.${ina?' Ele ja esta inativo.':' Use Desativar: sai das listas e o historico continua.'}</p><div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Fechar</button>${ina?'':'<button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>Desativar</button>'}</div></div>`;
        bx.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=()=>bx.innerHTML='';const g=bx.querySelector('[data-go]');if(g)g.onclick=async()=>{if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc((kind==='eq'?'equipamentos/':'estoque/')+id).update(kind==='eq'?{status:'inativo'}:{inativo:true}),'Desativado'))goBack()};
        bx.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});return}}
      askDelete(bx,warn,async()=>{if(await safe(run,'Excluido'))goBack()})}}});
}
const CORE={cats:['Filtro','Oleo','Outros'],locais:['DM1','PQ'],areas:['Planta','Mina'],frentes:['UG','Geral']};
const isCore=(k,v)=>(CORE[k]||[]).includes(v);
function formLista(k,mode='editar'){
  nav('lst:'+k+':'+mode,()=>formLista(k,mode));
  const L=LISTAS[k],list=cfgNow()[k];
  const uso=v=>L.est?S.estoqueAll.filter(s=>catGrp(s)===catKey(v)).length:S.equips.filter(e=>e[L.campo]===v).length;
  const UNL=['un','L','kg','m'],unSel=(v,attr)=>`<select ${attr} aria-label="Unidade">${[...new Set([...UNL,CATUN[v]||'un'])].map(u=>`<option${(CATUN[v]||'un')===u?' selected':''}>${u}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  openSheet({title:L.t,sub:`${list.length} cadastrado${list.length===1?'':'s'}`,
  body:`${mode==='novo'||mode==='tudo'?'':'<!--'}<form id="fl" class="fset"><label class="f">${L.f?'Nova':'Novo'} ${L.one}<div class="lst-add"><input id="l-v" required placeholder="${esc(L.ph)}" autocomplete="off">${L.est?unSel('','id="l-u" class="l-u"'):''}<button class="btn pri">Adicionar</button></div></label></form>${mode==='novo'||mode==='tudo'?'':'-->'}
    <div class="lbl">Cadastrados</div>${mode==='novo'?'':`<p class="note" style="margin:0 2px 8px">Toque num nome para ${mode==='excluir'?'excluir':mode==='tudo'?'renomear ou excluir':'renomear'}.</p>`}
    <div class="list">${list.map((v,i)=>[v,i]).sort((a,b)=>_an(a[0],b[0])).map(([v,i])=>`<div class="lst-item" data-i="${i}"><button class="lst-row" data-sel="${i}" aria-expanded="false"${mode==='novo'?' disabled':''}><span>${esc(v)}</span><em>${uso(v)} ${L.est?(uso(v)===1?'item':'itens')+' · '+esc(CATUN[v]||'un'):'equip.'}</em>${I.chev}</button>
      <div class="lst-act" hidden>${L.est?`<label class="lst-un">Unidade ${unSel(v,`data-cu="${i}"`)}</label>`:''}${isCore(k,v)?'<span class="note" style="margin-right:auto">Nome usado pela estrutura do app; nao pode ser alterado.</span>':(mode==='excluir'?`<button class="btn sm danger" data-rm="${i}">Excluir</button>`:mode==='tudo'?`<button class="btn sm danger" data-rm="${i}">Excluir</button><button class="btn sm" data-ren="${i}">Renomear</button>`:`<button class="btn sm" data-ren="${i}">Renomear</button>`)}</div><div class="lst-conf"></div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">Nenhum cadastrado.</div>'}</div>`,
  mount:sh=>{
    const save=async(arr,msg,ex)=>{const c={...cfgNow(),[k]:arr,...(ex||{})};applyCfg(c);if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('config/listas').set(c),msg))formLista(k,mode)};
    const fl=sh.querySelector('#fl');if(fl)fl.onsubmit=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();const v=sh.querySelector('#l-v').value.trim();if(!v)return;
      if(list.some(x=>x.toLowerCase()===v.toLowerCase())){toast('Esse nome ja esta cadastrado.');return}
      if(L.est){const p=list.indexOf('Outros'),arr=p<0?[...list,v]:[...list.slice(0,p),v,...list.slice(p)];save(arr,`${v} cadastrada`,{catUn:{...CATUN,[v]:sh.querySelector('#l-u').value}});return}
      save([...list,v],`${v} cadastrado`)};
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-cu]').forEach(sel=>sel.onchange=()=>{const v=list[+sel.dataset.cu];save(list,`Unidade de ${v}: ${sel.value}`,{catUn:{...CATUN,[v]:sel.value}})});
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-sel]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const it=b.closest('.lst-item'),open=b.getAttribute('aria-expanded')!=='true';
      sh.querySelectorAll('.lst-item').forEach(x=>{x.querySelector('.lst-row').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');x.querySelector('.lst-act').hidden=true;x.querySelector('.lst-conf').innerHTML=''});
      if(open){b.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');it.querySelector('.lst-act').hidden=false}});
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-ren]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const it=b.closest('.lst-item'),v=list[+b.dataset.ren],n=uso(v);it.querySelector('.lst-act').hidden=true;
      const box=it.querySelector('.lst-conf');box.innerHTML=`<div class="ren"><label class="f">Novo nome<input class="ren-in" value="${esc(v)}" autocomplete="off"></label>${n?`<p class="note" style="margin:0">Os ${n} ${L.est?'itens':'equipamentos'} que usam “${esc(v)}” serao atualizados.</p>`:''}<div class="b"><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-no>Cancelar</button><button type="button" class="btn sm pri" data-yes>Salvar nome</button></div></div>`;
      const inp=box.querySelector('.ren-in');inp.focus();inp.select();
      box.querySelector('[data-no]').onclick=()=>{box.innerHTML='';it.querySelector('.lst-act').hidden=false};
      box.querySelector('[data-yes]').onclick=async()=>{const nv=inp.value.trim();if(!nv||nv===v){box.innerHTML='';return}
        if(list.some(x=>x!==v&&x.toLowerCase()===nv.toLowerCase())){toast('Esse nome ja esta cadastrado.');return}
        const c={...cfgNow(),[k]:list.map(x=>x===v?nv:x)};if(L.est){const u={...CATUN,[nv]:CATUN[v]||'un'};delete u[v];c.catUn=u}
        if(await safe(async()=>{await S.db.doc('config/listas').set(c);applyCfg(c);if(L.est){for(const s of S.estoqueAll.filter(s=>!isKit(s)&&s.tipo===v))await S.db.doc('estoque/'+s.id).update({tipo:nv});return}for(const e of S.equips.filter(e=>e[L.campo]===v))await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({[L.campo]:nv})},`${v} agora e ${nv}`))formLista(k,mode)}});
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-rm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const it=b.closest('.lst-item'),v=list[+b.dataset.rm],n=uso(v);it.querySelector('.lst-act').hidden=true;
      askDelete(it.querySelector('.lst-conf'),`Excluir <b>${esc(v)}</b> da lista?${n?` Os ${n} ${L.est?'itens':'equipamentos'} que usam esse nome mantem o valor ate serem editados.`:''}`,()=>save(list.filter(x=>x!==v),`${v} excluido`));
      it.querySelector('.lst-conf [data-no]').addEventListener('click',()=>{it.querySelector('.lst-act').hidden=false})});
  }});
}

    ${f('Nº de serie / chassi',e.serie?`<button type="button" class="cp num" data-copy="${esc(e.serie)}" aria-label="Copiar numero de serie">${esc(e.serie)}</button>`:'')}${f('Nº do motor',e.motor?`<button type="button" class="cp num" data-copy="${esc(e.motor)}" aria-label="Copiar numero do motor">${esc(e.motor)}</button>`:'')}${f('Motorista',esc(e.motorista||''))}${f('Placa',e.placa?`<button type="button" class="cp num" data-copy="${esc(e.placa)}" aria-label="Copiar placa">${esc(e.placa)}</button>`:'')}

    ${f('Intervalo preventivas',e.intervaloPrev?`<span class="num">${nf(e.intervaloPrev)} h</span>`:'')}${f('Uso medio',e.usoDiario?`${nf(e.usoDiario,1)} h/dia`:'')}${f('Oleos e filtros no plano',String((e.itens||[]).length))}${f('Trocas registradas',String(hist.length))}${f('Observacoes',esc(e.obs))}</div>

  <div class="spec" style="margin-top:12px">${(()=>{const si=eqSituacao(e);return f('Situacao',`<span class="sit ${si.cls}">${si.label}</span>`)+(si.its.length?`<div class="r sit-l"><div class="es-l">${si.its.map(x=>itRow(x)).join('')}</div></div>`:'')+(si.est.length?`<div class="r ls sit-e"><span>Estoque</span><ul class="sl">${si.est.map(x=>`<li><span class="sl-n">${esc(x.s.desc)}<small class="num">${esc(stCod(x.s))}</small></span><b class="${x.c==='bad'?'sit-bad':'sit-warn'}">${x.t}</b></li>`).join('')}</ul></div>`:'')})()}</div>
  ${docsHTML(e)}

  <div class="lx-b" hidden>${itemList(e,eqItens(e).map(it=>({it,st:itemStatus(e,it)})),'<div class="empty">Sem oleos e filtros. Monte o plano pelo + › Editar cadastro.</div>')}</div>

      ${t.resp||t.obs?`<div class="s">${[t.resp?'Responsavel: '+esc(t.resp):'',t.obs?esc(t.obs):''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}</div>`:''}
      <button type="button" class="btn sm" data-tr="${esc(t.id)}">${t.tipo==='reprog'||t.tipo==='hmtroca'?'Abrir':'Abrir preventiva'}</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty">Sem preventivas registradas.</div>'}
  ${fotoEqHTML(e)}`,
  foot:avisarFoot('eq',id),
  mount:sh=>{bindFotoEq(sh);bindDocs(sh,e);novoBind(sh,e);
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-tr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openTroca(b.dataset.tr));sh.querySelectorAll('[data-encst]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formMov(b.dataset.encst))}});

/* ---------- fotos ---------- */
const blobUrl=id=>'/_blob/'+id;
/* prepara a foto antes de enviar: recorta o fundo branco em volta do objeto,
   centraliza com margem estreita e igual nos 4 lados, sem ampliar e sem mudar a proporcao */
/* modo 'proc' (Processada): limpa o fundo claro e recorta; modo 'orig' (Original): nao mexe no fundo,
   so deixa o quase-branco branco, recorta as margens brancas e redimensiona */
async function shrink(file,modo='proc'){
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(file);
  try{const img=await new Promise((ok,no)=>{const i=new Image();i.onload=()=>ok(i);i.onerror=no;i.src=url});
    const M=2400,k=Math.min(1,M/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
    const W=Math.round(img.naturalWidth*k),H=Math.round(img.naturalHeight*k);
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=W;c.height=H;const x=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
    x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect(0,0,W,H);x.drawImage(img,0,0,W,H);
    const id=x.getImageData(0,0,W,H),d=id.data;
    // fundo claro (branco, cinza ou degrade de estudio): pinta de branco a partir das bordas
    {let n=0,r=0,g=0,b=0;const bp=[];for(let X=0;X<W;X+=2)bp.push(X,(H-1)*W+X);for(let Y=0;Y<H;Y+=2)bp.push(Y*W,Y*W+W-1);
      bp.forEach(q=>{r+=d[q*4];g+=d[q*4+1];b+=d[q*4+2];n++});r/=n;g/=n;b/=n;
      const far=q=>Math.max(Math.abs(d[q*4]-r),Math.abs(d[q*4+1]-g),Math.abs(d[q*4+2]-b));
      const light=q=>Math.min(d[q*4],d[q*4+1],d[q*4+2])>175&&Math.abs((d[q*4]-d[q*4+2])-(r-b))<16&&Math.abs((d[q*4+1]-d[q*4+2])-(g-b))<16;
      if(modo==='proc'&&Math.min(r,g,b)>185&&bp.filter(q=>light(q)&&far(q)<35).length/bp.length>.8){
        const seen=new Uint8Array(W*H),st=[],band=[];for(let Y=0;Y<H;Y++)for(let X=0;X<W;X++)if(X<4||Y<4||X>=W-4||Y>=H-4)band.push(Y*W+X);band.forEach(q=>{if(light(q)&&far(q)<35){seen[q]=1;st.push(q)}});
        const step=(a,c)=>Math.max(Math.abs(d[a*4]-d[c*4]),Math.abs(d[a*4+1]-d[c*4+1]),Math.abs(d[a*4+2]-d[c*4+2]))<=6;
        const done=[];
        while(st.length){const q=st.pop();done.push(q);const X=q%W;
          for(const nq of [X>0?q-1:-1,X<W-1?q+1:-1,q-W,q+W])if(nq>=0&&nq<W*H&&!seen[nq]&&light(nq)&&far(nq)<35&&step(q,nq)){seen[nq]=1;st.push(nq)}}
        // devolve o que foi apagado por engano dentro do contorno do objeto (linha e coluna)
        const rx0=new Int32Array(H).fill(W),rx1=new Int32Array(H).fill(-1),cy0=new Int32Array(W).fill(H),cy1=new Int32Array(W).fill(-1);
        for(let Y=0;Y<H;Y++)for(let X=0;X<W;X++)if(!seen[Y*W+X]){if(X<rx0[Y])rx0[Y]=X;if(X>rx1[Y])rx1[Y]=X;if(Y<cy0[X])cy0[X]=Y;if(Y>cy1[X])cy1[X]=Y}
        done.forEach(q=>{const X=q%W,Y=(q-X)/W;if(X>rx0[Y]&&X<rx1[Y]&&Y>cy0[X]&&Y<cy1[X]&&!(X<4||Y<4||X>=W-4||Y>=H-4))return;d[q*4]=d[q*4+1]=d[q*4+2]=255});
        /* conferencia: se o contorno da peca ficou serrilhado (pedacos comidos), desiste e usa a Original */
        {const wb=i=>d[i]>236&&d[i+1]>236&&d[i+2]>236;let pl=-1,pr=-1,sj=0,sw=0,n=0;
          for(let Y=0;Y<H;Y+=2){let l=-1,r2=-1;for(let X=0;X<W;X++)if(!wb((Y*W+X)*4)){l=X;break}if(l<0){pl=-1;continue}for(let X=W-1;X>=0;X--)if(!wb((Y*W+X)*4)){r2=X;break}
            if(pl>=0){sj+=Math.abs(l-pl)+Math.abs(r2-pr);sw+=r2-l;n++}pl=l;pr=r2}
          if(n>10&&sj/Math.max(1,sw)>(shrink.lim||.028))return await shrink(file,'orig')}
        x.putImageData(id,0,0)}}
    /* original: so o quase-branco vira branco (fundo branco fica limpo e some na tela) */
    if(modo==='orig'){for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){const mn=Math.min(d[i],d[i+1],d[i+2]),mx=Math.max(d[i],d[i+1],d[i+2]);if(mn>244&&mx-mn<8){d[i]=d[i+1]=d[i+2]=255}}x.putImageData(id,0,0)}
    const bg=i=>d[i]>236&&d[i+1]>236&&d[i+2]>236;
    // so recorta quando a borda da imagem e praticamente toda branca (foto de produto)
    let edge=0,white=0;for(let X=0;X<W;X++){for(const Y of [0,H-1]){edge++;if(bg((Y*W+X)*4))white++}}for(let Y=0;Y<H;Y++){for(const X of [0,W-1]){edge++;if(bg((Y*W+X)*4))white++}}
    let out=c,trim=false;
    if(white/edge>.9){let x0=W,y0=H,x1=-1,y1=-1;
      for(let Y=0;Y<H;Y++)for(let X=0;X<W;X++)if(!bg((Y*W+X)*4)){if(X<x0)x0=X;if(X>x1)x1=X;if(Y<y0)y0=Y;if(Y>y1)y1=Y}
      if(x1>x0&&y1>y0){const w=x1-x0+1,h=y1-y0+1,m=Math.max(8,Math.round(Math.max(w,h)*.04));
        out=document.createElement('canvas');out.width=w+2*m;out.height=h+2*m;const o=out.getContext('2d');o.fillStyle='#fff';o.fillRect(0,0,out.width,out.height);o.drawImage(c,x0,y0,w,h,m,m,w,h);trim=true}}
    const png=trim&&out.width*out.height<=2600000;
    return await new Promise(ok=>out.toBlob(b=>ok(b||file),png?'image/png':'image/jpeg',png?undefined:.92));
  }catch(e){return file}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(url)}
}


/* ===================== Cartao Frota =====================
   Agrupa Equipamentos, Manutencoes (por enquanto: Preventivas), Combustivel e Alocacao. */
const FROTA_KIDS=['equip','manut','comb','aloc'];
function abreCard(k){S.focus=k;S.esQ='';S.esFoc=false;S.esAll=false;S.esFil=false;S.alocSite='';S.alocSel='';S.mf.equip='';S.showAllSched=false;S.showAllHist=false;S.pv={};
  try{localStorage.setItem('frota.focus',k)}catch(e){}render();SCR().scrollTo(0,0)}
function vFrota(){
  const b=(k,ic,t)=>`<section class="card acc fr-c"><button type="button" class="acc-h" data-fr="${k}"><span class="ic">${ic}</span><h2>${t}</h2><span class="tg">${I.chev}</span></button></section>`;

   Peca desconhecida: tira a foto ou escolhe da galeria e o app abre o Google Lens com ela.
   A foto sobe so para o Lens conseguir abrir; e apagada na proxima vez que o app abrir. */
const IDL='frota.lensFotos';
function idLimpa(){if(!S.assets)return;let l=[];try{l=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(IDL)||'[]')}catch(e){}
  /* rascunhos da versao anterior do cartao */
  ['frota.idCur','frota.idRec'].forEach(k=>{try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)||'null');[].concat(v||[]).forEach(x=>{if(x&&x.foto&&!x.usado)l.push(x.foto)});localStorage.removeItem(k)}catch(e){}});
  try{localStorage.removeItem(IDL)}catch(e){}l.forEach(id=>S.assets.delete(id).catch(()=>{}))}
function vIdent(){
  const IC={cam:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 8h3l2-3h6l2 3h3v11H4z"/><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="3.5"/></svg>',gal:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2"/><circle cx="9" cy="10" r="2"/><path d="M21 16l-5-5-8 9"/></svg>'};
  const h=toolbar('Descubra uma peca desconhecida pelo Google Lens');
  if(!window.__remoteUrl)return h+'<div class="empty"><b>Disponivel no app baixavel</b>A pesquisa pelo Google Lens precisa do app instalado no celular.</div>';
  return h+`<div class="id-fb"><button type="button" class="id-big" data-idf="cam">${IC.cam}<span>Tirar foto</span></button><button type="button" class="id-big" data-idf="gal">${IC.gal}<span>Galeria</span></button></div>
    <p class="note id-dica">Fotografe a peca de lado e, se houver, a marca ou os numeros gravados. O Google Lens abre com a foto.</p>`}
function bindIdent(app){
  app.querySelectorAll('[data-idf]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{
    if(!navigator.onLine){toast('Sem internet. O Google Lens precisa de conexao.');return}
    const f=await pickPhotoDireto(b.dataset.idf);if(!f)return;if(!S.assets){toast('Sem permissao para enviar fotos.');return}
    toast('Abrindo o Google Lens…');
    try{const bl=await shrink(f);const r=await S.assets.upload(bl,{type:bl.type&&/^image\/(jpeg|png)$/.test(bl.type)?bl.type:'image/jpeg'});
      try{const l=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(IDL)||'[]');l.push(r.id);localStorage.setItem(IDL,JSON.stringify(l))}catch(e){}
      await window.__assetPronto(r.id);
      const u='https://lens.google.com/uploadbyurl?url='+encodeURIComponent(window.__remoteUrl(r.id));
      const w=window.open(u,'_blank');if(!w)location.href=u}
    catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Nao foi possivel abrir o Google Lens. Confira a internet e tente de novo.')}})}
/* o app nao usa acentos: o que for digitado perde o acento na hora, sem erro */
document.addEventListener('input',ev=>{const t=ev.target;if(!t||!(t.tagName==='TEXTAREA'||(t.tagName==='INPUT'&&/^(text|search|)$/.test(t.type||''))))return;
  const v=t.value,n=v.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').normalize('NFC');if(n===v)return;
  let a=null,b=null;try{a=t.selectionStart;b=t.selectionEnd}catch(e){}t.value=n;try{if(a!=null)t.setSelectionRange(Math.min(a,n.length),Math.min(b,n.length))}catch(e){}},true);
/* foto direto da camera ou da galeria */
function pickPhotoDireto(kind){return new Promise(ok=>{const i=document.createElement('input');i.type='file';i.accept='image/*';if(kind==='cam')i.setAttribute('capture','environment');i.style.display='none';document.body.appendChild(i);
  i.onchange=()=>{const f=i.files&&i.files[0];i.remove();ok(f||null)};i.click()})}
/* foto: pergunta se vem da camera ou da galeria */
function pickPhoto(){return new Promise(ok=>{
  const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='pk-w';
  w.innerHTML=`<div class="pk-c" role="dialog" aria-label="Adicionar foto"><b>Adicionar foto</b><button type="button" class="pk-o" data-pk="cam">${'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 8h3l2-3h6l2 3h3v11H4z"/><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="3.5"/></svg>'}Tirar foto com a camera</button><button type="button" class="pk-o" data-pk="gal">${'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2"/><circle cx="9" cy="10" r="2"/><path d="M21 16l-5-5-8 9"/></svg>'}Escolher da galeria</button></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(w);const fim=f=>{w.remove();ok(f||null)};pickPhoto.close=()=>fim(null);
  w.onclick=ev=>{if(ev.target===w)fim(null)};
  w.querySelectorAll('[data-pk="cam"],[data-pk="gal"]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const i=document.createElement('input');i.type='file';i.accept='image/*';if(b.dataset.pk==='cam')i.setAttribute('capture','environment');i.style.display='none';document.body.appendChild(i);
    i.onchange=()=>{const f=i.files&&i.files[0];i.remove();fim(f)};w.style.display='none';i.click();
    /* se a pessoa desistir na camera/galeria, a janela some sozinha */
    const back=()=>{removeEventListener('focus',back);setTimeout(()=>{if(document.body.contains(w)&&!(i.files&&i.files.length)){i.remove();fim(null)}},800)};addEventListener('focus',back)})})}
/* foto de item: mostra a Processada e a Original lado a lado; a pessoa escolhe qual vai */
function escolheFoto(bp,bo){return new Promise(ok=>{const up=URL.createObjectURL(bp),uo=URL.createObjectURL(bo);
  const o=document.createElement('div');o.className='fe-w';o.innerHTML=`<div class="fe-c" role="dialog" aria-label="Escolha a foto"><b class="fe-t">Qual foto usar?</b>
    <div class="fe-g"><button type="button" class="fe-o" data-fe="proc"><span class="fe-i"><img src="${up}" alt=""></span><b>Processada</b><small>fundo limpo e recortada</small></button>
    <button type="button" class="fe-o" data-fe="orig"><span class="fe-i"><img src="${uo}" alt=""></span><b>Original</b><small>so encaixada e reduzida</small></button></div>
    <button type="button" class="btn block" data-fe="">Cancelar</button></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(o);const fim=v=>{o.remove();URL.revokeObjectURL(up);URL.revokeObjectURL(uo);ok(v)};
  o.querySelectorAll('[data-fe]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>fim(b.dataset.fe==='proc'?bp:b.dataset.fe==='orig'?bo:null));o.onclick=ev=>{if(ev.target===o)fim(null)}})}
async function uploadPhoto(escolha){
  if(!S.assets){toast('So quem pode editar o app consegue adicionar fotos.');return null}
  const f=await pickPhoto();if(!f)return null;
  if(!/^image\//.test(f.type||'image/')){toast('Escolha um arquivo de imagem.');return null}
  let pre=null;if(escolha){const [bp,bo]=await Promise.all([shrink(f,'proc'),shrink(f,'orig')]);pre=await escolheFoto(bp,bo);if(!pre)return null}
  toast('Enviando foto…');
  try{const b=pre||await shrink(f);const r=await S.assets.upload(b,{type:b.type&&/^image\/(jpeg|png|webp|gif)$/.test(b.type)?b.type:'image/jpeg'});return r.id}
  catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e&&e.code==='too_large'?'Foto grande demais.':e&&e.code==='quota_or_state'?'Espaco de fotos esgotado. Remova fotos antigas.':'Nao foi possivel enviar a foto. Tente de novo.');return null}
}
async function dropAsset(id){if(!id||!S.assets)return;try{await S.assets.delete(id)}catch(e){console.error(e)}}

  document.body.appendChild(o);const close=()=>o.remove();o.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=close;o.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===o)close()};
  const rm=o.querySelector('[data-rm]');rm&&(rm.onclick=()=>{const box=o.querySelector('.lb-conf');box.innerHTML='<div class="confirm"><span>Remover esta foto? Nao da para desfazer.</span><div class="b"><button class="btn sm" data-no>Cancelar</button><button class="btn sm danger" data-yes>Remover</button></div></div>';
    box.querySelector('[data-no]').onclick=()=>box.innerHTML='';box.querySelector('[data-yes]').onclick=async()=>{close();await onRemove()}});
}

const DOCS=[['docVeiculo','Documento do veiculo'],['notaFiscal','Nota fiscal']];
/* endereco do documento: no app instalado vem do armazenamento privado (link temporario); no app do Claude, o de sempre */

function docsHTML(e){const has=DOCS.filter(([k])=>e[k]&&e[k].id);if(!has.length)return '';

/* visualizacao: as paginas como imagem, uma embaixo da outra; botao Baixar PDF */
function viewDoc(e,k){const d=e[k];if(!d)return;const lab=(DOCS.find(x=>x[0]===k)||[])[1]||'Documento';
  const pages=d.pages&&d.pages.length?d.pages:(d.tipo&&/^image\//.test(d.tipo)?[d.id]:[]);
  const o=document.createElement('div');o.className='docv';o.setAttribute('role','dialog');o.setAttribute('aria-label',lab);
  o.innerHTML=`<div class="docv-h"><div><b>${lab}</b><small>${esc(e.codigo)}${pages.length>1?` · ${pages.length} paginas`:''} · toque para ampliar</small></div><button type="button" class="homebar" data-x aria-label="Fechar">✕</button></div>
    <div class="docv-b">${pages.length?pages.map((id,i)=>`<img data-did="${esc(id)}" alt="${lab}, pagina ${i+1}">`).join(''):'<div class="docv-e">Visualizacao indisponivel para este arquivo. Use Baixar PDF.</div>'}${d.trunc?'<div class="docv-e">Mostrando as primeiras paginas. O PDF completo esta no download.</div>':''}</div>
    <div class="docv-f">${pages.length?`<button type="button" class="btn docv-sh" data-sh style="flex:1">${I.share}${shareOk()?'Compartilhar':'Salvar imagem'}</button>`:''}<button type="button" class="btn pri" data-dl style="flex:1">Baixar PDF</button></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(o);const close=()=>o.remove();o.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=close;
  o.querySelectorAll('img[data-did]').forEach(async im=>{try{im.src=await docUrl(im.dataset.did)}catch(x){}});
  /* toque na pagina: amplia para ler detalhes; outro toque volta */
  o.querySelectorAll('.docv-b img').forEach(im=>im.onclick=()=>{const z=!o.classList.contains('zoom');o.classList.toggle('zoom',z);if(z)setTimeout(()=>im.scrollIntoView({block:'start',inline:'center'}),30)});
  /* compartilhar como imagem (paginas), pelo menu do celular — nunca link */
  const sb=o.querySelector('[data-sh]');sb&&(sb.onclick=async()=>{sb.disabled=true;
    try{const fs=await Promise.all(pages.map(async(id,i)=>{const r=await fetch(await docUrl(id));if(!r.ok)throw 0;const b=await r.blob();return new File([b],`${e.codigo} - ${lab}${pages.length>1?' p'+(i+1):''}.jpg`,{type:'image/jpeg'})}));
      let shared=false;
      try{if(shareOk()&&navigator.canShare({files:fs})){await navigator.share({files:fs,title:`${e.codigo} - ${lab}`});shared=true}}catch(x){if(x&&x.name==='AbortError')return;SHARE_BLOCKED=true}
      if(!shared)SHARE_BLOCKED=true;
      if(!shared){/* menu de compartilhar bloqueado neste app: salva a imagem no celular para enviar pelo WhatsApp */
        const img=fs.length===1?fs[0]:await joinPages(fs);const dl=await claude.use('downloads').catch(()=>null);
        if(!dl){toast('Compartilhar nao esta disponivel aqui. Use Baixar PDF.');return}
        await dl.save({filename:`${e.codigo} - ${lab}.jpg`,data:img});toast('Imagem salva no celular. Envie pelo WhatsApp a partir da galeria ou de Downloads.')}}
    catch(x){if(!(x&&(x.name==='AbortError'||x.code==='declined')))toast('Nao foi possivel compartilhar. Use Baixar PDF.')}
    finally{sb.disabled=false}});
  const bt=o.querySelector('[data-dl]');bt.onclick=async()=>{bt.disabled=true;bt.textContent='Preparando…';
    try{let blob;const r=await fetch(await docUrl(d.id));if(!r.ok)throw 0;blob=await r.blob();
      if(!(d.tipo==='application/pdf'||(blob.type||'')==='application/pdf'))blob=await jpgToPdf(blob);
      const dl=await claude.use('downloads').catch(()=>null);if(!dl){toast('Baixar arquivos nao esta disponivel aqui.');return}
      await dl.save({filename:`${e.codigo} - ${lab}.pdf`,data:blob})}
    catch(x){if(!(x&&x.code==='declined'))toast('Nao foi possivel baixar o arquivo.')}
    finally{bt.disabled=false;bt.textContent='Baixar PDF'}}}
/* o app do Claude pode bloquear o menu de compartilhar: depois da 1ª falha o botao passa a dizer o que faz (salvar) */
let SHARE_BLOCKED=false;const shareOk=()=>!SHARE_BLOCKED&&!!(navigator.share&&navigator.canShare);
/* junta as paginas numa imagem so (uma embaixo da outra) */
async function joinPages(files){const ims=await Promise.all(files.map(f=>new Promise((ok,no)=>{const u=URL.createObjectURL(f),x=new Image();x.onload=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(u);ok(x)};x.onerror=no;x.src=u})));
  const W=Math.max(...ims.map(i=>i.naturalWidth)),gap=16,H=ims.reduce((a,i)=>a+i.naturalHeight*(W/i.naturalWidth),0)+gap*(ims.length-1);
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=W;c.height=Math.round(H);const g=c.getContext('2d');g.fillStyle='#d9dde5';g.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
  let y=0;ims.forEach(i=>{const h=i.naturalHeight*(W/i.naturalWidth);g.drawImage(i,0,y,W,h);y+=h+gap});
  const b=await new Promise(r=>c.toBlob(r,'image/jpeg',.85));c.width=c.height=0;return b}
/* foto -> PDF (uma pagina A4 com a imagem), feito no proprio app, sem biblioteca */
async function jpgToPdf(blob){
  if(!/jpe?g/.test(blob.type||'')){const u=URL.createObjectURL(blob);try{const im=await new Promise((ok,no)=>{const x=new Image();x.onload=()=>ok(x);x.onerror=no;x.src=u});const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=im.naturalWidth;c.height=im.naturalHeight;const g=c.getContext('2d');g.fillStyle='#fff';g.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);g.drawImage(im,0,0);blob=await new Promise(r=>c.toBlob(r,'image/jpeg',.92))}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(u)}}
  const jpg=new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
  const dim=(()=>{let i=2;while(i<jpg.length){if(jpg[i]!==0xFF){i++;continue}const m=jpg[i+1],L=(jpg[i+2]<<8)|jpg[i+3];if(m>=0xC0&&m<=0xCF&&m!==0xC4&&m!==0xC8&&m!==0xCC)return {h:(jpg[i+5]<<8)|jpg[i+6],w:(jpg[i+7]<<8)|jpg[i+8]};i+=2+L}return {w:1000,h:1400}})();
  const PW=595.28,PH=841.89,M=18,sc=Math.min((PW-2*M)/dim.w,(PH-2*M)/dim.h),w=dim.w*sc,h=dim.h*sc,x=(PW-w)/2,y=(PH-h)/2;
  const enc=new TextEncoder(),parts=[],offs=[];let len=0;const put=b=>{const u=typeof b==='string'?enc.encode(b):b;parts.push(u);len+=u.length};
  const obj=(n,body)=>{offs[n]=len;put(`${n} 0 obj\n`);body();put('\nendobj\n')};
  const cs=`q ${w.toFixed(2)} 0 0 ${h.toFixed(2)} ${x.toFixed(2)} ${y.toFixed(2)} cm /Im0 Do Q`;
  put('%PDF-1.4\n%\xE2\xE3\xCF\xD3\n');
  obj(1,()=>put('<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>'));
  obj(2,()=>put('<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>'));
  obj(3,()=>put(`<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 ${PW} ${PH}] /Resources << /XObject << /Im0 4 0 R >> >> /Contents 5 0 R >>`));
  obj(4,()=>{put(`<< /Type /XObject /Subtype /Image /Width ${dim.w} /Height ${dim.h} /ColorSpace /DeviceRGB /BitsPerComponent 8 /Filter /DCTDecode /Length ${jpg.length} >>\nstream\n`);put(jpg);put('\nendstream')});
  obj(5,()=>put(`<< /Length ${cs.length} >>\nstream\n${cs}\nendstream`));
  const xref=len;put(`xref\n0 6\n0000000000 65535 f \n${[1,2,3,4,5].map(n=>String(offs[n]).padStart(10,'0')+' 00000 n \n').join('')}trailer\n<< /Size 6 /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n${xref}\n%%EOF`);
  return new Blob(parts,{type:'application/pdf'})}
/* leitor de PDF carregado so quando alguem anexa um PDF (roda na propria pagina, sem worker) */
let _pdfjs=null;
function loadPdfJs(){if(_pdfjs)return _pdfjs;const B='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/';
  const add=src=>new Promise((ok,no)=>{const sc=document.createElement('script');sc.src=src;sc.onload=ok;sc.onerror=no;document.head.appendChild(sc)});
  _pdfjs=add(B+'pdf.worker.min.js').then(()=>add(B+'pdf.min.js')).then(()=>{const L=window.pdfjsLib;L.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc=B+'pdf.worker.min.js';return L}).catch(e=>{_pdfjs=null;throw e});return _pdfjs}
const withTimeout=(p,ms,code)=>Promise.race([p,new Promise((_,no)=>setTimeout(()=>no({code}),ms))]);
async function pdfPages(file,max=10){const L=await withTimeout(loadPdfJs(),20000,'pdfjs_timeout');const doc=await L.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()),isEvalSupported:false}).promise;
  const out=[],n=Math.min(doc.numPages,max);
  for(let p=1;p<=n;p++){const pg=await doc.getPage(p),v0=pg.getViewport({scale:1}),sc=Math.min(1.4,950/v0.width),v=pg.getViewport({scale:sc});
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round(v.width);c.height=Math.round(v.height);const g=c.getContext('2d');g.fillStyle='#fff';g.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    await pg.render({canvasContext:g,viewport:v}).promise;out.push(await new Promise(r=>c.toBlob(r,'image/jpeg',.82)));pg.cleanup();c.width=c.height=0}
  const total=doc.numPages;doc.destroy();return {imgs:out,trunc:total>max}}
function pickDoc(){return new Promise(ok=>{const i=document.createElement('input');i.type='file';i.accept='.pdf,application/pdf,image/*';i.style.display='none';document.body.appendChild(i);i.onchange=()=>{const f=i.files&&i.files[0];i.remove();ok(f||null)};i.click()})}
/* imagem vai como JPEG (sem recorte); PDF vai como esta */
async function docBlob(f){if(f.type==='application/pdf'||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name))return f;
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(f);try{const im=await new Promise((ok,no)=>{const x=new Image();x.onload=()=>ok(x);x.onerror=no;x.src=url});
    const sc=Math.min(1,2400/Math.max(im.naturalWidth,im.naturalHeight)),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round(im.naturalWidth*sc);c.height=Math.round(im.naturalHeight*sc);
    const g=c.getContext('2d');g.fillStyle='#fff';g.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);g.drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);return await new Promise(r=>c.toBlob(r,'image/jpeg',.9))}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(url)}}
function docEditor(box,st,ctx={}){
  const draw=()=>{box.innerHTML=DOCS.map(([k,lab])=>{const d=st[k];return `<div class="doc-ed"><div class="doc-i"><b>${lab}</b><span>${d&&d.id?esc(d.nome||'arquivo'):'Nenhum arquivo'}</span></div>${S.assets?(d&&d.id?`<label class="btn sm fpick" data-dt="${k}">Trocar<input type="file" accept=".pdf,application/pdf,image/*"></label><button type="button" class="btn sm danger" data-dr="${k}">Remover</button>`:`<label class="btn sm fpick" data-dt="${k}">Anexar<input type="file" accept=".pdf,application/pdf,image/*"></label>`):''}</div>`}).join('')+(S.assets?'<p class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0">PDF ou foto. Na ficha, abre como imagem e pode ser baixado em PDF.</p>':'<p class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0">So quem pode editar o app consegue anexar documentos.</p>');
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-dt] input[type=file]').forEach(inp=>inp.onchange=async()=>{const b=inp.closest('[data-dt]'),f=inp.files&&inp.files[0];inp.value='';if(!f)return;if(!(f.type==='application/pdf'||/^image\//.test(f.type)||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name))){toast('Escolha um PDF ou uma foto.');return}
      const k=b.dataset.dt,bx=box.querySelector(`[data-dt="${k}"]`).closest('.doc-ed'),msg=t=>{const x=bx.querySelector('.doc-i span');if(x)x.textContent=t};bx.classList.add('busy');msg('Enviando…');
      /* 1º o arquivo original (garante o anexo mesmo que a visualizacao falhe); equipamento ja cadastrado: grava na hora */
      let doc;
      try{const bl=await docBlob(f),isPdf=(bl.type||'application/pdf')==='application/pdf';
        const r=await S.assets.upload(bl,{type:isPdf?'application/pdf':'image/jpeg',priv:true});
        doc={id:r.id,nome:isPdf?f.name:f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')+'.jpg',tipo:isPdf?'application/pdf':'image/jpeg',pages:isPdf?[]:[r.id]};
        if(ctx.eqId){await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+ctx.eqId).update({[k]:doc});const o=ctx.orig&&ctx.orig[k];if(ctx.orig)ctx.orig[k]=doc;if(o&&o.id!==doc.id)[o.id,...(o.pages||[])].filter((x,i,a)=>x&&a.indexOf(x)===i).forEach(dropAsset)}
        else PEND.add(r.id);
        st[k]=doc;
        /* 2º a visualizacao (PDF): paginas em imagem, leves */
        if(isPdf){msg('Preparando a visualizacao…');try{const rr=await withTimeout(pdfPages(bl,6),60000,'render_timeout');const pages=[];
            for(const im of rr.imgs){const u=await S.assets.upload(im,{type:'image/jpeg',priv:true});if(!ctx.eqId)PEND.add(u.id);pages.push(u.id)}
            doc={...doc,pages,...(rr.trunc?{trunc:true}:{})};st[k]=doc;if(ctx.eqId){await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+ctx.eqId).update({[k]:doc});if(ctx.orig)ctx.orig[k]=doc}}
          catch(x){console.error(x);toast('PDF anexado. A visualizacao nao pode ser preparada neste aparelho; o Baixar PDF funciona.')}}
        draw();toast(ctx.eqId?'Documento anexado.':'Arquivo anexado. Toque em Salvar para gravar.')}
      catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e&&e.code==='too_large'?'Arquivo grande demais (max. 20 MB).':e&&e.code==='quota_or_state'?'Espaco de arquivos esgotado.':e&&e.code==='not_granted'?'Sem permissao para enviar arquivos.':'Nao foi possivel enviar o arquivo'+(e&&e.code?` (${e.code})`:'')+'.');draw()}});
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-dr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{st[b.dataset.dr]=null;draw()})};
  draw();return st}
const PEND=new Set();
function dropPending(){PEND.forEach(id=>dropAsset(id));PEND.clear()}
/* editor de fotos dentro do formulario (salva junto com o cadastro) */
function photoEditor(box,list,max,escolha){
  const draw=()=>{box.innerHTML=`${list.length?`<div class="ph-grid">${list.map((id,i)=>`<div class="ph-ed"><button type="button" class="ph-th" data-pi="${i}">${imgTag(id)}</button><button type="button" class="btn sm danger ph-rm" data-pr="${i}" hidden>Remover</button></div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}
    ${list.length<max?(S.assets?`<button type="button" class="ph-add" data-pa>${I.plus}${max===1?'Adicionar foto':`Adicionar foto (${list.length}/${max})`}</button>`:'<p class="note" style="margin:0">So quem pode editar o app consegue adicionar fotos.</p>'):''}`;
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-pi]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const r=box.querySelector(`[data-pr="${b.dataset.pi}"]`);const was=r.hidden;box.querySelectorAll('.ph-rm').forEach(x=>x.hidden=true);r.hidden=!was});
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-pr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{list.splice(+b.dataset.pr,1);draw()});
    const a=box.querySelector('[data-pa]');a&&(a.onclick=async()=>{const id=await uploadPhoto(escolha);if(!id)return;PEND.add(id);list.push(id);draw()});};
  draw();return list;
}
function commitPhotos(orig,now){now.forEach(id=>PEND.delete(id));orig.filter(x=>!now.includes(x)).forEach(dropAsset)}

function formPickEdit(kind){
  nav('pk:'+kind,()=>formPickEdit(kind));
  const isEq=kind==='eq',K=kind==='kit';
  const all=(isEq?S.equipsAll:S.estoqueAll.filter(x=>isKit(x)===K)).slice().sort(byCode);
  const draw=l=>isEq?porTipo(l,row):l.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${l.map(row).join('')}</div>`:'';
  const row=x=>!isEq?esRow(x,{attr:`data-pe="${esc(x.id)}"`}):`<button type="button" class="tq" data-pe="${esc(x.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(isEq?x.codigo:stCod(x))}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc(isEq?([x.marca,x.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'—'):x.desc)}</span>${I.chev}</button>`;
  openSheet({title:isEq?'Editar equipamento':K?'Editar kit':'Editar filtros e oleos',sub:'Busque e toque no item para editar',
  body:`<div class="search">${I.search}<input id="pe-q" type="search" placeholder="${isEq?'TAG, tipo ou modelo':'Codigo, referencia ou descricao'}" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Buscar"></div>${isEq?'':rdBtn('pe-cam')}
    <div id="pe-l" style="margin-top:8px">${draw(all)||'<div class="empty">Nada cadastrado ainda.</div>'}</div>`,
  mount:sh=>{const l=sh.querySelector('#pe-l'),q=sh.querySelector('#pe-q');
    const norm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase();
    const bind=()=>l.querySelectorAll('[data-pe]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>isEq?formEq(b.dataset.pe,1):K?formKit(b.dataset.pe):formStock(b.dataset.pe));
    q.oninput=()=>{const t=norm(q.value.trim()),tc=t.replace(/[\s-]/g,'');const hit=all.filter(x=>!t||norm(x.codigo).replace(/[\s-]/g,'').includes(tc)||norm(isEq?[x.tipo,x.marca,x.modelo].join(' '):[x.ref,x.desc].join(' ')).includes(t));
      l.innerHTML=draw(hit)||'<div class="empty">Nada encontrado.</div>';bind()};bind();
    const pc=sh.querySelector('#pe-cam');if(pc)pc.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item&&all.some(x=>x.id===v.item.id))K?formKit(v.item.id):formStock(v.item.id);else{q.value=v.item?stCod(v.item):v.text||'';q.oninput()}}})}});
}
/* + › Equipamentos / Filtros e oleos / Kits: novo, editar e excluir na mesma tela */
function formGerir(kind){
  nav('gr:'+kind,()=>formGerir(kind));
  const isEq=kind==='eq',K=kind==='kit';
  const all=(isEq?S.equipsAll:S.estoqueAll.filter(x=>isKit(x)===K)).slice().sort(byCode);
  const TR='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M6 7l1 13h10l1-13"/></svg>';
  const inner=x=>!isEq?esRow(x,{attr:`data-ge="${esc(x.id)}"`}):`<button type="button" class="tq" data-ge="${esc(x.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(x.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc([x.marca,x.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'—')}${x.status==='inativo'?' · inativo':''}</span>${I.chev}</button>`;
  const row=x=>`<div class="gr-r">${inner(x)}<button type="button" class="gr-x" data-gx="${esc(x.id)}" aria-label="Excluir ${esc(isEq?x.codigo:stCod(x))}">${TR}</button></div>`;
  const draw=l=>isEq?porTipo(l,row):l.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${l.map(row).join('')}</div>`:'';
  const nome=isEq?'equipamento':K?'kit':'item';
  openSheet({title:isEq?'Equipamentos':K?'Kits':'Filtros e oleos',sub:'Toque para editar · lixeira para excluir',
  body:`<button type="button" class="btn pri block" id="gr-new">${I.plus}Novo ${nome}</button>
    <div class="search" style="margin-top:10px">${I.search}<input id="gr-q" type="search" placeholder="${isEq?'TAG, tipo ou modelo':'Codigo, referencia ou descricao'}" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Buscar"></div>${isEq?'':rdBtn('gr-cam')}
    <div id="gr-l" style="margin-top:8px">${draw(all)||'<div class="empty">Nada cadastrado ainda.</div>'}</div>`,
  mount:sh=>{const l=sh.querySelector('#gr-l'),q=sh.querySelector('#gr-q');
    const norm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
    const edit=id=>isEq?formEq(id,1):K?formKit(id):formStock(id);
    sh.querySelector('#gr-new').onclick=()=>isEq?formEq():K?formKit():formStock();
    const bind=()=>{l.querySelectorAll('[data-ge]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>edit(b.dataset.ge));l.querySelectorAll('[data-gx]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openDelete(kind,b.dataset.gx))};
    q.oninput=()=>{const t=norm(q.value.trim()),tc=t.replace(/[\s-]/g,'');const hit=all.filter(x=>!t||norm(x.codigo).replace(/[\s-]/g,'').includes(tc)||norm(isEq?[x.tipo,x.marca,x.modelo].join(' '):[x.ref,x.desc].join(' ')).includes(t));
      l.innerHTML=draw(hit)||'<div class="empty">Nada encontrado.</div>';bind()};bind();
    const pc=sh.querySelector('#gr-cam');if(pc)pc.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item&&all.some(x=>x.id===v.item.id))edit(v.item.id);else{q.value=v.item?stCod(v.item):v.text||'';q.oninput()}}})}});
}
/* lista dentro de um quadro de dados: um item por linha, nome a esquerda e quantidade a direita */
function specList(lab,rows){if(!rows.length)return `<div class="r"><span>${esc(lab)}</span><b>—</b></div>`;
  return `<div class="r ls"><span>${esc(lab)}</span><ul class="sl">${rows.map(([n,c,q])=>`<li><span class="sl-n">${esc(n)}${c?`<small class="num">${esc(c)}</small>`:''}</span>${q?`<b class="num">${esc(q)}</b>`:''}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>`}
/* atalhos que levam ao caminho oficial de edicao */
function goEditar(kind,id){kind==='eq'?formEq(id):isKit(stById(id))?formKit(id):formStock(id)}
function itemList(e,its,empty){
  if(!its.length)return empty;
  const G=[['kit','Kits'],...CATS.map(c=>[catKey(c),catPl(c)])],gk=t=>t==='kit'?'kit':CATS.includes(catNm(t))?catKey(catNm(t)):'outro';
  return G.map(([k,lab])=>{const g=its.filter(x=>gk(x.it.tipo)===k);return g.length?`<div class="ig-h">${lab} <span>${g.length}</span></div>${itemRows(e,g)}`:''}).join('');
}
function itemRows(e,its){
  return `<div class="ilist">${its.map(({it,st},i)=>{const sk=it.estoqueId&&stById(it.estoqueId);return `<div class="ia"><button type="button" class="ia-h" data-ia aria-expanded="false"><span class="idot ${st.cls}"></span><span class="ia-t">${esc(itNome(it))}</span><span class="ist ${st.cls}">${st.label.toLowerCase()}</span>${I.chev}</button>
    <div class="ia-b" hidden><div class="s">${esc(it.espec||'Sem especificacao')}${it.qtd!==''&&it.qtd!=null?` · ${nf(it.qtd,1)} ${esc(it.un||'')}`:''}${sk?` · estoque ${esc(sk.codigo)}`:''}</div>
    <div class="meter"><i style="width:${Math.round(st.pct*100)}%;background:${meterColor(st.cls)}"></i></div>
    <div class="facts"><div>Ultima troca<b>${it.ultimaH!==''&&it.ultimaH!=null?(()=>{const o=it.ultimaData?hmOrig(e.id,it.ultimaH,it.ultimaData):{v:Number(it.ultimaH),d:0};return nf(o.v)+' h'+(o.d?'<small class="rp-mk">relogio antigo</small>':'')})():'—'}</b>${it.ultimaData?sdate(it.ultimaData):''}</div><div>${st.cls==='bad'?'Venceu em':'Proxima'}<b>${st.prox!=null?(()=>{const lt=hmTrocas(e.id).sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||''))[0];
      /* vencimento que caiu antes da troca do relogio: mostra no relogio antigo */
      if(st.cls==='bad'&&lt&&it.ultimaData&&(it.ultimaData<(lt.data||''))&&st.prox<=Number(lt.horimetro)){const o=hmOrig(e.id,st.prox,it.ultimaData);return nf(o.v)+' h<small class="rp-mk">relogio antigo</small>'}
      return nf(st.prox)+' h'})():'—'}</b>${it.reprog&&st.manual?'<span class="rp-mk">reprogramada</span>':st.cls!=='bad'&&st.prev?sdate(st.prev):''}</div><div>Horimetro<b>${hmTxt(e)} h</b></div></div>${(()=>{const def=!it.virt&&!it.ivDef&&it.ultimaH!==''&&it.ultimaH!=null&&!(sk&&isKit(sk));const rp=st.cls==='bad'&&!it.virt;
      return rp||def?`<div class="pn-bt">${def?`<button type="button" class="btn sm" data-ivd="${esc(e.id)}|${esc(it.pid)}">Intervalo</button>`:''}${rp?`<button type="button" class="btn sm" data-rp="${esc(e.id)}|${esc(it.pid)}">Reprogramar</button>`:''}</div><div class="ivd-box"></div>`:''})()}</div></div>`}).join('')}</div>`;
}
