/* ---------- reprogramar vencimento (so o item daquele equipamento; o cadastro do estoque nao muda) ---------- */
const RP_MOT=['Avaliado em bom estado','Sem peca no estoque','Aguardando parada','Outro'];
const tTit=t=>t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?'Filtro de ar · '+esc(t.sub||''):t.tipo==='reprog'?'Reprogramacao':t.tipo==='hmtroca'?'Troca de horimetro':'Preventiva';
const rpMotHTML=k=>`<div class="rp-ms" role="group" aria-label="Motivo">${RP_MOT.map(m=>`<button type="button" data-rpm="${esc(k)}" data-v="${esc(m)}" aria-pressed="false">${m}</button>`).join('')}</div><input class="rp-o" data-rpo="${esc(k)}" placeholder="Qual o motivo?" hidden>`;
function bindRpMot(root){root.querySelectorAll('[data-rpm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const k=b.dataset.rpm;root.querySelectorAll(`[data-rpm="${CSS.escape(k)}"]`).forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',x===b));const o=root.querySelector(`[data-rpo="${CSS.escape(k)}"]`);o.hidden=b.dataset.v!=='Outro';if(!o.hidden)o.focus();root.dispatchEvent(new Event('rpchange'))});
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-rpo]').forEach(o=>o.oninput=()=>root.dispatchEvent(new Event('rpchange')))}
const rpMot=(root,k)=>{const b=root.querySelector(`[data-rpm="${CSS.escape(k)}"][aria-pressed="true"]`);if(!b)return '';if(b.dataset.v!=='Outro')return b.dataset.v;const o=root.querySelector(`[data-rpo="${CSS.escape(k)}"]`).value.trim();return o?'Outro: '+o:''};
/* aplica a nova proxima no plano e devolve o registro para o historico */
const rpApply=(it,r)=>({...it,proxH:r.para,reprog:{de:r.de,motivo:r.motivo,data:r.data}});
const rpDoc=(e,list,resp,dt)=>({tipo:'reprog',equipId:e.id,data:dt,horimetro:Number(e.horimetro)||0,resp:resp||'',obs:'',ajuste:false,exemplo:!!e.exemplo,
  itens:list.map(r=>{const it=e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===r.pid)||{};return {pid:r.pid,desc:it.desc||'',espec:it.espec||'',estoqueId:it.estoqueId||'',qtd:0,un:it.un||'',de:r.de,para:r.para,proxAnt:it.proxH??'',motivo:r.motivo}})});
function formReprog(eqId,pid){
  nav('rp:'+eqId+':'+pid,()=>formReprog(eqId,pid));
  const e=eqById(eqId),it=e&&(e.itens||[]).find(i=>i.pid===pid);if(!it){goBack();return}
  const st=itemStatus(e,it),h=Number(e.horimetro)||0;
  openSheet({title:'Reprogramar',sub:`${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(itNome(it))}`,
  body:`<div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Horimetro atual</span><b class="num">${hmTxt(e)} h</b></div><div class="r"><span>${st.cls==='bad'?'Venceu em':'Proxima'}</span><b class="num">${st.prox!=null?nf(st.prox)+' h':'—'}</b></div></div>
  <form id="frp"><div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px">
    <label class="f">Nova proxima (h)<input id="rp-v" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" required placeholder="Maior que ${nf(h)}"></label>
    <div class="f">Motivo${rpMotHTML('x')}</div>
    <label class="f">Responsavel<input id="rp-r" placeholder="Mecanico ou oficina"></label></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="frp">Reprogramar</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{bindRpMot(sh);
    sh.querySelector('#frp').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      const v=Number(sh.querySelector('#rp-v').value),mot=rpMot(sh,'x');
      if(!(v>h)){toast(`A nova proxima precisa ser maior que ${nf(h)} h.`);return}
      if(!mot){toast('Escolha o motivo.');return}
      const r={pid,de:st.prox,para:v,motivo:mot,data:today()};
      const ok=await safe(async()=>{await S.db.collection('trocas').add(rpDoc(e,[r],sh.querySelector('#rp-r').value.trim(),r.data));
        await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({itens:e.itens.map(i=>i.pid===pid?rpApply(i,r):i)})},'Item reprogramado');
      if(ok)goBack()}}});
}
document.addEventListener('click',ev=>{const b=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('[data-rp]');if(!b)return;const[a,c]=b.dataset.rp.split('|');formReprog(a,c)});
function openPend(id,all){
  nav((all?'cr:':'pd:')+id,()=>openPend(id,all));
  const e=eqById(id);if(!e){openSheet({title:'Carregando…',body:'<div class="empty">Abrindo…</div>'});return}
  const its=eqItens(e).map(it=>({it,st:itemStatus(e,it)})).filter(x=>all?true:(x.st.cls==='bad'||x.st.cls==='warn')).sort((a,b)=>a.st.rest-b.st.rest);
  const npend=its.filter(x=>x.st.cls==='bad'||x.st.cls==='warn').length;
  const si=eqSituacao(e),stk=si.mot.filter(m=>/estoque/.test(m));
  openSheet({title:esc(e.codigo),sub:`Horimetro ${hmTxt(e)} h${e.horimetroData?` em ${sdate(e.horimetroData)}`:''}`,
  body:`<div class="pend-sit"><span class="sit ${si.cls}">${si.label}</span><span class="note">${npend?`${npend} ${npend===1?'item pendente':'itens pendentes'}`:'Nenhum item pendente'}</span></div>
    <div class="lbl">${all?'Cronograma de trocas':'O que esta pendente'}</div>
    ${itemList(e,its,`<div class="empty">${all?'Sem oleos e filtros no plano.':'Nenhuma troca pendente.'}</div>`)}
    ${stk.length?`<div class="lbl">Estoque</div><div class="spec">${stk.map(m=>`<div class="r"><span>${esc(m)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}`,
  foot:`<button class="btn" data-k="fi">Ver ficha</button><button class="btn pri" data-k="rg">Registrar preventiva</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{sh.querySelector('[data-k="fi"]').onclick=()=>openEq(id);sh.querySelector('[data-k="rg"]').onclick=()=>formRegistrar(id)}});
}
/* ---------- leitura rapida de horimetros ---------- */
const HMD_KEY='frota.hmDraft';
function hmDraft(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(HMD_KEY)||'{}')||{}}catch(e){return {}}}
function hmDraftSave(d){try{localStorage.setItem(HMD_KEY,JSON.stringify(d))}catch(e){}}
/* virada do dia: o contador zera sozinho mesmo com o app aberto */
setInterval(()=>{if(quickHm.day&&quickHm.day!==today())quickHm()},60000);
/* conta como lido hoje: leitura com data de hoje ou horimetro maior informado hoje em qualquer lancamento */
/* leitura menor que a atual (com dt, converte leituras de antes de uma troca de relogio) */
const hmLow=(e,v,dt)=>e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null&&Number(v)+(dt?hmDeltaApos(e.id,dt):0)<Number(e.horimetro);
const lidoHoje=e=>e.horimetroData===today()||e.horimetroReg===today();
function quickHm(){
  const el=$('#sidetabs');if(!el)return;
  if(!S.db||!S.eqCtl.length){el.hidden=true;return}
  const t=today(),lidos=S.eqCtl.filter(lidoHoje).length,n=S.eqCtl.length;
  el.hidden=false;const sn=el.querySelector('.sh-n');sn.textContent=`${lidos}/${n}`;sn.classList.toggle('zero',!lidos);sn.classList.toggle('all',lidos>=n);quickHm.day=t;
  $('#sidehm').setAttribute('aria-label',`Horimetros, ${lidos} de ${n} lidos hoje`);
}
const hmGroupOf=e=>e.alocacao||'';
/* faixa de dias da leitura (ultimos 21 dias, hoje no fim). Ponto vermelho: dia com leitura lancada
   em algum equipamento. O icone de calendario abre o seletor para datas mais antigas; datas futuras bloqueadas */
const DSEM=['DOM','SEG','TER','QUA','QUI','SEX','SAB'];
const CAL_IC='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="16" rx="2"/><path d="M16 3v4M8 3v4M3 11h18"/></svg>';
function dayStrip(sel){
  const t=today(),lidos=new Set(S.eqCtl.map(e=>e.horimetroData).filter(Boolean));
  const d0=new Date(t+'T12:00:00');d0.setDate(d0.getDate()-20);
  let ini=iso(d0);if(sel&&sel<ini)ini=sel;
  const out=[];for(const d=new Date(ini+'T12:00:00');iso(d)<=t;d.setDate(d.getDate()+1)){const k=iso(d);
    const nome=k===t?'HOJE':DSEM[d.getDay()];
    out.push(`<button type="button" class="dday" data-dd="${k}" aria-pressed="${k===sel}" aria-label="${nome} ${fdate(k)}"><small>${nome}</small><b>${d.getDate()}</b><i class="${lidos.has(k)?'on':''}"></i></button>`)}
  return `<div class="dstrip-l"><span>Data da leitura</span><b>${sel===t?'Hoje':''} ${fdate(sel)}</b></div>
  <div class="dstrip-w"><div class="dstrip">${out.join('')}</div><label class="dcal" aria-label="Escolher outra data">${CAL_IC}<input id="lr-dt" type="date" max="${t}" value="${esc(sel)}"></label></div>`}
function formLeituras(focusId){
  nav('leit',()=>formLeituras());
  const D=hmDraft();D.v=D.v||{};D.jok=false;/* data da leitura: sempre hoje; uma data alterada vale so para aquele dia */if(!D.dt||D.dtDia!==today()){D.dt=today();delete D.dtDia}D.g=D.g??'';if(focusId)D.g='';
  const t=today();
  const groups=['','PQ','DM1'];if(!groups.includes(D.g))D.g='';
  const list=S.eqCtl.filter(e=>!D.g||hmGroupOf(e)===D.g).sort((a,b)=>tipoOrd(a.tipo,b.tipo)||byCode(a,b));
  let lastT='';
  /* faixa lateral pela idade da leitura: verde < 7 dias, amarelo 7–15, vermelho > 15.
     Com preventiva a menos de 100 h: verde < 3 dias, amarelo 3–4, vermelho 5 ou mais. Sem leitura: vermelho */
  const idade=e=>{const dt=lidoHoje(e)?t:e.horimetroData;if(!dt||e.horimetro===''||e.horimetro==null)return 'r';
    const d=Math.max(0,Math.round((Date.parse(t)-Date.parse(dt))/864e5));
    const perto=(e.itens||[]).some(it=>{const r=itemStatus(e,it).rest;return isFinite(r)&&r<100});
    return perto?(d>=5?'r':d>=3?'y':'g'):(d>15?'r':d>=7?'y':'g')};
  const jmp=(e,v)=>v===''?null:hmSalto(e,Number(v)+hmDeltaApos(e.id,D.dt),D.dt);
  const jtx=j=>j?`Confira: +${nf(j.dif)} h em ${j.days} dia${j.days>1?'s':''}`:'';
  const rows=list.map(e=>{const v=D.v[e.id]??'',low=v!==''&&e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null&&Number(v)<Number(e.horimetro),jp=!low&&jmp(e,v),id8=idade(e);
    const head=e.tipo!==lastT?`<div class="lr-t">${esc(lastT=e.tipo||'Sem tipo')}</div>`:'';
    return head+`<div class="lr lr-${id8}${v!==''?' fill':''}${low?' low':''}" data-id="${esc(e.id)}"><div class="lr-i"><span class="lr-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span>
      <span class="lr-p">${e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?`ultima <b>${nf(e.horimetro)}</b> h${e.horimetroData?` · ${sdate(e.horimetroData)}`:''}`:'sem leitura'}${lidoHoje(e)?' <span class="ok-tag">lido hoje</span>':''}</span>
      <span class="lr-w">${low?`Nao pode ser menor que ${nf(e.horimetro)} h`:jtx(jp)}</span><button type="button" class="lr-x" data-thm="${esc(e.id)}"${low?'':' hidden'}>Trocou o relogio do horimetro?</button></div>
      <input class="lr-in" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" enterkeyhint="next" placeholder="${e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?nf(e.horimetro):'—'}" value="${esc(v)}" aria-label="Horimetro de ${esc(e.codigo)}"></div>`}).join('');
  const cnt=Object.keys(D.v).length;
  openSheet({title:'Lancar horimetros',sub:'So as preenchidas sao salvas',
  body:`<div class="lr-top">${dayStrip(D.dt)}</div>
    <div class="chips lr-chips">${groups.map(g=>`<button class="chip" data-g="${esc(g)}" aria-pressed="${D.g===g}">${esc(g||'Todos')} <em>${g?S.eqCtl.filter(e=>hmGroupOf(e)===g).length:S.equips.length}</em></button>`).join('')}</div>
    <div class="lr-list">${rows||'<div class="empty">Nenhum equipamento neste grupo.</div>'}</div><div id="lr-conf"></div>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" id="lr-save"${cnt?'':' disabled'}>${cnt?`Salvar ${cnt} leitura${cnt===1?'':'s'}`:'Salvar leituras'}</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{
    const upd=()=>{const c=Object.keys(D.v).length,b=sh.querySelector('#lr-save');b.disabled=!c;b.textContent=c?`Salvar ${c} leitura${c===1?'':'s'}`:'Salvar leituras';hmDraftSave(D)};
    const setDt=v=>{v=v||today();if(v>today())v=today();D.dt=v;if(D.dt===today())delete D.dtDia;else D.dtDia=today();hmDraftSave(D);
      const top=sh.querySelector('.lr-top');top.innerHTML=dayStrip(D.dt);bindStrip()};
    const bindStrip=()=>{const top=sh.querySelector('.lr-top');
      top.querySelectorAll('[data-dd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setDt(b.dataset.dd));
      top.querySelector('#lr-dt').onchange=ev=>setDt(ev.target.value);
      const st=top.querySelector('.dstrip'),sel=st.querySelector('[aria-pressed="true"]');
      st.scrollLeft=st.scrollWidth;if(sel&&sel.offsetLeft<st.scrollLeft)st.scrollLeft=sel.offsetLeft-4};
    bindStrip();
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-g]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{D.g=b.dataset.g;hmDraftSave(D);formLeituras();sh.querySelector('.sh-body').scrollTop=0});
    const ins=[...sh.querySelectorAll('.lr-in')];
    ins.forEach((inp,i)=>{const row=inp.closest('.lr'),e=eqById(row.dataset.id);
      inp.oninput=()=>{const v=inp.value.trim();if(v==='')delete D.v[e.id];else D.v[e.id]=v;
        const low=v!==''&&hmLow(e,v,D.dt);
        row.classList.toggle('fill',v!=='');row.classList.toggle('low',low);row.querySelector('.lr-w').textContent=low?`Nao pode ser menor que ${nf(e.horimetro)} h`:jtx(jmp(e,v));D.jok=false;sh.querySelector('#lr-conf').innerHTML='';row.querySelector('.lr-x').hidden=!low;upd()};
      inp.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();const nx=ins[i+1];nx?nx.focus():inp.blur()}};
      inp.onfocus=()=>row.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'})});
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-thm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formTrocaHm(b.dataset.thm,D.v[b.dataset.thm]));
    if(focusId){const fi=ins.find(i=>i.closest('.lr').dataset.id===focusId);if(fi){fi.closest('.lr').scrollIntoView({block:'center'});setTimeout(()=>fi.focus({preventScroll:true}),250)}}
    const doSave=async()=>{const ids=Object.keys(D.v).filter(id=>eqById(id));let ok=0;
      const b=sh.querySelector('#lr-save');b.disabled=true;b.textContent='Salvando…';
      const res=await safe(async()=>{for(const id of ids){await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+id).update({horimetro:Math.round((Number(D.v[id])+hmDeltaApos(id,D.dt))*10)/10,horimetroData:D.dt,horimetroReg:today()});ok++;delete D.v[id];hmDraftSave(D)}},null);
      toast(res?`${ok} leitura${ok===1?'':'s'} salva${ok===1?'':'s'}`:`${ok} salvas. As demais ficaram no rascunho.`);
      if(res){D.v={};D.dt=today();delete D.dtDia;hmDraftSave(D);closeSheet()}else formLeituras()};
    /* a nova leitura precisa ser maior que a ultima registrada no equipamento */
    sh.querySelector('#lr-save').onclick=()=>{const lows=Object.entries(D.v).filter(([id,v])=>{const e=eqById(id);return e&&hmLow(e,v,D.dt)}).map(([id])=>eqById(id).codigo);
      if(lows.length){toast(`Corrija ${lows.join(', ')}: a leitura nao pode ser menor que a ultima.`);const r=sh.querySelector('.lr.low');r&&r.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});return}
      /* salto acima de 24 h por dia: pede confirmacao antes de salvar */
      const js=D.jok?[]:Object.entries(D.v).map(([id,v])=>{const e=eqById(id);const j=e&&jmp(e,v);return j?`${e.codigo} (+${nf(j.dif)} h em ${j.days} dia${j.days>1?'s':''})`:''}).filter(Boolean);
      if(js.length){const box=sh.querySelector('#lr-conf');box.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask"><b>Leitura muito acima do esperado</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">${esc(js.join(', '))}. Uma maquina trabalha no maximo 24 h por dia. Confira se nao houve erro de digitacao.</p><div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Revisar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>Os valores estao certos</button></div></div>`;
        box.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=()=>{box.innerHTML='';const r=[...sh.querySelectorAll('.lr')].find(r=>r.querySelector('.lr-w').textContent.startsWith('Confira'));r&&r.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'})};
        box.querySelector('[data-go]').onclick=()=>{D.jok=true;box.innerHTML='';doSave()};box.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});return}
      doSave()};
  }});
}

/* ---------- troca do relogio do horimetro ----------
   O relogio novo comeca noutra contagem. Para nao quebrar o cronograma, cada item do plano e
   deslocado pela diferenca (novo − antigo): as horas que faltavam para cada troca continuam as mesmas.
   As preventivas antigas continuam com as leituras do relogio antigo. */
/* ---------- protecoes de horimetro usadas por todo o app ----------
   hmDeltaApos: soma das trocas de relogio registradas DEPOIS da data do lancamento (converte leitura antiga para a contagem atual)
   hmRecua: leitura menor que a atual, com data igual ou posterior a ultima leitura (impossivel sem troca de relogio) */
const _hmIx={};const hmTrocas=eqId=>{if(_hmIx.a!==S.trocas){_hmIx.a=S.trocas;_hmIx.m={};S.trocas.forEach(t=>{if(t.tipo==='hmtroca')(_hmIx.m[t.equipId]=_hmIx.m[t.equipId]||[]).push(t)})}return (_hmIx.m[eqId]||[]).slice()};
const hmDeltaApos=(eqId,dt)=>Math.round(hmTrocas(eqId).filter(t=>(t.data||'')>(dt||'')).reduce((a,t)=>a+(Number(t.delta)||0),0)*10)/10;
const hmRecua=(e,h,dt)=>e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null&&h<Number(e.horimetro)&&(dt||'')>=(e.horimetroData||'');
/* informacao lancada antes de uma troca de relogio e mostrada como foi lida (sem converter) */
const hmOrig=(eqId,v,dt)=>{const d=hmDeltaApos(eqId,dt);return {v:Math.round((Number(v)-d)*10)/10,d}};
/* salto impossivel: mais de 24 h por dia desde a ultima leitura (provavel erro de digitacao) */
function hmSalto(e,h,dt){if(e.horimetro===''||e.horimetro==null||!e.horimetroData)return null;const last=Number(e.horimetro);if(!(h>last))return null;
  if((dt||'')<e.horimetroData)return null;const days=Math.max(1,Math.round((Date.parse(dt)-Date.parse(e.horimetroData))/864e5));const lim=24*days;
  return h-last>lim?{dif:Math.round((h-last)*10)/10,days,lim}:null}
function hmGuard(box,e,h0,dt,st,again){
  const dl=hmDeltaApos(e.id,dt),h=Math.round((h0+dl)*10)/10;
  const show=(html,go)=>{box.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask">${html}</div>`;box.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=()=>box.innerHTML='';go(box.querySelector('[data-go]'));box.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'})};
  if(hmRecua(e,h,dt)&&!st.corr){show(`<b>Horimetro menor que o atual</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">${nf(h0)} h e menor que a ultima leitura de ${esc(e.codigo)} (${nf(e.horimetro)} h). Confira o valor. Se a leitura anterior estava errada, corrija. Se o relogio foi trocado, registre a troca.</p><div class="eqv-f hm-3"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Revisar</button><button type="button" class="btn" data-cor>Leitura anterior errada</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>Troca de horimetro</button></div>`,b=>b.onclick=()=>{box.innerHTML='';formTrocaHm(e.id,h0)});
    /* leitura anterior errada: o horimetro passa a ser o valor menor, sem troca de relogio; fica registrado na ficha */
    box.querySelector('[data-cor]').onclick=()=>{box.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask"><b>Corrigir leitura anterior</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">A leitura de <b>${nf(e.horimetro)} h</b> estava errada. O horimetro de ${esc(e.codigo)} passa a ser <b>${nf(h)} h</b>, sem troca de relogio.</p><div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Cancelar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>Confirmar correcao</button></div></div>`;
      box.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=()=>box.innerHTML='';
      box.querySelector('[data-go]').onclick=async()=>{const cur=eqById(e.id)||e,u=EU();const reg={data:today(),de:Number(cur.horimetro),para:h,por:(u&&u.nome)||''};
        if(!await safe(()=>S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({horimetro:h,horimetroData:dt||today(),hmCorr:[...(cur.hmCorr||[]),reg]}),'Leitura corrigida'))return;
        st.corr=true;box.innerHTML='';setTimeout(again,150)}};
    return null}
  if(!st.salto){const sj=hmSalto(e,h,dt);if(sj){show(`<b>Horimetro muito acima do esperado</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">${nf(h0)} h fica ${nf(sj.dif)} h acima da ultima leitura de ${esc(e.codigo)} (${nf(e.horimetro)} h em ${fdate(e.horimetroData)}). Em ${sj.days} dia${sj.days>1?'s':''} a maquina trabalha no maximo ${nf(sj.lim)} h. Confira se nao houve erro de digitacao.</p><div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Revisar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>O valor esta certo</button></div>`,b=>b.onclick=()=>{st.salto=true;box.innerHTML='';again()});return null}}
  if(dl&&!st.ok){const t=hmTrocas(e.id).filter(t=>(t.data||'')>(dt||'')).sort((a,b)=>(a.data||'').localeCompare(b.data||''))[0];
    show(`<b>Lancamento de antes da troca do relogio</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">O relogio de ${esc(e.codigo)} foi trocado em ${fdate(t.data)}. ${nf(h0)} h e do relogio antigo e equivale a <b>${nf(h)} h</b> no relogio atual. O cronograma usa o valor convertido.</p><div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Revisar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go>Converter e continuar</button></div>`,b=>b.onclick=()=>{st.ok=true;box.innerHTML='';again()});return null}
  return {h,dl};
}
function formTrocaHm(eqId,novo0){
  nav('thm:'+(eqId||''),()=>formTrocaHm(eqId,novo0));
  const eqs=S.eqCtl.slice().sort(byCode);
  openSheet({title:'Troca de horimetro',sub:'Relogio de horas substituido',
  body:`<div class="thm-warn"><b>Atencao: altera o cronograma do equipamento</b>Use so quando o relogio de horas foi trocado. As proximas trocas de cada item sao recalculadas para a contagem do relogio novo.</div>
  <form id="fthm"><div class="fset">
    <label class="f">Equipamento<select id="thm-eq" required data-eqs><option value="">Escolha…</option>${eqs.map(e=>`<option value="${esc(e.id)}"${eqId===e.id?' selected':''}>${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(e.tipo)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
    <label class="f">Relogio antigo (h) <span class="hint">leitura final</span><input id="thm-a" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" required></label>
    <p class="warnline" id="thm-w"></p>
    <label class="f">Relogio novo (h) <span class="hint">leitura inicial</span><input id="thm-b" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" required value="${esc(novo0??'')}" placeholder="Ex.: 0"></label>
    <label class="f">Data da troca<input id="thm-dt" type="date" required value="${today()}"></label>
    <label class="f">Responsavel<input id="thm-r" placeholder="Mecanico ou oficina"></label>
    <label class="f">Motivo<input id="thm-o" placeholder="Ex.: relogio queimado"></label></div>
    <div class="lbl" id="thm-pl" hidden>Como fica o cronograma</div><div class="spec" id="thm-pv" hidden></div>
    <div id="thm-conf"></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="fthm">Registrar troca</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{const sel=sh.querySelector('#thm-eq'),a=sh.querySelector('#thm-a'),b=sh.querySelector('#thm-b'),w=sh.querySelector('#thm-w'),pv=sh.querySelector('#thm-pv'),pl=sh.querySelector('#thm-pl');
    const fillA=()=>{const e=eqById(sel.value);a.value=e&&e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?e.horimetro:'';a.placeholder=e&&e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?'Minimo '+nf(e.horimetro):'';draw()};
    const draw=()=>{const e=eqById(sel.value),A=Number(a.value),B=Number(b.value),ok=e&&a.value!==''&&b.value!=='';
      w.textContent=e&&a.value!==''&&e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null&&A<Number(e.horimetro)?`O relogio antigo nao pode ser menor que a ultima leitura (${nf(e.horimetro)} h).`:'';
      const its=ok?(e.itens||[]).map(it=>({it,st:itemStatus(e,it)})).filter(x=>x.st.prox!=null):[];
      pl.hidden=pv.hidden=!its.length;
      pv.innerHTML=its.length?`<div class="r" style="display:block"><div class="thm-tb">${its.map(({it,st})=>`<span class="n">${esc(itNome(it))}</span><s class="num">${nf(st.prox)}</s><span class="ar">→</span>${st.prox+(B-A)<=B?'<b class="num" style="color:var(--bad)">vencida</b>':`<b class="num">${nf(st.prox+(B-A))} h</b>`}`).join('')}</div><p class="note" style="margin:8px 0 0">Faltam as mesmas horas para cada troca.</p></div>`:''};
    sel.onchange=fillA;a.oninput=draw;b.oninput=draw;fillA();
    sh.querySelector('#fthm').onsubmit=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();const e=eqById(sel.value);if(!e)return;
      const A=Number(a.value),B=Number(b.value),dt=sh.querySelector('#thm-dt').value||today();
      if(e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null&&A<Number(e.horimetro)){a.focus();return}
      if(B<0){toast('A leitura do relogio novo nao pode ser negativa.');return}
      const cf=sh.querySelector('#thm-conf');
      askConfirm(cf,'Continuar',`Registrar a troca do horimetro de <b>${esc(e.codigo)}</b>? O relogio passa de ${nf(A)} h para ${nf(B)} h e o cronograma e recalculado.`,()=>
      askConfirm(cf,'Confirmar troca',`<b>Confirmacao final.</b> Todos os vencimentos de ${esc(e.codigo)} passam a contar pelo relogio novo.`,async()=>{
        const d=Math.round((B-A)*10)/10,sh_=v=>v===''||v==null||isNaN(Number(v))?v:Math.round((Number(v)+d)*10)/10;
        const ok=await safe(async()=>{
          await S.db.collection('trocas').add({tipo:'hmtroca',ts:Date.now(),equipId:e.id,data:dt,horimetro:B,antigo:A,delta:d,resp:sh.querySelector('#thm-r').value.trim(),obs:sh.querySelector('#thm-o').value.trim(),itens:[],exemplo:!!e.exemplo});
          await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({horimetro:B,horimetroData:dt,horimetroReg:today(),
            itens:(e.itens||[]).map(i=>({...i,ultimaH:sh_(i.ultimaH),proxH:sh_(i.proxH)})),...(e.combBase&&e.combBase.h!=null?{combBase:{...e.combBase,h:sh_(e.combBase.h)}}:{})})},'Troca de horimetro registrada');
        if(ok){const D=hmDraft();if(D.v&&D.v[e.id]!=null){delete D.v[e.id];hmDraftSave(D)}goBack()}}))}}});
}
/* + › Desfazer troca de horimetro: escolhe a troca e confirma duas vezes.
   So a troca mais recente de cada equipamento pode ser desfeita (as outras dependem dela). */
function openDesfazHm(){
  nav('thmx',()=>openDesfazHm());
  const ts=S.trocas.filter(t=>t.tipo==='hmtroca'&&eqById(t.equipId)).sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||'')||(b.ts||0)-(a.ts||0));
  const latest=new Set();const seen=new Set();ts.forEach(t=>{if(!seen.has(t.equipId)){seen.add(t.equipId);latest.add(t.id)}});
  /* cada troca so pode ser desfeita ate 30 dias depois de registrada */
  const reg=t=>t.ts||new Date((t.data||today())+'T12:00').getTime(),vivo=t=>Date.now()-reg(t)<=30*864e5,ate=t=>sdate(iso(new Date(reg(t)+30*864e5)));
  const lst=ts.filter(vivo);
  openSheet({title:'Desfazer troca de horimetro',sub:'Ate 30 dias apos o registro',
  body:lst.length?`<div class="ilist">${lst.map(t=>{const e=eqById(t.equipId),ok=latest.has(t.id);return `<div class="ia"><button type="button" class="ia-h" data-ia aria-expanded="false"><span class="ia-t"><b class="num">${esc(e.codigo)}</b> · ${fdate(t.data)}</span><span class="hh-hm num">${nf(t.antigo)} → ${nf(t.horimetro)} h</span>${I.chev}</button>
    <div class="ia-b" hidden><div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Relogio antigo</span><b class="num">${nf(t.antigo)} h</b></div><div class="r"><span>Relogio novo</span><b class="num">${nf(t.horimetro)} h</b></div><div class="r"><span>Responsavel</span><b>${esc(t.resp||'—')}</b></div>${t.obs?`<div class="r"><span>Motivo</span><b>${esc(t.obs)}</b></div>`:''}</div>
    ${ok?`<p class="note" style="margin:8px 0 0">Pode ser desfeita ate ${ate(t)}.</p><div class="pn-bt"><button type="button" class="btn sm danger" data-ux="${esc(t.id)}">Desfazer esta troca</button></div><div data-uxc="${esc(t.id)}"></div>`:'<p class="note" style="margin:8px 0 0">Ha uma troca mais recente neste equipamento. Desfaca aquela primeiro.</p>'}</div></div>`}).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty">Nenhuma troca de horimetro nos ultimos 30 dias.</div>',
  mount:sh=>sh.querySelectorAll('[data-ux]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const t=S.trocas.find(x=>x.id===b.dataset.ux),e=t&&eqById(t.equipId);if(!e)return;const cf=sh.querySelector(`[data-uxc="${CSS.escape(t.id)}"]`);
    askConfirm(cf,'Continuar',`Desfazer a troca de horimetro de <b>${esc(e.codigo)}</b> de ${fdate(t.data)}?`,()=>
    askConfirm(cf,'Desfazer troca',`<b>Confirmacao final.</b> O horimetro e todos os vencimentos de ${esc(e.codigo)} voltam para a contagem do relogio antigo.`,async()=>{
      const d=Number(t.delta)||0,bk=v=>v===''||v==null||isNaN(Number(v))?v:Math.round((Number(v)-d)*10)/10;
      if(await safe(async()=>{await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({horimetro:bk(e.horimetro),itens:(e.itens||[]).map(i=>({...i,ultimaH:bk(i.ultimaH),proxH:bk(i.proxH)})),...(e.combBase&&e.combBase.h!=null?{combBase:{...e.combBase,h:bk(e.combBase.h)}}:{})});await S.db.doc('trocas/'+t.id).delete()},'Troca de horimetro desfeita'))goBack()}))})});
}
function formHm(id){
  nav('hm:'+id,()=>formHm(id));
  const e=eqById(id);
  openSheet({title:'Horimetro',sub:`${esc(e.codigo)} · ${e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?`ultima leitura ${nf(e.horimetro)} h`:'sem leitura anterior'}`,
  body:`<form id="fh"><div class="fset">
    <label class="f">Nova leitura (h)<input id="h-val" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" required placeholder="${e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?'Minimo '+nf(e.horimetro):''}" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:22px;min-height:58px"></label>
    <p class="warnline" id="h-warn"></p>
    <label class="f">Data da leitura<input id="h-dt" type="date" value="${today()}"></label></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="fh">Salvar leitura</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{const v=sh.querySelector('#h-val'),w=sh.querySelector('#h-warn');
    v.oninput=()=>{w.textContent=v.value!==''&&hmLow(e,v.value)?`Menor que a ultima leitura (${nf(e.horimetro)} h).`:''};
    const hmStH={ok:true,salto:true};sh.querySelector('#h-warn').insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div id="h-g"></div>');
    sh.querySelector('#fh').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();const e1=eqById(id)||e,dtH=sh.querySelector('#h-dt').value||today();if(!hmStH.corr&&hmLow(e1,v.value)){w.textContent='';if(!hmGuard(sh.querySelector('#h-g'),e1,Number(v.value),dtH,hmStH,()=>sh.querySelector('#fh').onsubmit({preventDefault(){}})))return}if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+id).update({horimetro:Number(v.value),horimetroData:sh.querySelector('#h-dt').value,horimetroReg:today()}),'Horimetro atualizado'))after(()=>openEq(id))}}});
}
function stockOpts(sel){const o=s=>`<option value="${esc(s.id)}"${sel===s.id?' selected':''}>${esc(stCod(s))} · ${esc(s.desc)}</option>`,its=S.estoque.filter(s=>!isKit(s)).sort(byCode),kits=S.estoque.filter(isKit).sort(byCode);
  return `<option value="">Nao ligar ao estoque</option>`+(kits.length?`<optgroup label="Itens">${its.map(o).join('')}</optgroup><optgroup label="Kits">${kits.map(o).join('')}</optgroup>`:its.map(o).join(''))}
/* item do plano no Editar equipamento: linha fechada (nome, codigo, quantidade); tocar abre os campos e o Remover */
function planRow(it,aberto){const P=esc(it.pid||uid());const cod=it.espec||(it.estoqueId&&stById(it.estoqueId)?stCod(stById(it.estoqueId)):'');
  return `<div class="tr-i pe2${aberto?' op':''}" data-pid="${P}"><div class="tr-h" data-trh role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="${!!aberto}"><div class="main"><b data-pn>${esc(it.desc||'Novo item')}</b><div class="s"><span class="num" data-pc>${esc(cod)}</span></div></div><span class="pe-q num" data-pq>${it.qtd!==''&&it.qtd!=null?nf(it.qtd,1)+' '+esc(it.un||''):''}</span>${I.chev}</div>
  <div class="tr-b pe-b"${aberto?'':' hidden'}>
  <label class="f w">Descricao<input data-p="desc" value="${esc(it.desc||'')}" placeholder="Oleo do motor"></label>
  <label class="f">Tipo<select data-p="tipo">${catOpts(it.tipo||'oleo',true)}</select></label>
  <label class="f">Especificacao<input data-p="espec" value="${esc(it.espec||'')}" placeholder="15W-40 / cod."></label>
  <label class="f">Qtd<input data-p="qtd" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(it.qtd??'')}"></label>
  <label class="f">Unid.<input data-p="un" value="${esc(it.un||(it.tipo?catUn(it.tipo):'un'))}"></label>
  <label class="f w">Estoque<select data-p="estoqueId">${stockOpts(it.estoqueId)}</select></label>
  <button type="button" class="btn danger w" data-rm>Remover do plano</button></div></div>`;
}

      if(id&&hm!==''){let gb=sh.querySelector('#e-hmg');if(!gb){sh.querySelector('#e-horimetro').closest('label').insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div id="e-hmg" class="w"></div>');gb=sh.querySelector('#e-hmg')}
        if(!hmGuard(gb,eqById(id)||e,Number(hm),today(),hmStE,()=>saveEq()))return;gb.innerHTML=''}
      const body={codigo:g('codigo').toUpperCase(),tipo:g('tipo'),marca:g('marca'),modelo:g('modelo'),ano:g('ano'),serie:g('serie'),placa:g('placa').toUpperCase(),status:(id&&eqById(id)||e).status||'ativo',
        horimetro:hm===''?'':Number(hm),intervaloPrev:g('ivp')===''?'':Number(g('ivp')),usoDiario:g('uso')===''?'':Number(g('uso')),alocacao:g('aloc'),area:g('aloc')==='DM1'?g('area'):'',frente:g('aloc')==='DM1'&&g('area')!==RES?g('frente'):'',motor:g('motor'),posse:'Rondaflex Ltda',locador:g('locador'),obs:g('obs'),itens,foto:fotoNow[0]||'',docVeiculo:docNow.docVeiculo||null,notaFiscal:docNow.notaFiscal||null,
        horimetroData:(id&&Number(hm)===Number(e.horimetro))?(e.horimetroData||''):today(),horimetroReg:(hm!==''&&(!id||Number(hm)>(Number(e.horimetro)||0)))?today():(e.horimetroReg||''),exemplo:!!e.exemplo};
      if(S.equipsAll.some(x=>x.id!==id&&String(x.codigo).toLowerCase()===body.codigo.toLowerCase())){toast('Ja existe um equipamento com essa TAG.');return}
      /* edicao: mantem os campos que este formulario nao mostra (base do combustivel, equipamento novo, correcoes...) */
      if(id){const cur=eqById(id)||e,{id:_x,...resto}=cur;Object.keys(body).forEach(k=>delete resto[k]);Object.assign(body,resto,{...body})}
      const ref=id?S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+id):S.db.collection('equipamentos').doc();
      if(await safe(()=>ref.set(body),id?'Equipamento atualizado':'Equipamento cadastrado')){commitPhotos(fotoOrig,fotoNow);const docIds=d=>d?[d.id,...(d.pages||[])].filter((x,i,a)=>x&&a.indexOf(x)===i):[];DOCS.forEach(([k])=>{const n=docNow[k],o=docOrig[k];docIds(n).forEach(x=>PEND.delete(x));if(o&&(!n||n.id!==o.id))docIds(o).forEach(dropAsset)});after(()=>openEq(ref.id))}
    };
  }});
}

/* ---------- registrar preventiva: escolha ---------- */
function formRegistrar(eqId,menu){
  nav('reg',()=>formRegistrar(eqId,menu));
  const EDT='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 20h4L19 9l-4-4L4 16z"/><path d="M13.5 6.5l4 4"/></svg>';
  const mi=(a,ic,t,d)=>`<button class="mi" data-rg="${a}"><span class="ic">${ic}</span><span class="tx"><b>${t}</b><span>${d}</span></span>${I.chev}</button>`;
  const AIR='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 8h11a3 3 0 1 0-3-3"/><path d="M3 12h15a3 3 0 1 1-3 3"/><path d="M3 16h7"/></svg>';
  openSheet({title:'Registrar preventiva',sub:'O que foi feito?',body:`<div class="menu">${mi('ar',AIR,'Filtros de ar','Motor ou ar condicionado')}${mi('prev',I.manut,'Preventiva','Troca de oleos e filtros do plano')}${menu?mi('ed',EDT,'Editar preventiva','Corrige data, horimetro e pecas usadas'):''}</div>`,
    mount:sh=>sh.querySelectorAll('[data-rg]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>b.dataset.rg==='ar'?formFiltroAr(eqId):b.dataset.rg==='ed'?formPickTr():formTroca(eqId))});
}
function formFiltroAr(eqId,sub){
  if(!sub){
    nav('ar:esc',()=>formFiltroAr(eqId));
    const mi=(v,t,d)=>`<button class="mi" data-ar="${v}"><span class="tx"><b>${t}</b><span>${d}</span></span>${I.chev}</button>`;
    openSheet({title:'Filtros de ar',sub:'Qual filtro?',body:`<div class="menu">${mi('Motor','Motor','Filtro de ar do motor')}${mi('Ar condicionado','Ar condicionado','Filtro da cabine / ar condicionado')}</div>`,
      mount:sh=>sh.querySelectorAll('[data-ar]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formFiltroAr(eqId,b.dataset.ar))});
    return;
  }
  nav('ar:'+sub,()=>formFiltroAr(eqId,sub));
  const eqs=S.eqCtl.slice().sort(byCode);
  const cur=eqId&&eqById(eqId)?eqId:'';
  const filtros=S.estoque.filter(s=>s.tipo==='filtro'&&!isKit(s)).sort(byCode);
  openSheet({title:'Filtro de ar · '+esc(sub),sub:'Registre a troca ou limpeza do filtro',
  body:`${S.ajuste?AJ_BANNER:''}<form id="fa"><div class="fset">
    <label class="f">Equipamento<select id="a-eq" required data-eqs><option value="">Escolha…</option>${eqs.map(e=>`<option value="${esc(e.id)}"${cur===e.id?' selected':''}>${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(e.tipo)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
    <div class="f">Servico <span class="hint">marque um ou os dois</span><div class="seg ar-seg" role="group" aria-label="Servico: marque um ou os dois"><button type="button" data-sv="Troca" aria-pressed="true">Troca</button><button type="button" data-sv="Limpeza" aria-pressed="false">Limpeza</button></div></div>
    <div class="grid"><label class="f">Data<input id="a-dt" type="date" required value="${today()}"></label>
    <label class="f">Horimetro (h)<input id="a-hm" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" required></label></div></div>
    <div class="fset" id="a-stw" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Filtros trocados</h3>
      <div id="a-sug"></div>
      <div class="sug-w t-add"><div class="search">${I.search}<input id="a-q" type="search" placeholder="Adicionar filtro por codigo ou nome" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Adicionar filtro"></div><div class="sug" id="a-qh"></div></div>
      ${rdBtn('a-cam','Adicionar filtro pela camera')}<div id="a-its"></div></div>
    <div id="a-plan"></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><label class="f">Responsavel<input id="a-resp" placeholder="Mecanico ou oficina"></label>
    <label class="f">Observacoes<textarea id="a-obs"></textarea></label></div><div id="a-hmg"></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="fa">Registrar ${esc(sub==='Motor'?'filtro de ar':'filtro da cabine')}</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{
    /* Troca e Limpeza podem ser marcadas juntas (servico "Troca e limpeza"); pelo menos uma fica marcada */
    const SV={Troca:true,Limpeza:false};let sv='Troca';const tr=()=>SV.Troca;const sel=sh.querySelector('#a-eq'),hm=sh.querySelector('#a-hm');
    /* filtros do lancamento: um ou mais, cada um com a sua quantidade */
    const L=[];const box=sh.querySelector('#a-its'),sug=sh.querySelector('#a-sug');
    const drawL=()=>{box.innerHTML=L.length?`<div class="kl ar-kl">${L.map((c,i)=>{const x=stById(c.id);return `<div class="kr"><span class="n"><b>${esc(x?x.desc:'Item removido')}</b><small class="num">${esc(x?stCod(x):'')}${x?` · saldo ${nf(stQtd(x),1)}`:''}</small></span><input type="number" step="1" min="0" inputmode="numeric" value="${esc(c.q)}" data-aq="${i}" aria-label="Quantidade"><span class="u">${esc(x?stUn(x):'un')}</span><button type="button" class="x" data-ax="${i}" aria-label="Tirar">✕</button></div>`}).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="note" style="margin:10px 2px 0">Nenhum filtro adicionado. Sem filtro, o servico e registrado sem baixa no estoque.</p>';
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-aq]').forEach(q=>q.oninput=()=>{L[Number(q.dataset.aq)].q=q.value});
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-ax]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{L.splice(Number(b.dataset.ax),1);drawL();drawSug()});drawSug()};
    const add=x=>{if(!x)return;if(isKit(x)){toast('Escolha um filtro, nao um kit.');return}if(L.some(c=>c.id===x.id)){toast(`${x.desc} ja esta na lista`);return}L.push({id:x.id,q:1});drawL()};
    /* sugestoes: filtros de ar com encaixe neste equipamento (motor ou cabine primeiro) */
    const drawSug=()=>{const eid=sel.value,fit=filtros.filter(s=>(s.encaixe||[]).includes(eid)&&isAr(s)),pref=fit.filter(s=>sub==='Motor'?!isCabine(s):isCabine(s)),l=[...pref,...fit.filter(s=>!pref.includes(s))].filter(s=>!L.some(c=>c.id===s.id));
      sug.innerHTML=l.length?`<div class="ti-g ar-sg">Encaixe de fabrica · toque para adicionar</div><div class="tsec-l">${l.map(s=>esRow(s,{attr:`data-as="${esc(s.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'';
      sug.querySelectorAll('[data-as]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>add(stById(b.dataset.as)))};
    const q=sh.querySelector('#a-q'),qh=sh.querySelector('#a-qh');
    q.oninput=()=>{const k=nzCod(q.value);if(!k){qh.innerHTML='';return}
      const sc=x=>{const a=nzCod(stCod(x)),b=nzCod(x.ref);return a===k||b===k?0:a.startsWith(k)||b.startsWith(k)?1:a.includes(k)||b.includes(k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k)?2:9};
      const m=filtros.map(x=>[sc(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,6).map(a=>a[1]);
      qh.innerHTML=m.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-ap2="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum filtro encontrado no estoque.</div>';
      qh.querySelectorAll('[data-ap2]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{q.value='';qh.innerHTML='';add(stById(b.dataset.ap2))})};
    const cm=sh.querySelector('#a-cam');if(cm)cm.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(!v.item){toast('Filtro nao encontrado no estoque.');return}add(v.item)}});
    /* itens do plano do equipamento que esta troca cumpre (pre-marcados pelo nome) */
    const fillPlan=()=>{const e=eqById(sel.value),bx=sh.querySelector('#a-plan');const its=(e&&e.itens||[]).filter(i=>/\bar\b/i.test(itNome(i)+' '+(i.desc||'')));
      if(!its.length||!tr()){bx.innerHTML='';return}
      const cab=i=>/condicionad|cabine/i.test(itNome(i)+' '+(i.desc||'')),pre=i=>sub==='Motor'?!cab(i):cab(i);
      bx.innerHTML=`<div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Itens do plano cumpridos</h3><div class="list">${its.map(i=>`<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" data-pl="${esc(i.pid)}"${pre(i)?' checked':''}><span class="box"></span><div class="main"><b>${esc(itNome(i))}</b></div></label>`).join('')}</div></div>`};
    const fillHm=()=>{const e=eqById(sel.value);hm.value=e&&e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?e.horimetro:'';
      /* troca de equipamento: sugere de novo o filtro de encaixe (so se a lista estiver vazia) */
      if(!L.length){const fit=filtros.filter(s=>(s.encaixe||[]).includes(sel.value)&&isAr(s)),pref=fit.filter(s=>sub==='Motor'?!isCabine(s):isCabine(s));if(pref.length===1)L.push({id:pref[0].id,q:1})}
      drawL();fillPlan()};sel.onchange=fillHm;fillHm();
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-sv]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const k=b.dataset.sv;if(SV[k]&&!SV[k==='Troca'?'Limpeza':'Troca'])return;SV[k]=!SV[k];b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',SV[k]);
      sv=SV.Troca&&SV.Limpeza?'Troca e limpeza':SV.Troca?'Troca':'Limpeza';sh.querySelector('#a-stw').hidden=!tr();fillPlan()});
    const hmSt={ok:false};[hm,sh.querySelector('#a-dt')].forEach(x=>x.addEventListener('input',()=>{hmSt.ok=false;hmSt.salto=false}));sel.addEventListener('change',()=>{hmSt.ok=false;hmSt.salto=false});
    sh.querySelector('#fa').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      const e=eqById(sel.value);if(!e)return;
      const h0=Number(hm.value),dt0=sh.querySelector('#a-dt').value,g=hmGuard(sh.querySelector('#a-hmg'),e,h0,dt0,hmSt,()=>sh.querySelector('#fa').onsubmit({preventDefault(){}}));if(!g)return;
      const h=g.h,dl=g.dl,dt=dt0,nome=`Filtro de ar ${sub==='Motor'?'do motor':'do ar condicionado'}`;
      const lst=tr()?L.map(c=>({st:stById(c.id),q:Number(c.q)||0})).filter(x=>x.st):[];
      const itens=lst.length?lst.map(({st,q})=>({pid:'',desc:st.desc,espec:stCod(st),qtd:q,un:st.un||'un',estoqueId:st.id})):[{pid:'',desc:`${nome} (${sv.toLowerCase()})`,espec:'',qtd:0,un:'un',estoqueId:''}];
      const ok=await safe(async()=>{
        const isAj=!!S.ajuste;
        await S.db.collection('trocas').add({tipo:'filtro_ar',sub,servico:sv,ajuste:isAj,equipId:e.id,data:dt,horimetro:h0,...(dl?{hmConv:h}:{}),resp:sh.querySelector('#a-resp').value.trim(),obs:sh.querySelector('#a-obs').value.trim(),proximaH:'',itens});
        const done=new Set([...sh.querySelectorAll('[data-pl]:checked')].map(c=>c.dataset.pl));
        const upd={};if(done.size)upd.itens=e.itens.map(i=>done.has(i.pid)?{...i,ultimaH:h,ultimaData:dt,proxH:'',reprog:''}:i);
        if(h>(Number(e.horimetro)||0)){upd.horimetro=h;upd.horimetroData=dt;upd.horimetroReg=today()}
        if(Object.keys(upd).length)await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update(upd);
        if(!isAj)for(const {st,q} of lst){if(!q)continue;await logBaixa(st.id,q,dt,e.id);await S.db.doc('estoque/'+st.id).update({qtd:Math.round(((Number(st.qtd)||0)-q)*10)/10})}
      },`Filtro de ar registrado (${sv.toLowerCase()})`);
      if(ok)finishReg(e.id);
    };
  }});
}

/* ---------- troca ---------- */
function formTroca(eqId){
  nav('fp:new',()=>formTroca(eqId));
  const eqs=S.eqCtl.slice().sort(byCode);
  if(!eqs.length){NAV.pop();toast('Cadastre um equipamento antes.');return}
  const cur=eqId&&eqById(eqId)?eqId:'';
  openSheet({title:'Registrar preventiva',sub:'Cronograma e estoque se ajustam',
  body:`${S.ajuste?AJ_BANNER:''}<form id="ft"><div class="fset">
    <label class="f">Equipamento<select id="t-eq" required data-eqs><option value="">Escolha…</option>${eqs.map(e=>`<option value="${esc(e.id)}"${cur===e.id?' selected':''}>${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(e.tipo)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
    <div class="grid"><label class="f">Data<input id="t-dt" type="date" required value="${today()}"></label>
    <label class="f">Horimetro (h)<input id="t-hm" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" required></label></div>
    <label class="f">Responsavel<input id="t-resp" placeholder="Mecanico ou oficina"></label></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="f">Intervalo <span class="hint">opcional</span><div class="seg iv-seg" role="group" aria-label="Intervalo">${[250,300,350,400,500].map(v=>`<button type="button" data-iv="${v}" aria-pressed="false">${nf(v)} h</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    <label class="f">Proxima (h) <span class="hint">opcional</span><input id="t-prox" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="Automatico"></label>
    <p class="note" id="t-proxhint" style="margin:0">Em branco, a proxima e agendada sozinha pelo intervalo de cada item.</p></div>
    <div class="lbl" style="margin-top:16px">Itens trocados <span class="hint" style="text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-weight:400">vencidos ja entram</span></div>
    <div id="t-kits" hidden></div><div class="sug-w t-add"><div class="search">${I.search}<input id="t-q" type="search" placeholder="Adicionar item por codigo ou nome" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Adicionar item"></div><div class="sug" id="t-qh"></div></div>
    ${rdBtn('t-cam','Adicionar item pela camera')}<div class="list" id="t-items"></div><div class="list" id="t-extra" hidden></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><label class="f">Observacoes<textarea id="t-obs"></textarea></label></div><div id="t-eqv"></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="ft">Registrar preventiva</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{
    const sel=sh.querySelector('#t-eq'),box=sh.querySelector('#t-items'),hm=sh.querySelector('#t-hm');
    const fill=()=>{const e=eqById(sel.value);if(!e){box.innerHTML='<div class="empty">Escolha o equipamento.</div>';sh.querySelector('#t-kits').hidden=true;return}
      hm.value=e.horimetro||'';
      /* lista mostra so o que esta no lancamento; tocar na linha abre codigo usado, intervalo e retirar */
      const row=it=>{const s=itemStatus(e,it),st=it.estoqueId&&stById(it.estoqueId),on=s.cls==='bad'||s.cls==='warn',P=esc(it.pid);return `<div class="tr-i" data-row="${P}"${on?'':' hidden'}><input type="checkbox" hidden data-pid="${P}"${on?' checked':''}>
        <div class="tr-h" data-trh role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="main"><b>${esc(itNome(it))}</b><div class="s"><span class="pill ${s.cls}">${s.label}</span><span class="num" data-cdv="${P}">${esc(it.espec||(st?stCod(st):''))}</span>${st?`<span>${isKit(st)?'disponivel':'saldo'} ${nf(stQtd(st),1)} ${esc(stUn(st))}</span>`:''}</div></div>
        <input class="q" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" data-q="${P}" value="${esc(it.qtd??'')}" aria-label="Quantidade usada de ${esc(it.desc)}"><span class="u">${esc(it.un||'')}</span>${I.chev}</div>
        <div class="tr-b" hidden><label class="f">Codigo usado<input class="cd" data-cd="${P}" value="${esc(it.espec||'')}" placeholder="Codigo usado" autocapitalize="characters" aria-label="Codigo usado em ${esc(itNome(it))}"></label>${st&&isKit(st)?'':ivSelect(ivPad(e,it),e.intervaloPrev,`data-ivp="${P}"`)}${st&&isKit(st)?`<button type="button" class="btn" data-kv>Ver itens do kit</button><div class="kv-w" hidden>${kitLista(st,it.qtd||1)}</div>`:''}<button type="button" class="btn" data-trm="${P}">Retirar do lancamento</button></div></div>`};
      const its=e.itens||[];
      box.innerHTML=its.length?its.map(row).join('')+'<div class="empty" id="t-none" hidden>Nenhum item no lancamento. Pesquise acima para adicionar.</div>':`<div class="empty"><b>Sem oleos e filtros no plano</b>Monte o plano pelo + › Editar cadastro › Equipamento para registrar trocas.</div>`;
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-ivp]').forEach(i=>{i.onclick=ev=>ev.stopPropagation()});
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-cd]').forEach(i=>{i.oninput=()=>{const p=i.dataset.cd,cb=box.querySelector(`input[type=checkbox][data-pid="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`),v=box.querySelector(`[data-cdv="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`);if(v)v.textContent=i.value.trim().toUpperCase();if(cb&&!cb.checked){cb.checked=true;box.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))}}});
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-trm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const p=b.dataset.trm,cb=box.querySelector(`input[type=checkbox][data-pid="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`);if(cb)cb.checked=false;const r=b.closest('.tr-i');r.classList.remove('op');r.querySelector('.tr-b').hidden=true;r.querySelector('[data-trh]').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');box.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));toast('Retirado do lancamento')});
      extras.length=0;drawX();
      /* kits que servem neste equipamento */
      const kb=sh.querySelector('#t-kits'),ks=eqCompat(e).kits;kb.hidden=!ks.length;
      kb.innerHTML=ks.length?`<div class="ti-g">Kits compativeis</div><div class="list">${ks.map(k=>`<button type="button" class="kt-r" data-kit="${esc(k.id)}"><span class="kt-n"><b>${esc(k.desc)}</b><small class="num">${esc(stCod(k))} · ${kitComps(k).length} itens · disponivel ${nf(stQtd(k),1)}</small></span><span class="kt-a">Adicionar kit</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
      kb.querySelectorAll('[data-kit]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(!b.classList.contains('on'))addKit(stById(b.dataset.kit))});
      };
    /* adicionar item (texto ou camera): se e do plano, marca a linha; se nao, entra como item avulso com baixa no estoque */
    const extras=[],xb=sh.querySelector('#t-extra');
    const xOpen=new Set();
    const drawX=()=>{xb.hidden=!extras.length;xb.innerHTML=`<div class="ti-g">Itens adicionados</div>`+extras.map((x,k)=>{const st=x.s,op=xOpen.has(st.id);return `<div class="tr-i${op?' op':''}" data-xrow="${k}">
        <div class="tr-h" data-trh role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="${op}"><div class="main"><b>${esc(st.desc)}</b><div class="s"><span class="num">${esc(stCod(st))}</span><span>${isKit(st)?'disponivel':'saldo'} ${nf(stQtd(st),1)} ${esc(stUn(st))}</span></div></div>
        <input class="q" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" inputmode="decimal" data-xq="${k}" value="${esc(x.q)}" aria-label="Quantidade"><span class="u">${esc(isKit(st)?'kit':st.un||'un')}</span>${I.chev}</div>
        <div class="tr-b"${op?'':' hidden'}>${isKit(st)?'':ivSelect(x.iv??((eqById(sel.value)||{}).ivEnc||{})[st.id]??'',(eqById(sel.value)||{}).intervaloPrev,`data-xiv="${k}"`)}${isKit(st)?`<button type="button" class="btn" data-kv>Ver itens do kit</button><div class="kv-w" hidden>${kitLista(st,x.q)}</div>`:''}<button type="button" class="btn" data-xr="${k}">Retirar do lancamento</button></div></div>`}).join('');
      xb.querySelectorAll('[data-xr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const x=extras.splice(Number(b.dataset.xr),1)[0];if(x)xOpen.delete(x.s.id);drawX();hint();toast('Retirado do lancamento')});
      xb.querySelectorAll('[data-xq]').forEach(q=>{q.oninput=()=>{extras[Number(q.dataset.xq)].q=q.value}});
      xb.querySelectorAll('[data-xiv]').forEach(v=>{v.onclick=ev=>ev.stopPropagation();v.onchange=()=>{extras[Number(v.dataset.xiv)].iv=v.value}});sh.querySelectorAll('[data-kit]').forEach(b=>{const on=extras.some(x=>x.s.id===b.dataset.kit);b.querySelector('.kt-a').textContent=on?'Adicionado':'Adicionar kit';b.classList.toggle('on',on)});vis()};
    /* mostra so as linhas que estao no lancamento */
    const vis=()=>{let n=0;box.querySelectorAll('[data-row]').forEach(r=>{const on=r.querySelector('input[type=checkbox]').checked;r.hidden=!on;if(on)n++});const z=box.querySelector('#t-none');if(z)z.hidden=!!(n||extras.length)};
    box.addEventListener('change',()=>vis());
    sh.addEventListener('click',ev=>{const h=ev.target.closest('[data-trh]');if(!h||ev.target.closest('input,select,button:not([data-trh])'))return;const r=h.closest('.tr-i'),b=r.querySelector('.tr-b'),o=b.hidden;b.hidden=!o;r.classList.toggle('op',o);h.setAttribute('aria-expanded',o);
      const xr=r.dataset.xrow;if(xr!=null){const x=extras[Number(xr)];if(x){o?xOpen.add(x.s.id):xOpen.delete(x.s.id)}}});
    sh.addEventListener('keydown',ev=>{const h=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('[data-trh]');if(h&&ev.target===h&&(ev.key==='Enter'||ev.key===' ')){ev.preventDefault();h.click()}});
    const addPeca=st=>{const e2=eqById(sel.value);if(!e2){toast('Escolha o equipamento antes.');return}if(!st)return;
      const it=(e2.itens||[]).find(i=>i.estoqueId===st.id||(isKit(stById(i.estoqueId)||{})&&kitComps(stById(i.estoqueId)).some(c=>c.s.id===st.id))||(itCod(i)&&nzCod(itCod(i))===nzCod(stCod(st))));
      if(it){const cb=box.querySelector(`input[type=checkbox][data-pid="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`),cdI=box.querySelector(`[data-cd="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`);
        if(cdI){cdI.value=stCod(st);cdI.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'))}if(cb&&!cb.checked){cb.checked=true;box.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))}
        const r=box.querySelector(`[data-row="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`);r&&r.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});toast(`${itNome(it)} adicionado`);return}
      if(!extras.some(x=>x.s.id===st.id))extras.push({s:st,q:1});drawX();hint();
      const r=xb.querySelector(`[data-xrow="${extras.findIndex(x=>x.s.id===st.id)}"]`);r&&r.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});toast(`${st.desc} adicionado`)};
    /* kit: entra como item adicionado (baixa dos componentes) e marca no plano os itens que ele cobre, sem baixa dupla */
    const addKit=k=>{const e2=eqById(sel.value);if(!e2||!k)return;
      const inPlan=(e2.itens||[]).find(i=>i.estoqueId===k.id);if(inPlan){addPeca(k);return}

      (e2.itens||[]).forEach(it=>{const so=stOfIt(it);if(!so||!comp.has(so.id))return;const cb=box.querySelector(`input[type=checkbox][data-pid="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`);if(!cb)return;
        cb.checked=true;const q=box.querySelector(`[data-q="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`);if(q)q.value=0;const cd=box.querySelector(`[data-cd="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`);if(cd&&!cd.value)cd.value=stCod(so);n++});
      box.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
      if(!extras.some(x=>x.s.id===k.id))extras.push({s:k,q:1});drawX();hint();
      toast(n?`${k.desc} adicionado · ${n} ${n===1?'item do plano marcado':'itens do plano marcados'} (baixa pelo kit)`:`${k.desc} adicionado`)};
    const tq=sh.querySelector('#t-q'),tqh=sh.querySelector('#t-qh');
    tq.oninput=()=>{const k=nzCod(tq.value);if(!k){tqh.innerHTML='';return}
      const score=x=>{const a=nzCod(stCod(x)),b=nzCod(x.ref);return a===k||b===k?0:a.startsWith(k)||b.startsWith(k)?1:a.includes(k)||b.includes(k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k)?2:9};
      const m=S.estoque.map(x=>[score(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,6).map(a=>a[1]);
      const v=tq.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      tqh.innerHTML=(m.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-tk="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum item encontrado no estoque.</div>')+`<button type="button" class="et-fora" id="t-cad">Cadastrar item ${esc(v)}</button>`;
      tqh.querySelectorAll('[data-tk]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{tq.value='';tqh.innerHTML='';addPeca(stById(b.dataset.tk))});
      tqh.querySelector('#t-cad').onclick=()=>{if(!eqById(sel.value)){toast('Escolha o equipamento antes.');return}const d=snap();tq.value='';tqh.innerHTML='';formTroca.draft=d;
        const cb=async sid=>{d.add=sid};cb.msg='Item cadastrado';formStock(null,{ref:v},cb)}};
    const tc=sh.querySelector('#t-cam');if(tc)tc.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(!v.item){toast('Peca nao encontrada no estoque.');return}addPeca(v.item)}});
    /* rascunho: guarda o que foi preenchido ao sair para cadastrar um item novo, e devolve na volta */
    const val=q=>sh.querySelector(q).value;
    const snap=()=>({eq:sel.value,dt:val('#t-dt'),hm:val('#t-hm'),resp:val('#t-resp'),obs:val('#t-obs'),prox:val('#t-prox'),iv,
      chk:[...box.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox][data-pid]')].map(c=>{const p=c.dataset.pid;return [p,c.checked,(box.querySelector(`[data-q="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`)||{}).value,(box.querySelector(`[data-cd="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`)||{}).value]}),
      ex:extras.map(x=>[x.s.id,x.q])});
    sh._restore=()=>{const d=formTroca.draft;formTroca.draft=null;if(!d)return;
      if(d.eq&&eqById(d.eq)){const e1=eqById(d.eq);sel.value=d.eq;fill();const ei=sh.querySelector('.eqs-in');if(ei){ei.value=`${e1.codigo} · ${e1.tipo||''}`;ei.setCustomValidity('')}}
      sh.querySelector('#t-dt').value=d.dt;hm.value=d.hm;sh.querySelector('#t-resp').value=d.resp;sh.querySelector('#t-obs').value=d.obs;sh.querySelector('#t-prox').value=d.prox;
      if(d.iv){iv=d.iv;sh.querySelectorAll('[data-iv]').forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',Number(x.dataset.iv)===iv))}
      d.chk.forEach(([p,c,q,cd])=>{const cb=box.querySelector(`input[type=checkbox][data-pid="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`);if(!cb)return;cb.checked=c;const qi=box.querySelector(`[data-q="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`);if(qi&&q!=null)qi.value=q;const ci=box.querySelector(`[data-cd="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`);if(ci&&cd!=null){ci.value=cd;const v=box.querySelector(`[data-cdv="${CSS.escape(p)}"]`);if(v&&cd)v.textContent=cd}});vis();
      d.ex.forEach(([id,q])=>{const st=stById(id);if(st)extras.push({s:st,q})});drawX();hint();
      if(d.add){const st=stById(d.add);if(st)addPeca(st);else setTimeout(()=>{const s2=stById(d.add);if(s2)addPeca(s2)},800)}};
    const ph=sh.querySelector('#t-proxhint'),pin=sh.querySelector('#t-prox');
    let iv=0;sh.querySelectorAll('[data-iv]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const v=Number(b.dataset.iv);iv=iv===v?0:v;sh.querySelectorAll('[data-iv]').forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',Number(x.dataset.iv)===iv));hint()});
    const hint=()=>{const e=eqById(sel.value);if(e&&iv&&!pin.value.trim()){const h=Number(hm.value)||0;ph.textContent=`Intervalo de ${nf(iv)} h: a proxima fica para ${nf(h+iv)} h.`;return}
      if(!e||pin.value.trim()){ph.textContent=pin.value.trim()?'Todos os itens marcados ficam agendados para esse horimetro.':'Em branco, a proxima e agendada sozinha pelo intervalo de cada item.';return}
      const h=Number(hm.value)||0;const ivs=[...box.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]:checked')].map(c=>e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===c.dataset.pid)).filter(Boolean).map(i=>itIv(e,i));
      const w=ivs.filter(Boolean);ph.textContent=!ivs.length?'Adicione os itens trocados.':!w.length?'Sem intervalo na ficha do equipamento. Anote a proxima aqui ou preencha o intervalo na ficha.':`Em branco, fica agendada automaticamente para ${[...new Set(w)].map(v=>nf(h+v)+' h').join(' / ')}.`};
    pin.oninput=hint;hm.addEventListener('input',hint);box.addEventListener('change',hint);
    sel.onchange=()=>{fill();hint()};fill();hint();setTimeout(()=>sh._restore&&sh._restore(),0);
    const hmSt={ok:false};[hm,sh.querySelector('#t-dt'),sel].forEach(x=>x.addEventListener(x===sel?'change':'input',()=>{hmSt.ok=false;hmSt.salto=false}));
    sh.querySelector('#ft').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      const e=eqById(sel.value);if(!e)return;
      const picked=[...box.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]:checked')].map(c=>{const it=e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===c.dataset.pid);const q=box.querySelector(`[data-q="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`).value;const cd=box.querySelector(`[data-cd="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`).value.trim().toUpperCase();const ivI=box.querySelector(`[data-ivp="${CSS.escape(it.pid)}"]`);return {...it,usado:q===''?0:Number(q),espec:cd||it.espec||'',ivEsc:ivI?ivVal(ivI.value):undefined}});
      const avul=extras.map(x=>({pid:isKit(x.s)?'':novoPid(),desc:x.s.desc,espec:stCod(x.s),usado:Number(x.q)||0,un:isKit(x.s)?'kit':x.s.un||'un',estoqueId:x.s.id,tipo:x.s.tipo||'',ivEsc:isKit(x.s)?undefined:ivVal(x.iv??((e.ivEnc||{})[x.s.id]??''))}));
      if(!picked.length&&!avul.length){toast('Marque pelo menos um item.');return}
      const h0=Number(hm.value),dt=sh.querySelector('#t-dt').value;
      const g=hmGuard(sh.querySelector('#t-eqv'),e,h0,dt,hmSt,()=>sh.querySelector('#ft').onsubmit({preventDefault(){},eqvOk:ev.eqvOk,rpOk:ev.rpOk,rp:ev.rp}));if(!g)return;
      const h=g.h,dl=g.dl;
      const pv=sh.querySelector('#t-prox').value.trim(), proxV=pv!==''?Math.round((Number(pv)+dl)*10)/10:iv?h+iv:'';
      if(proxV!==''&&proxV<=h){toast('A proxima precisa ser maior que o horimetro atual.');return}
      /* codigo diferente do usado na preventiva anterior: pergunta se sao equivalentes antes de registrar */
      const lastCod=pid=>{const pc=(e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===pid)||{}).espec;if(pc)return pc;const t=S.trocas.filter(t=>t.equipId===e.id&&t.tipo!=='filtro_ar'&&t.tipo!=='reprog'&&t.tipo!=='hmtroca'&&(t.itens||[]).some(i=>i.pid===pid&&i.espec)).sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||''))[0];return t?t.itens.find(i=>i.pid===pid).espec:(e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===pid)||{}).espec||''};
      const pend=picked.map(p=>({p,a:lastCod(p.pid),b:p.espec})).filter(x=>x.a&&x.b&&nzCod(x.a)!==nzCod(x.b)&&!eqvDec(x.a,x.b));
      if(pend.length&&!ev.eqvOk){const bx=sh.querySelector('#t-eqv'),dec={};
        bx.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask"><b>Confirme antes de registrar</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">O codigo usado e diferente do codigo do plano. Para efeito de preventiva, eles se equivalem? Se sim, o item fica cumprido; se nao, continua pendente.</p>${pend.map((x,i)=>`<div class="eqv" data-i="${i}"><div class="eqv-h"><b>${esc(itNome(x.p))}</b></div>
          <div class="eqv-c"><div><small>no plano</small><span class="num">${esc(x.a)}</span></div><span class="eqv-ar">→</span><div><small>agora</small><span class="num">${esc(x.b)}</span></div></div>
          <div class="eqv-b"><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-eq="eq" aria-pressed="false">Equivalentes</button><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-eq="dif" aria-pressed="false">Diferentes</button></div></div>`).join('')}
          <div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-x>Revisar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go disabled>Confirmar e registrar</button></div></div>`;
        bx.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'});
        const goB=bx.querySelector('[data-go]');
        bx.querySelectorAll('.eqv').forEach(r=>r.querySelectorAll('[data-eq]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{dec[r.dataset.i]=b.dataset.eq;r.querySelectorAll('[data-eq]').forEach(x=>{x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',x===b);x.classList.toggle('pri',x===b)});goB.disabled=Object.keys(dec).length<pend.length}));
        bx.querySelector('[data-x]').onclick=()=>{bx.innerHTML=''};
        goB.onclick=async()=>{const add=pend.map((x,i)=>({a:x.a,b:x.b,desc:itNome(x.p),equipId:e.id,status:dec[i],data:dt}));
          const keys=new Set(add.map(x=>eqvKey(x.a,x.b)));
          if(!await safe(()=>S.db.doc('config/equivalencias').set({pares:[...S.equiv.filter(x=>!keys.has(eqvKey(x.a,x.b))),...add]}),''))return;
          S.equiv=[...S.equiv.filter(x=>!keys.has(eqvKey(x.a,x.b))),...add];bx.innerHTML='';
          sh.querySelector('#ft').onsubmit({preventDefault(){},eqvOk:true})};
        return}
      /* vencidos que ficaram sem trocar: oferece reprogramar antes de registrar */
      const ids0=new Set(picked.map(p=>p.pid)),miss=e.itens.filter(i=>!ids0.has(i.pid)&&itemStatus(e,i).cls==='bad');
      if(miss.length&&!ev.rpOk){const bx=sh.querySelector('#t-eqv');
        bx.innerHTML=`<div class="eqv-ask"><b>Itens vencidos nao trocados</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">Se ficaram para depois, reprograme o vencimento. So muda neste equipamento.</p>${miss.map(i=>`<div class="eqv" data-rpi="${esc(i.pid)}"><div class="eqv-h"><b>${esc(itNome(i))}</b></div>
          <label class="f">Nova proxima (h)<input type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" data-rpv placeholder="Maior que ${nf(h)}"></label><div class="f">Motivo${rpMotHTML(i.pid)}</div></div>`).join('')}
          <div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-skip>Nao reprogramar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-go disabled>Reprogramar</button></div></div>`;
        bx.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'});bindRpMot(bx);
        const goB=bx.querySelector('[data-go]'),rows=[...bx.querySelectorAll('[data-rpi]')];
        const read=()=>rows.map(r=>({pid:r.dataset.rpi,para:Number(r.querySelector('[data-rpv]').value),motivo:rpMot(bx,r.dataset.rpi),de:itemStatus(e,e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===r.dataset.rpi)).prox,data:dt}));
        const chk=()=>{goB.disabled=!read().every(r=>r.para>h&&r.motivo)};
        bx.addEventListener('rpchange',chk);rows.forEach(r=>r.querySelector('[data-rpv]').oninput=chk);
        const go=rp=>{bx.innerHTML='';sh.querySelector('#ft').onsubmit({preventDefault(){},eqvOk:true,rpOk:true,rp})};
        bx.querySelector('[data-skip]').onclick=()=>go([]);goB.onclick=()=>go(read());
        return}
      const rp=ev.rp||[],rpM={};rp.forEach(r=>rpM[r.pid]=r);
      const ok=await safe(async()=>{
        if(rp.length)await S.db.collection('trocas').add(rpDoc({...e,horimetro:h},rp,sh.querySelector('#t-resp').value.trim(),dt));
        await S.db.collection('trocas').add({equipId:e.id,data:dt,horimetro:h0,...(dl?{hmConv:h}:{}),resp:sh.querySelector('#t-resp').value.trim(),obs:sh.querySelector('#t-obs').value.trim(),
          proximaH:proxV,intervalo:iv||'',ajuste:!!S.ajuste,itens:[...picked,...avul].map(p=>({pid:p.pid,desc:p.desc,espec:p.espec||'',qtd:p.usado,un:p.un||'',estoqueId:p.estoqueId||''})),exemplo:!!e.exemplo});
        const ids=new Set(picked.map(p=>p.pid));
        /* peca diferente da do plano (marcada como "Diferentes") nao cumpre o item: ele continua pendente */
        const cumpre=new Set(picked.filter(p=>{const pc=(e.itens.find(i=>i.pid===p.pid)||{}).espec;return !pc||!p.espec||nzCod(pc)===nzCod(p.espec)||eqvDec(pc,p.espec)!=='dif'}).map(p=>p.pid));
        const ivOf={};picked.forEach(p=>{if(p.ivEsc!==undefined)ivOf[p.pid]=p.ivEsc});
        const upd={itens:e.itens.map(i=>{if(ids.has(i.pid)&&cumpre.has(i.pid)){const v=ivOf[i.pid];return v===undefined?{...i,ultimaH:h,ultimaData:dt,proxH:proxV,reprog:''}:{...i,ultimaH:h,ultimaData:dt,proxH:ivProx(v,h,proxV),reprog:'',ivSel:v,ivDef:true}}return rpM[i.pid]?rpApply(i,rpM[i.pid]):i})};
        /* itens adicionados (fora do plano, exceto kits) passam a seguir o proprio intervalo no plano do equipamento */
        avul.filter(a=>a.pid).forEach(a=>{const v=a.ivEsc;const base=proxV;
          upd.itens.push({pid:a.pid,desc:a.desc,espec:a.espec,estoqueId:a.estoqueId,qtd:a.usado,un:a.un,tipo:a.tipo||'outro',intervalo:'',ultimaH:h,ultimaData:dt,proxH:ivProx(v,h,base),reprog:'',ivSel:v,ivDef:true})});
        if(e.novo0)upd.novo0=false;
        if(h>(Number(e.horimetro)||0)){upd.horimetro=h;upd.horimetroData=dt;upd.horimetroReg=today()}
        await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update(upd);
        const baixa={};if(!S.ajuste)[...picked,...avul].forEach(p=>{if(p.estoqueId)addBaixa(baixa,p.estoqueId,p.usado)});
        for(const[sid,q]of Object.entries(baixa)){const s=stById(sid);await logBaixa(sid,q,dt,e.id);await S.db.doc('estoque/'+sid).update({qtd:Math.round(((Number(s.qtd)||0)-q)*10)/10})}
      },'Preventiva registrada');
      if(ok)finishReg(e.id);
    };
  }});
}
/* + › Frota › Editar preventiva: escolhe o registro (preventivas e filtros de ar) */
function formPickTr(){
  nav('pktr',()=>formPickTr());
  const norm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]/g,'');
  const all=S.trocas.filter(t=>t.tipo!=='hmtroca'&&t.tipo!=='reprog'&&eqById(t.equipId)).sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||'')||(b.ts||0)-(a.ts||0));
  const row=x=>{const e=eqById(x.equipId);return `<button type="button" class="tq" data-pt="${esc(x.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${tTit(x)}${x.ajuste?AJS:''}</span><span class="tq-d">${fdate(x.data)}</span>${I.chev}</button>`};
  const draw=l=>l.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${l.slice(0,80).map(row).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty">Nada encontrado.</div>';
  openSheet({title:'Editar preventiva',sub:'Toque no registro para editar',
  body:`<div class="search">${I.search}<input id="pt-q" type="search" placeholder="TAG do equipamento" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Buscar"></div><div id="pt-l" style="margin-top:8px">${all.length?draw(all):'<div class="empty">Nenhuma preventiva registrada.</div>'}</div>`,
  mount:sh=>{const l=sh.querySelector('#pt-l'),q=sh.querySelector('#pt-q');
    const bind=()=>l.querySelectorAll('[data-pt]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formEditTroca(b.dataset.pt));
    q.oninput=()=>{const t=norm(q.value.trim());l.innerHTML=draw(all.filter(x=>!t||norm(eqById(x.equipId).codigo).includes(t)));bind()};bind()}});
}
/* pecas que sairam do estoque num registro (kits viram componentes; ajuste nao conta) */
function pecasUsadas(t,itens){const acc={};if(t.ajuste)return acc;(itens||t.itens||[]).forEach(i=>{const q=Number(i.qtd)||0;if(i.estoqueId&&q>0)addBaixa(acc,i.estoqueId,q)});
  Object.keys(acc).forEach(k=>{acc[k]=Math.round(acc[k]*10)/10;if(!stById(k))delete acc[k]});return acc}
/* editar preventiva: data, horimetro, responsavel, observacoes, codigo e quantidade de cada peca.
   Peca a mais sai do estoque; peca a menos pode voltar ao estoque (pergunta antes). */
function formEditTroca(id){
  nav('edtr:'+id,()=>formEditTroca(id));
  const t=S.trocas.find(x=>x.id===id),e=t&&eqById(t.equipId);if(!e){goBack();return}
  const ar=t.tipo==='filtro_ar',its=[...(t.itens||[])];
  openSheet({title:'Editar '+(ar?'filtro de ar':'preventiva'),sub:`${esc(e.codigo)} · ${fdate(t.data)}`,
  body:`${t.ajuste?`<p class="note" style="margin:0 2px 10px">Lancada em ajuste ${AJS} · alterar pecas nao mexe no estoque.</p>`:''}<form id="fet"><div class="list" id="et-fs"></div>
    <div class="lbl" style="margin-top:16px">Pecas usadas <span class="hint" style="text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-weight:400">toque para editar ou substituir</span></div><div class="list" id="et-l"></div>
    <div class="sug-w t-add" style="margin-top:10px"><div class="search">${I.search}<input id="et-add" type="search" placeholder="Adicionar item por codigo ou nome" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Adicionar item"></div><div class="sug" id="et-addh"></div></div>${rdBtn('et-addcam','Adicionar item pela camera')}
    <div id="et-conf"></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="fet">Salvar alteracoes</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{const f=sh.querySelector('#fet'),cf=sh.querySelector('#et-conf');
    f.addEventListener('input',()=>cf.innerHTML='');
    /* pecas: linhas fechadas; um toque abre Editar (quantidade) ou Substituir (outra peca) */
    const cur=its.map(i=>({espec:i.espec||(i.estoqueId&&stById(i.estoqueId)?stCod(stById(i.estoqueId)):''),qtd:i.qtd??0,sid:i.estoqueId||''}));
    let aberto=-1,modo='';const L=sh.querySelector('#et-l');

    const draw=()=>{if(!L)return;L.innerHTML=its.map((i,k)=>{const c=cur[k],x=c.sid&&stById(c.sid),op=aberto===k,ch=codTx(k)!==cur0[k],qch=Number(c.qtd)!==Number(i.qtd||0);
      return `<div class="et-i${op?' op':''}"><button type="button" class="et-h" data-k="${k}" aria-expanded="${op}"><span class="et-n"><b>${esc(itNome(i))}</b><small class="num">${esc(codTx(k))}${x?'':' · fora do estoque'}${ch?` <em>substituiu ${esc(cur0[k])}</em>`:''}${i.novo?' <em>adicionado</em>':''}</small></span><span class="et-q num${qch?' mud':''}">${nf(c.qtd,1)} ${esc(i.un||'')}</span>${I.chev}</button>
      ${!op?'':modo==='ed'?`<div class="et-b"><label class="f">Quantidade usada<div class="et-qw"><input type="number" step="0.1" min="0" inputmode="decimal" id="et-qv" value="${esc(c.qtd)}"><span class="u">${esc(i.un||'')}</span><button type="button" class="btn pri" id="et-ok">Pronto</button></div></label><p class="note" style="margin:0">0 = nao usou do estoque.</p></div>`
       :modo==='sb'?`<div class="et-b"><div class="sug-w"><label class="f">Peca aplicada no lugar<input id="et-sq" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="Codigo, referencia ou descricao" style="font-family:var(--mono)"></label><div class="sug" id="et-sh"></div></div>${CAM_OK?rdBtn('et-cam'):''}<button type="button" class="btn" id="et-x">Cancelar</button></div>`
       :modo==='kv'?`<div class="et-b">${kitLista(x,c.qtd||1)}<button type="button" class="btn" data-md="">Fechar lista</button></div>`
       :`<div class="et-b et-a"><button type="button" class="btn" data-md="ed">Editar</button>${i.novo?'<button type="button" class="btn" data-md="rm">Tirar</button>':'<button type="button" class="btn" data-md="sb">Substituir</button>'}${x&&isKit(x)?'<button type="button" class="btn" data-md="kv">Ver itens</button>':''}</div>`}</div>`}).join('');
      L.querySelectorAll('.et-h').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const k=Number(b.dataset.k);aberto=aberto===k?-1:k;modo='';draw()});
      L.querySelectorAll('[data-md]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(b.dataset.md==='rm'){const k2=aberto;its.splice(k2,1);cur.splice(k2,1);cur0.splice(k2,1);aberto=-1;modo='';draw();cf.innerHTML='';return}modo=b.dataset.md;draw();const fi=L.querySelector('#et-qv,#et-sq');fi&&setTimeout(()=>fi.focus(),40)});
      const k=aberto;if(k<0)return;
      const qv=L.querySelector('#et-qv');if(qv){const ok=()=>{const v=qv.value===''?0:Math.round(Number(qv.value)*10)/10;if(!(v>=0)){toast('Quantidade invalida.');return}cur[k].qtd=v;aberto=-1;draw();cf.innerHTML=''};
        L.querySelector('#et-ok').onclick=ok;qv.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();ok()}}}
      const sq=L.querySelector('#et-sq');if(sq){const sh2=L.querySelector('#et-sh');
        const ivE=L.querySelector('#et-iv');if(ivE)ivE.onchange=()=>{cur[k].iv=ivVal(ivE.value)};
        const pick=(x,cod)=>{cur[k].sid=x?x.id:'';cur[k].espec=x?stCod(x):cod;if(ivE)cur[k].iv=ivVal(ivE.value);aberto=-1;draw();cf.innerHTML=''};
        sq.oninput=()=>{const k2=nzCod(sq.value);if(!k2){sh2.innerHTML='';return}
          const score=x=>{const a=nzCod(stCod(x)),b=nzCod(x.ref);return a===k2||b===k2?0:a.startsWith(k2)||b.startsWith(k2)?1:a.includes(k2)||b.includes(k2)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k2)?2:9};
          const m=S.estoque.filter(x=>!isKit(x)).map(x=>[score(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,5).map(a=>a[1]),v=sq.value.trim().toUpperCase();
          sh2.innerHTML=`${m.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-pk="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:''}<button type="button" class="et-fora" data-fx>Usar ${esc(v)} <span>fora do estoque, sem baixa</span></button>`;
          sh2.querySelectorAll('[data-pk]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>pick(stById(b.dataset.pk)));sh2.querySelector('[data-fx]').onclick=()=>pick(null,v)};
        L.querySelector('#et-x').onclick=()=>{modo='';draw()};
        const cm=L.querySelector('#et-cam');if(cm)cm.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item&&!isKit(v.item))pick(v.item);else{sq.value=v.text||'';sq.oninput()}}})}};
    const cur0=cur.map((c,k)=>codTx(k));draw();
    /* incluir itens que ficaram de fora; ao salvar, o app pergunta se da baixa no estoque deles */
    const addIt=x=>{if(!x)return;const j=cur.findIndex(c=>c.sid===x.id);if(j>=0){aberto=j;modo='';draw();toast(`${x.desc} ja esta na lista`);return}
      its.push({pid:'',desc:x.desc,espec:stCod(x),qtd:1,un:isKit(x)?'kit':x.un||'un',estoqueId:x.id,tipo:x.tipo||'',novo:true});cur.push({espec:stCod(x),qtd:1,sid:x.id});cur0.push(stCod(x));
      aberto=-1;modo='';draw();cf.innerHTML='';toast(`${x.desc} adicionado`)};
    const aq=sh.querySelector('#et-add'),aqh=sh.querySelector('#et-addh');
    aq.oninput=()=>{const k2=nzCod(aq.value);if(!k2){aqh.innerHTML='';return}
      const sc=x=>{const a=nzCod(stCod(x)),b=nzCod(x.ref);return a===k2||b===k2?0:a.startsWith(k2)||b.startsWith(k2)?1:a.includes(k2)||b.includes(k2)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k2)?2:9};
      const m=S.estoque.map(x=>[sc(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,6).map(a=>a[1]);
      aqh.innerHTML=m.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-ea="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum item encontrado no estoque.</div>';
      aqh.querySelectorAll('[data-ea]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{aq.value='';aqh.innerHTML='';addIt(stById(b.dataset.ea))})};
    const acm=sh.querySelector('#et-addcam');if(acm)acm.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(!v.item){toast('Item nao encontrado no estoque.');return}addIt(v.item)}});
    /* campos do registro: cada um abre uma janela para digitar e confirmar */
    const V={data:t.data||'',hm:t.horimetro??'',resp:t.resp||'',obs:t.obs||''};
    const FD=[['data','Data','date',v=>v?fdate(v):'—'],['hm','Horimetro','number',v=>v===''?'—':nf(v)+' h'],['resp','Responsavel','text',v=>v||'—'],['obs','Observacoes','textarea',v=>v||'—']];
    const FS=sh.querySelector('#et-fs');
    let fAb='';
    const drawF=()=>{FS.innerHTML=FD.map(([k,l,tp,fmt])=>{const op=fAb===k,orig=k==='data'?t.data||'':k==='hm'?t.horimetro??'':t[k]||'';
        const inp=tp==='textarea'?`<textarea id="ef-v" rows="3">${esc(V[k])}</textarea>`:`<input id="ef-v" type="${tp}"${tp==='number'?' step="0.1" inputmode="decimal"':''} value="${esc(V[k])}"${k==='resp'?' placeholder="Mecanico ou oficina"':''} autocomplete="off">`;
        return `<div class="et-i${op?' op':''}"><button type="button" class="et-h" data-fd="${k}" aria-expanded="${op}"><span class="et-l">${l}</span><span class="et-v${String(V[k])!==String(orig)?' mud':''}">${esc(fmt(V[k]))}</span>${I.chev}</button>
        ${op?`<div class="et-b"><div class="et-qw">${inp}${k==='hm'?'<span class="u">h</span>':''}</div><div class="et-a" style="display:flex;gap:8px"><button type="button" class="btn" id="ef-x">Cancelar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" id="ef-ok">Confirmar</button></div></div>`:''}</div>`}).join('');
      FS.querySelectorAll('[data-fd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{fAb=fAb===b.dataset.fd?'':b.dataset.fd;drawF();const fi=FS.querySelector('#ef-v');fi&&setTimeout(()=>fi.focus(),40)});
      const fi=FS.querySelector('#ef-v');if(!fi)return;const k=fAb,tp=FD.find(x=>x[0]===k)[2];
      const ok=()=>{const v=fi.value.trim();if((k==='data'||k==='hm')&&v===''){fi.focus();return}if(tp==='number'&&(isNaN(Number(v))||Number(v)<0)){toast('Valor invalido.');return}
        V[k]=k==='hm'?Number(v):v;fAb='';cf.innerHTML='';drawF()};
      FS.querySelector('#ef-ok').onclick=ok;FS.querySelector('#ef-x').onclick=()=>{fAb='';drawF()};
      if(tp!=='textarea')fi.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();ok()}}};
    drawF();
    f.onsubmit=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      const dt=V.data,h0=Number(V.hm);
      if(!dt||isNaN(h0)||h0<0){toast('Confira a data e o horimetro.');return}
      const novos=its.map((i,k)=>{const {novo,...r}=i;return {...r,qtd:cur[k].qtd,espec:cur[k].espec,estoqueId:cur[k].sid}});
      const A=pecasUsadas(t),B=pecasUsadas(t,novos),mais={},menos={};
      new Set([...Object.keys(A),...Object.keys(B)]).forEach(k=>{const d=Math.round(((B[k]||0)-(A[k]||0))*10)/10;if(d>0)mais[k]=d;else if(d<0)menos[k]=-d});
      const go=async(dev,bx)=>{
        const ok=await safe(async()=>{
          /* horimetro convertido (troca de relogio) segue a mesma diferenca */
          const effOld=t.hmConv??t.horimetro,effNew=Math.round((h0+(Number(effOld)-Number(t.horimetro)||0))*10)/10,dh=Math.round((effNew-Number(effOld))*10)/10;
          await S.db.doc('trocas/'+id).update({data:dt,horimetro:h0,...(t.hmConv!=null?{hmConv:effNew}:{}),resp:V.resp.trim(),obs:V.obs.trim(),itens:novos,editado:Date.now()});
          /* o plano so muda nos itens que ainda apontam para este registro */
          const pids=new Set(novos.map(i=>i.pid).filter(Boolean));
          if((dh||dt!==t.data)&&pids.size){let mud=false;const its2=(e.itens||[]).map(i=>{if(!pids.has(i.pid)||Number(i.ultimaH)!==Number(effOld))return i;mud=true;
