              return {...i,ultimaH:effNew,ultimaData:dt,proxH:i.proxH===''||i.proxH==null||isNaN(Number(i.proxH))?i.proxH:Math.round((Number(i.proxH)+dh)*10)/10}});
            if(mud)await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({itens:its2})}
          /* peca substituida com intervalo escolhido: vale para o item do plano se este registro e a ultima troca dele */
          const ivK=its.map((i,k)=>[i.pid,cur[k].iv]).filter(([pid,v])=>pid&&v!==undefined);
          if(ivK.length){const eNow=eqById(e.id)||e;let mud2=false;const its3=(eNow.itens||[]).map(i=>{const m=ivK.find(x=>x[0]===i.pid);if(!m||Number(i.ultimaH)!==Number(effNew))return i;mud2=true;return {...i,ivSel:m[1],ivDef:true,proxH:ivProx(m[1],effNew,i.proxH)}});
            if(mud2)await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update({itens:its3})}
          if(bx)for(const[sid,q]of Object.entries(mais)){const x=stById(sid);await logBaixa(sid,q,today(),e.id);await S.db.doc('estoque/'+sid).update({qtd:Math.round(((Number(x.qtd)||0)-q)*10)/10})}
          if(dev)for(const[sid,q]of Object.entries(menos)){const x=stById(sid);const saldo=Math.round(((Number(x.qtd)||0)+q)*10)/10;
            await S.db.doc('estoque/'+sid).update({qtd:saldo});try{await S.db.collection('movs').add({estoqueId:sid,tipo:'entrada',qtd:q,data:today(),obs:'Preventiva editada',equipId:e.id,origem:'estorno',saldo,ts:Date.now()})}catch(er){console.error(er)}}
        },'Preventiva salva');
        if(ok)goBack()};
      const lst=o=>specList('',Object.entries(o).map(([sid,q])=>{const x=stById(sid);return[x.desc,stCod(x),nf(q,1)+' '+stUn(x)]}));
      const ask=(tit,txt,o,no,yes,fn)=>{cf.innerHTML=`<div class="dv-ask"><b>${tit}</b><p class="note" style="margin:0">${txt}</p><div class="spec dv-l">${lst(o)}</div>
          <div class="eqv-f"><button type="button" class="btn" data-n>${no}</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" data-y>${yes}</button></div></div>`;
        cf.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'});cf.querySelector('[data-n]').onclick=()=>fn(false);cf.querySelector('[data-y]').onclick=()=>fn(true)};
      /* 1º pergunta se devolve o que saiu do registro; 2º se da baixa no que entrou no lugar */
      const passo2=dev=>Object.keys(mais).length?ask('Dar baixa no estoque?','Pecas aplicadas no lugar:',mais,'Nao dar baixa','Dar baixa',bx=>go(dev,bx)):go(dev,false);
      if(Object.keys(menos).length)ask('Devolver ao estoque?','Pecas retiradas do registro:',menos,'Nao devolver','Devolver',passo2);
      else passo2(false)}}});
}
/* equipamento: digita TAG, tipo, modelo ou placa e escolhe nas sugestoes (a lista fica escondida por tras) */
function eqSearch(sel){if(sel.dataset.eqsOn)return;sel.dataset.eqsOn=1;
  const obrig=sel.required;sel.required=false;sel.hidden=true;
  const norm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase(),nz=v=>norm(v).replace(/[\s.\-\/]/g,'');
  const ids=[...sel.options].map(o=>o.value).filter(Boolean),all=ids.map(eqById).filter(Boolean);
  const lab=e=>e?`${e.codigo} · ${e.tipo||''}`:'';
  const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='sug-w';
  w.innerHTML=`<input type="search" class="eqs-in" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="Digite a TAG, tipo, modelo ou placa" aria-label="Equipamento"><div class="sug"></div>`;
  sel.after(w);const inp=w.querySelector('input'),box=w.querySelector('.sug');
  const valid=()=>inp.setCustomValidity(sel.value||(!obrig&&!inp.value.trim())?'':'Escolha um equipamento nas sugestoes');
  const pick=e=>{sel.value=e?e.id:'';inp.value=lab(e);box.innerHTML='';valid();sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))};
  inp.value=lab(eqById(sel.value));valid();
  const busca=()=>{const t=norm(inp.value.trim()),tz=nz(inp.value);
    if(sel.value&&inp.value!==lab(eqById(sel.value))){sel.value='';sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))}
    valid();if(!t){box.innerHTML='';return}
    const sc=e=>{const c=nz(e.codigo);return c===tz?0:c.startsWith(tz)?1:c.includes(tz)?2:[e.tipo,e.marca,e.modelo,e.placa,e.descricao].some(x=>norm(x).includes(t))||nz(e.placa).includes(tz)?3:9};
    const m=all.map(e=>[sc(e),e]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,8).map(a=>a[1]);
    box.innerHTML=m.length?m.map(e=>`<button type="button" class="tq eqs-o" data-eo="${esc(e.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc([e.tipo,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></button>`).join(''):'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum equipamento encontrado.</div>';
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-eo]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();pick(eqById(b.dataset.eo))})};
  inp.oninput=busca;inp.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();const f=box.querySelector('[data-eo]');if(f)f.click()}};
  inp.onblur=()=>setTimeout(()=>{if(!w.contains(document.activeElement))box.innerHTML=''},200);
  inp.onfocus=()=>{if(inp.value&&!sel.value)busca()}}
/* Preventivas › busca: equipamento ainda sem preventiva registrada.
   Mostra so o basico e leva ao registro, avisando da baixa no estoque. */
/* botao "Equipamento novo": aparece sem preventiva registrada e com horimetro abaixo do intervalo da ficha */
function novoBox(e){const iv=Number(e.intervaloPrev)||0,h=Number(e.horimetro)||0;if(!iv||!semPrev(e))return '';
  if(e.novo0)return `<div class="nv-b on"><div><b>Equipamento novo · conta do zero</b><span>${h>=iv?`Primeira preventiva vencida ha ${nf(h-iv)} h.`:`Primeira preventiva em ${nf(iv)} h (faltam ${nf(iv-h)} h).`}</span></div><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-nv="0">Desfazer</button></div>`;
  if(h>=iv)return '';
  return `<div class="nv-b"><div><b>Veio zerado de fabrica?</b><span>Marque como novo: a primeira preventiva vence em ${nf(iv)} h (faltam ${nf(iv-h)} h).</span></div><button type="button" class="btn sm pri" data-nv="1">Equipamento novo</button></div>`}
function novoBind(sh,e){sh.querySelectorAll('[data-nv]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{const on=b.dataset.nv==='1';const e2=eqById(e.id);if(!e2)return;
  const upd={novo0:on};if(on)upd.itens=(e2.itens||[]).map(i=>i.reprog?i:{...i,ultimaH:'',ultimaData:'',proxH:''});
  if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).update(upd),on?'Marcado como novo':'Desfeito'))refreshSheet()})}
function openSemPrev(id){
  nav('sp:'+id,()=>openSemPrev(id));
  const e=eqById(id);if(!e){goBack();return}
  const hm=e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null;
  openSheet({title:`<span class="code" style="font-size:16px">${esc(e.codigo)}</span>${esc(e.tipo||'')}`,sub:'Sem preventiva registrada',
  body:`<div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Marca / modelo</span><b>${esc([e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'—')}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Alocado em</span><b>${esc(alocTxt(e)||'—')}</b></div>
    <div class="r"><span>Ultima leitura</span><b class="num">${hm?`${nf(e.horimetro)} h${e.horimetroData?` <small class="sp-d">${fdate(e.horimetroData)}</small>`:''}`:'—'}</b></div></div>
    <div class="empty" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Nenhuma preventiva registrada</b>Este equipamento ainda nao tem preventivas lancadas.</div>${novoBox(e)}
    <div class="sp-aj${S.ajuste?' on':''}"><div><b>${S.ajuste?'Ajuste ligado':'O lancamento da baixa no estoque'}</b><span>${S.ajuste?'Os itens usados nao serao baixados do estoque.':'Os itens usados saem do estoque. Se nao for a intencao, ligue o Ajuste.'}</span></div>
    <button type="button" class="sp-sw" role="switch" aria-checked="${!!S.ajuste}" aria-label="Ajuste"></button></div>`,
  foot:`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="sp-go">${I.manut}<span>Registrar preventiva</span></button>`,
  mount:sh=>{novoBind(sh,e);sh.querySelector('.sp-sw').onclick=()=>{setAjuste(!S.ajuste);refreshSheet()};sh.querySelector('#sp-go').onclick=()=>formRegistrar(id)}});
}

/* ===================== Avisos entre a equipe =====================
   Quem envia escolhe as pessoas, escreve e anexa itens, equipamentos ou fotos.
   Quem recebe ve o sino com o numero de novos, abre, responde e marca como resolvido. */
const EU=()=>window.__frotaEu||null;
const podeEscrever=()=>{const u=EU();return !!u&&u.papel!=='consulta'};
const pessoa=uid=>(S.equipe||[]).find(p=>p.uid===uid)||(EU()&&EU().uid===uid?EU():{uid,nome:'Alguem'});
const nomeP=p=>(p&&(p.nome||(p.email||'').split('@')[0]))||'Alguem';
const iniP=p=>{const n=nomeP(p).trim().split(/\s+/);return ((n[0]||'?')[0]+(n.length>1?n[n.length-1][0]:(n[0]||'')[1]||'')).toUpperCase()};
const avHora=ts=>{const d=new Date(ts||0),h=new Date();const dd=iso(d),hj=today(),on=iso(new Date(h.getTime()-864e5));return dd===hj?d.toTimeString().slice(0,5):dd===on?'ontem':sdate(dd)};
const avNovo=a=>{const u=EU();return u&&(a.para||[]).includes(u.uid)&&!(a.estado||{})[u.uid]};
function avSit(a){const u=EU(),e=a.estado||{};if(u&&(a.para||[]).includes(u.uid)){const x=e[u.uid];return x==='resolvido'?['r','Resolvido']:x==='visto'?['v','Visto']:['n','Novo']}
  const v=(a.para||[]).map(p=>e[p]);return v.length&&v.every(x=>x==='resolvido')?['r','Resolvido']:v.some(Boolean)?['v','Visto']:['n','Novo']}
function pintaSino(){const b=$('#bell');if(!b)return;const n=(S.avisos||[]).filter(avNovo).length,i=b.querySelector('i');i.hidden=!n;i.textContent=n>9?'9+':n;b.setAttribute('aria-label',n?`Avisos: ${n} novo${n>1?'s':''}`:'Avisos');fitTop()}
/* "Sincronizado" vira so a bolinha quando nao cabe no topo */
function fitTop(){const t=document.querySelector('.top');if(!t)return;setTimeout(()=>{try{setHdr()}catch(e){}},0);t.classList.remove('cmp','cmp2');const o=()=>t.scrollWidth>t.clientWidth+1;if(o()){t.classList.add('cmp');if(o())t.classList.add('cmp2')}}
addEventListener('resize',fitTop);try{new MutationObserver(fitTop).observe($('#sync'),{childList:true,characterData:true,subtree:true})}catch(e){}
$('#bell').addEventListener('click',ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();ev.preventDefault();NAV.length=0;openAvisos()});
function avisarFoot(t,id){return podeEscrever()?`<button type="button" class="btn av-btn" data-avisar="${t}:${esc(id)}">Avisar sobre este ${t==='eq'?'equipamento':'item'}</button>`:''}
document.addEventListener('click',ev=>{const b=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('[data-avisar]');if(!b)return;const [t,id]=b.dataset.avisar.split(/:(.*)/s);formAviso({anexos:[{t,id}]})});
/* cartoes dos anexos */
function avCard(x,rm){let h='';
  if(x.t==='item'){const s=stById(x.id);if(!s)return `<div class="icard"><div class="tx"><b>Item removido do estoque</b></div>${rm}</div>`;
    const q=stQtd(s),bx=q<=(Number(s.minimo)||0),enc=(s.encaixe||[]).map(eqById).filter(Boolean).map(e=>e.codigo).slice(0,3).join(', ');
    h=`<button type="button" class="icard" data-avo="item:${esc(s.id)}"><span class="ph">${(s.fotos||[])[0]?imgTag(s.fotos[0]):''}</span><span class="tx"><b>${esc(s.desc)}</b><small class="num">${esc(stCod(s))}${s.marca?' · '+esc(s.marca):''}</small><span class="sd"><span>Saldo <b class="${bx?'bad':''}">${nf(q,1)} ${esc(stUn(s))}</b></span><span>Minimo ${nf(s.minimo,1)}</span>${enc?`<span>${esc(enc)}</span>`:''}</span></span></button>`}
  else if(x.t==='eq'){const e=eqById(x.id);if(!e)return '';
    h=`<button type="button" class="icard ecard" data-avo="eq:${esc(e.id)}"><span class="ph">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tx"><b>${esc(e.tipo||'')}</b><small>${esc([e.marca,e.modelo,e.serie].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><span class="sd"><span>${e.horimetro!==''&&e.horimetro!=null?`Ultima leitura ${nf(e.horimetro)} h${e.horimetroData?' · '+sdate(e.horimetroData):''}`:'Sem leitura'}</span></span></span></button>`}
  else if(x.t==='foto')h=`<div class="icard fcard">${imgTag(x.id)}</div>`;
  return rm?h.replace(/<\/(button|div)>$/,`${rm}</$1>`):h}
function bindAvCards(sh){sh.querySelectorAll('[data-avo]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=ev=>{if(ev.target.closest('[data-arm]'))return;const [t,id]=b.dataset.avo.split(/:(.*)/s);t==='item'?formMov(id):openEq(id)})}
/* lista: recebidos e enviados */
function openAvisos(tab){
  nav('avs:'+(tab||'r'),()=>openAvisos(tab));tab=tab||'r';const u=EU();if(!u){goBack();return}
  const all=(S.avisos||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(b.ts||0)-(a.ts||0));
  const rec=all.filter(a=>(a.para||[]).includes(u.uid)),env=all.filter(a=>a.de===u.uid),l=tab==='r'?rec:env,nn=rec.filter(avNovo).length;
  const thumb=a=>{const x=(a.anexos||[])[0];if(!x)return '';if(x.t==='item'){const s=stById(x.id);return s&&(s.fotos||[])[0]?`<span class="av-t">${imgTag(s.fotos[0])}</span>`:''}if(x.t==='foto')return `<span class="av-t">${imgTag(x.id)}</span>`;if(x.t==='eq'){const e=eqById(x.id);return e?`<span class="av-t av-tq num">${esc(e.codigo)}</span>`:''}return ''};
  const prev=a=>a.texto||((a.anexos||[]).length?`${a.anexos.length} anexo${a.anexos.length>1?'s':''}`:'');
  const row=a=>{const nv=tab==='r'&&avNovo(a),st=avSit(a),p=tab==='r'?pessoa(a.de):pessoa((a.para||[])[0]),mais=tab==='e'&&(a.para||[]).length>1?` +${a.para.length-1}`:'';
    return `<button type="button" class="av-r${nv?' nv':''}" data-av="${esc(a.id)}">${nv?'<span class="av-dot"></span>':''}<span class="av-a">${esc(iniP(p))}</span><span class="av-m"><span class="l1"><b>${tab==='e'?'Para ':''}${esc(nomeP(p))}${mais}</b><em>${avHora(a.ts)}</em></span><span class="av-p">${esc(prev(a))}</span><span><span class="pl ${st[0]}">${st[1]}</span>${(a.resp||[]).length?`<span class="av-nr">${a.resp.length} resposta${a.resp.length>1?'s':''}</span>`:''}</span></span>${thumb(a)}</button>`};
  openSheet({title:'Avisos',sub:nn?`${nn} novo${nn>1?'s':''}`:'Recebidos e enviados',
  body:`<div class="seg av-tabs" role="tablist"><button type="button" data-avt="r" aria-pressed="${tab==='r'}">Recebidos${nn?` <b class="av-c">${nn}</b>`:''}</button><button type="button" data-avt="e" aria-pressed="${tab==='e'}">Enviados</button></div>
    ${l.length?`<div class="list">${l.map(row).join('')}</div>`:`<div class="empty">${tab==='r'?'Nenhum aviso recebido.':'Nenhum aviso enviado.'}</div>`}`,
  foot:podeEscrever()?`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="av-new" style="flex:1">Escrever aviso</button>`:'',
  mount:sh=>{sh.querySelectorAll('[data-avt]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(b.dataset.avt!==tab){NAV.pop();openAvisos(b.dataset.avt)}});
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-av]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openAviso(b.dataset.av));const n=sh.querySelector('#av-new');if(n)n.onclick=()=>formAviso({})}});
}
/* aviso aberto */
function openAviso(id){
  nav('av:'+id,()=>openAviso(id));const u=EU(),a=(S.avisos||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);if(!a||!u){goBack();return}
  const souPara=(a.para||[]).includes(u.uid),eu=(a.estado||{})[u.uid],st=avSit(a),de=pessoa(a.de);
  if(souPara&&!eu&&podeEscrever())S.db.doc('avisos/'+id).update({estado:{...(a.estado||{}),[u.uid]:'visto'}}).catch(e=>console.error(e));
  const paraTx=(a.para||[]).map(x=>nomeP(pessoa(x))).join(', ');
  const situ=a.de===u.uid&&(a.para||[]).length?`<div class="av-st">${(a.para||[]).map(x=>{const v=(a.estado||{})[x];return `<span><span class="av-a sm">${esc(iniP(pessoa(x)))}</span>${esc(nomeP(pessoa(x)))} <span class="pl ${v==='resolvido'?'r':v==='visto'?'v':'n'}">${v==='resolvido'?'Resolvido':v==='visto'?'Visto':'Novo'}</span></span>`}).join('')}</div>`:'';
  openSheet({title:'Aviso',sub:`De ${esc(nomeP(de))} · ${avHora(a.ts)}`,
  body:`<div class="av-h"><span class="av-a">${esc(iniP(de))}</span><div><b>${esc(nomeP(de))}</b><small>Para ${esc(paraTx)}</small></div><span class="pl ${st[0]}" style="margin-left:auto">${st[1]}</span></div>${situ}
    ${a.texto?`<div class="bub">${esc(a.texto).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div>`:''}${(a.anexos||[]).map(x=>avCard(x)).join('')}
    ${(a.resp||[]).length?`<div class="thr">${a.resp.map(r=>`<div class="bub${r.de===u.uid?' me':''}">${esc(r.texto).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div><small class="${r.de===u.uid?'':'l'}">${esc(nomeP(pessoa(r.de)))} · ${avHora(r.ts)}</small>`).join('')}</div>`:''}
    <div id="av-rp" hidden><div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><textarea id="av-rt" placeholder="Escreva a resposta" style="min-height:80px"></textarea></div><div class="eqv-f" style="margin-top:8px"><button type="button" class="btn" id="av-rx">Cancelar</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" id="av-rs">Enviar resposta</button></div></div>`,
  foot:podeEscrever()?`<button type="button" class="btn" id="av-r">Responder</button>${souPara?(eu==='resolvido'?`<button type="button" class="btn" id="av-re" style="flex:1">Reabrir</button>`:`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="av-ok" style="flex:1">Marcar como resolvido</button>`):''}`:'',
  mount:sh=>{bindAvCards(sh);const rp=sh.querySelector('#av-rp'),rt=sh.querySelector('#av-rt');
    const r=sh.querySelector('#av-r');if(r)r.onclick=()=>{rp.hidden=false;rt.focus();rp.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'})};
    const rx=sh.querySelector('#av-rx');if(rx)rx.onclick=()=>{rp.hidden=true;rt.value=''};
    const rs=sh.querySelector('#av-rs');if(rs)rs.onclick=async()=>{const t=rt.value.trim();if(!t){rt.focus();return}const cur=(S.avisos||[]).find(x=>x.id===id)||a;
      if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('avisos/'+id).update({resp:[...(cur.resp||[]),{de:u.uid,texto:t,ts:Date.now()}]}),'Resposta enviada'))refreshSheet()};
    const ok=sh.querySelector('#av-ok');if(ok)ok.onclick=async()=>{const cur=(S.avisos||[]).find(x=>x.id===id)||a;if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('avisos/'+id).update({estado:{...(cur.estado||{}),[u.uid]:'resolvido'}}),'Aviso resolvido'))goBack()};
    const re=sh.querySelector('#av-re');if(re)re.onclick=async()=>{const cur=(S.avisos||[]).find(x=>x.id===id)||a;if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('avisos/'+id).update({estado:{...(cur.estado||{}),[u.uid]:'visto'}}),'Aviso reaberto'))refreshSheet()}}});
}
/* escrever: para quem, mensagem e anexos (item, equipamento ou foto) */
function formAviso(pre){
  if(!podeEscrever()){toast('So quem pode editar o app envia avisos.');return}
  const u=EU(),key='avn:'+Date.now();
  const D={para:new Set(pre.para||[]),texto:pre.texto??'',anexos:(pre.anexos||[]).slice(),busca:''};
  if(pre.texto==null&&D.anexos.length===1){const x=D.anexos[0];
    if(x.t==='item'){const s=stById(x.id);if(s){const q=stQtd(s);D.texto=q<=(Number(s.minimo)||0)?`Comprar ${stCod(s)} · ${s.desc}. Saldo ${nf(q,1)} ${stUn(s)}, minimo ${nf(s.minimo,1)}.`:`${s.desc} · ${stCod(s)}.`}}
    else if(x.t==='eq'){const e=eqById(x.id);if(e)D.texto=`${e.codigo} · ${e.tipo||''}: `}}
  const show=()=>{nav(key,show);
  const pes=(S.equipe||[]).filter(p=>p.uid!==u.uid).sort((a,b)=>nomeP(a).localeCompare(nomeP(b)));
  const IC={it:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M21 8l-9-5-9 5 9 5 9-5z"/><path d="M3 8v8l9 5 9-5V8"/><path d="M12 13v8"/></svg>',eq:I.equip,ft:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 8h3l2-3h6l2 3h3v11H4z"/><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="3.5"/></svg>'};
  openSheet({title:'Novo aviso',sub:'Mensagem para a equipe',
  body:`<div class="lbl" style="margin-top:4px">Para</div>${pes.length?`<div class="chips">${pes.map(p=>`<button type="button" class="chip${D.para.has(p.uid)?' on':''}" data-pp="${esc(p.uid)}"><span class="av-a">${esc(iniP(p))}</span>${esc(nomeP(p))}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="note" style="margin:0 2px">A lista da equipe aparece depois que o administrador abrir o app com internet.</p>'}
    <div class="lbl">Mensagem</div><div class="fset"><textarea id="avn-t" placeholder="Escreva o aviso" style="min-height:100px">${esc(D.texto)}</textarea></div>
    <div id="avn-ax"></div><div class="lbl" id="avn-al">${D.anexos.length?'Anexar mais':'Anexar (opcional)'}</div>
    <div class="anx"><button type="button" data-ax="item">${IC.it}Item</button><button type="button" data-ax="eq">${IC.eq}Equipamento</button><button type="button" data-ax="foto">${IC.ft}Foto</button></div><div id="avn-s"></div>`,
  foot:`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="avn-go" style="flex:1">Enviar aviso</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{const go=sh.querySelector('#avn-go'),ta=sh.querySelector('#avn-t'),ax=sh.querySelector('#avn-ax'),sb=sh.querySelector('#avn-s');
    const upd=()=>{go.disabled=!D.para.size||!(ta.value.trim()||D.anexos.length)};
    const drawAx=()=>{ax.innerHTML=D.anexos.length?`<div class="lbl">Anexados · ${D.anexos.length}</div>${D.anexos.map((x,i)=>avCard(x,`<span class="x" data-arm="${i}" role="button" aria-label="Tirar anexo">✕</span>`)).join('')}`:'';
      sh.querySelector('#avn-al').textContent=D.anexos.length?'Anexar mais':'Anexar (opcional)';
      ax.querySelectorAll('[data-arm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();ev.preventDefault();D.anexos.splice(Number(b.dataset.arm),1);drawAx();upd()});
      ax.querySelectorAll('[data-avo]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=ev=>{ev.preventDefault()});upd()};
    const add=x=>{if(!D.anexos.some(y=>y.t===x.t&&y.id===x.id))D.anexos.push(x);sb.innerHTML='';drawAx()};
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-pp]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const k=b.dataset.pp;D.para.has(k)?D.para.delete(k):D.para.add(k);b.classList.toggle('on',D.para.has(k));upd()});
    ta.oninput=()=>{D.texto=ta.value;upd()};
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-ax]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{const t=b.dataset.ax;
      if(t==='foto'){const id=await uploadPhoto();if(id)add({t:'foto',id});return}
      sb.innerHTML=`<div class="sug-w t-add" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="search">${I.search}<input id="avn-q" type="search" placeholder="${t==='item'?'Item: codigo, referencia ou nome':'Equipamento: TAG, tipo ou modelo'}" autocomplete="off"></div><div class="sug" id="avn-h"></div></div>${t==='item'?rdBtn('avn-cam','Anexar item pela camera'):''}`;
      const q=sb.querySelector('#avn-q'),h=sb.querySelector('#avn-h');q.focus();
      q.oninput=()=>{const k=nzCod(q.value);if(!k){h.innerHTML='';return}
        if(t==='item'){const sc=x=>{const a=nzCod(stCod(x)),r=nzCod(x.ref);return a===k||r===k?0:a.startsWith(k)||r.startsWith(k)?1:a.includes(k)||r.includes(k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k)?2:9};
          const m=S.estoque.map(x=>[sc(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,6).map(a=>a[1]);
          h.innerHTML=m.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-pk="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum item encontrado.</div>';
          h.querySelectorAll('[data-pk]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>add({t:'item',id:b.dataset.pk}))}
        else{const nm=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase();const t2=nm(q.value.trim());
          const m=S.equips.filter(e=>nzCod(e.codigo).includes(k)||nm([e.tipo,e.marca,e.modelo,e.placa].join(' ')).includes(t2)).sort((a,b)=>(nzCod(a.codigo).startsWith(k)?0:1)-(nzCod(b.codigo).startsWith(k)?0:1)||byCode(a,b)).slice(0,8);
          h.innerHTML=m.length?m.map(e=>`<button type="button" class="tq eqs-o" data-pe="${esc(e.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc([e.tipo,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></button>`).join(''):'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum equipamento encontrado.</div>';
          h.querySelectorAll('[data-pe]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>add({t:'eq',id:b.dataset.pe}))}};
      const cm=sb.querySelector('#avn-cam');if(cm)cm.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item)add({t:'item',id:v.item.id});else toast('Item nao encontrado no estoque.')}})});
    drawAx();
    go.onclick=async()=>{if(go.disabled)return;go.disabled=true;
      const ok=await safe(()=>S.db.collection('avisos').add({de:u.uid,para:[...D.para],texto:ta.value.trim(),anexos:D.anexos,ts:Date.now(),estado:{},resp:[]}),'Aviso enviado');
      if(ok)goBack();else upd()}}})};
  show();
}
/* item do estoque pelo codigo exato (codigo, referencia ou codigo de barras) */
function stFind(v){const t=nzCod(v);if(t.length<3)return null;const l=S.estoque.filter(x=>[stCod(x),x.codigo,x.ref,...(x.eans||[])].some(c=>c&&nzCod(c)===t));return l.length===1?l[0]:null}
/* tela: preventivas realizadas de um equipamento (cada linha abre o detalhamento) */
/* selo de ajuste: lancamento feito com o modo Ajuste ligado (sem mexer no estoque).
   Um toque no selo mostra o significado num balao pequeno, sem abrir a linha. */
document.addEventListener('click',ev=>{const a=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('.ajs');if(!a||a.closest('.toast'))return;ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation();
  document.querySelectorAll('.ajtip').forEach(x=>x.remove());const r=a.getBoundingClientRect(),t=document.createElement('div');t.className='ajtip';t.setAttribute('role','status');t.textContent='Lancado em ajuste, sem alterar o estoque';document.body.appendChild(t);
  const w=t.offsetWidth,h=t.offsetHeight,x=Math.max(8,Math.min(innerWidth-w-8,r.left+r.width/2-w/2)),up=r.top>h+16;
  t.style.left=x+'px';t.style.top=(up?r.top-h-8:r.bottom+8)+'px';t.style.setProperty('--ax',(r.left+r.width/2-x)+'px');t.classList.add(up?'up':'dn');
  const off=()=>{t.remove();document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',off,true);removeEventListener('scroll',off,true)};setTimeout(off,3500);setTimeout(()=>{document.addEventListener('pointerdown',off,true);addEventListener('scroll',off,true)},0)},true);
const AJS='<span class="ajs" role="img" aria-label="Ajuste" title="Ajuste"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8.95 5.6h2.1l3.35 8.8h-1.95l-.75-2.1H8.3l-.75 2.1H5.6zm-.12 5.15h2.34L10 7.5z"/></svg></span>';
function openHistEq(eqId){
  nav('hq:'+eqId,()=>openHistEq(eqId));
  const e=eqById(eqId);if(!e){goBack();return}
  const ts=S.trocas.filter(x=>x.equipId===eqId).sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||'')||(Number(b.horimetro)||0)-(Number(a.horimetro)||0));
  openSheet({title:esc(e.codigo),sub:'Preventivas realizadas',
  body:ts.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${ts.map(t=>`<button class="tq" data-tr="${esc(t.id)}"><span class="tq-c" style="min-width:auto">${fdate(t.data)}</span>${t.ajuste?AJS:''}<span class="tq-m">${t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?'filtro de ar · '+esc(t.sub||''):t.tipo==='reprog'?'reprogramacao':t.tipo==='hmtroca'?'troca de horimetro':''}</span><span class="hh-hm num">${nf(t.horimetro)} h</span>${I.chev}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty">Nenhuma preventiva registrada.</div>',
  mount:sh=>sh.querySelectorAll('[data-tr]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openTroca(b.dataset.tr))});
}
function openTroca(id){
  nav('tr:'+id,()=>openTroca(id));
  const t=S.trocas.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!t)return;const e=eqById(t.equipId);
  if(t.tipo==='hmtroca'){openSheet({title:`Troca de horimetro de ${fdate(t.data)}`,sub:e?`${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(e.tipo)}`:'',
    body:`<div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Relogio antigo</span><b class="num">${nf(t.antigo)} h</b></div><div class="r"><span>Relogio novo</span><b class="num">${nf(t.horimetro)} h</b></div><div class="r"><span>Data</span><b>${fdate(t.data)}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Responsavel</span><b>${esc(t.resp||'—')}</b></div>${t.obs?`<div class="r"><span>Motivo</span><b>${esc(t.obs)}</b></div>`:''}</div>
    <p class="note" style="margin:10px 2px 0">Registros anteriores a esta data usam a contagem do relogio antigo.</p>`});return}
  if(t.tipo==='reprog'){openSheet({title:`Reprogramacao de ${fdate(t.data)}`,sub:e?`${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(e.tipo)}`:'',
    body:`<div class="lbl">Itens reprogramados</div><div class="spec">${(t.itens||[]).map(i=>`<div class="r"><span>${esc(itNome(i))}<small class="rp-mk">${esc(i.motivo||'')}</small></span><b class="rp-de"><s>${i.de!=null&&i.de!==''?nf(i.de):'—'}</s>→<b>${nf(i.para)} h</b></b></div>`).join('')}</div>
    <div class="spec" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="r"><span>Data</span><b>${fdate(t.data)}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Horimetro</span><b class="num">${nf(t.horimetro)} h</b></div><div class="r"><span>Responsavel</span><b>${esc(t.resp||'—')}</b></div></div>`});return}
  openSheet({title:t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?`Filtro de ar · ${esc(t.sub)}`:`Preventiva de ${fdate(t.data)}`,sub:[e?`${esc(e.codigo)} · ${esc(e.tipo)}`:'',t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?`${esc(t.servico||'')} em ${fdate(t.data)}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '),
  body:`<div class="hmcard"><div><div class="k">Horimetro na troca</div><div class="v">${nf(t.horimetro)}<small>h</small></div>${t.hmConv!=null?`<div class="d">relogio antigo · no relogio atual: ${nf(t.hmConv)} h</div>`:''}</div>${t.ajuste?AJS:''}</div>
  <div class="lbl">Material aplicado</div><div class="es-l">${(t.itens||[]).map(i=>`<div class="es-r st"><span class="es-b neu"><b>${nf(i.qtd,1)}</b><small>${esc(i.un||'')}</small></span><span class="es-t"><span class="es-n">${esc(itNome(i))}</span>${itCod(i)||i.espec?`<span class="es-c">${esc(itCod(i)||i.espec)}</span>`:''}</span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">Nenhum item informado.</div>'}</div>
  <div class="spec" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="r"><span>Tipo</span><b>${t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?`Filtro de ar · ${esc(t.sub||'')}${t.servico?' · '+esc(t.servico):''}`:'Preventiva'}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Data</span><b>${fdate(t.data)}</b></div><div class="r"><span>Horimetro</span><b class="num">${nf(t.horimetro)} h${t.hmConv!=null?`<small class="rp-mk" style="text-align:right">no relogio atual: ${nf(t.hmConv)} h</small>`:''}</b></div>${t.intervalo?`<div class="r"><span>Intervalo</span><b class="num">${nf(t.intervalo)} h</b></div>`:''}${(()=>{const c=cartaoDados(t);return c.prox!=null&&t.proximaH!==''&&t.proximaH!=null?`<div class="r"><span>Proxima</span><b class="num">${nf(c.prox)} h${c.trocasHm.length||t.hmConv!=null?'<small class="rp-mk" style="text-align:right">no relogio atual</small>':''}</b></div>`:''})()}${(()=>{const c=cartaoDados(t);return c.trocasHm.map(x=>`<div class="r"><span>Troca de horimetro</span><b>${fdate(x.data)}<small class="rp-mk" style="text-align:right">${nf(x.antigo)} → ${nf(x.horimetro)} h</small></b></div>`).join('')})()}<div class="r"><span>Responsavel</span><b>${esc(t.resp||'—')}</b></div>${t.obs?`<div class="r"><span>Observacoes</span><b>${esc(t.obs)}</b></div>`:''}</div>
  <div id="delbox"></div>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" id="t-card" style="flex:1">${I.share}Cartao preventiva</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{sh.querySelector('#t-card').onclick=()=>openCartao(id)}});
}

/* ---------- estoque ---------- */
function formStock(id,preset,onSaved){
  nav('fs:'+(id||'novo'),()=>formStock(id,preset,onSaved));
  const s=id?stById(id):{tipo:'filtro',un:'un',qtd:0,minimo:2,...(preset||{})};
  /* item novo: primeiro identifica (digitando ou pela camera); se ja existir, mostra; senao abre o resto do cadastro */
  const idStep=!id&&!(preset&&(preset.ref||preset.codigo));
  openSheet({title:id?'Editar item':'Novo item do estoque',sub:id?esc(stCod(s)):'',
  body:`${idStep?`<div class="fset" id="s-idf"><div class="sug-w"><label class="f">Codigo ou referencia<input id="s-idq" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="Ex.: 222-9021" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:19px;min-height:52px"></label><div class="sug" id="s-idh" aria-live="polite"></div></div>${rdBtn('s-cam')}</div>`:''}<form id="fs"${idStep?' hidden':''}><div class="fset"><div class="grid">
    <label class="f">Codigo <span class="hint">almoxarifado</span><input id="s-codigo" value="${esc(s.codigo||'')}" placeholder="Se tiver" autocapitalize="characters"></label>
    <label class="f">Referencia <span class="hint">codigo de fabrica</span><input id="s-ref" value="${esc(s.ref||'')}" placeholder="Ex.: WIX 51515" autocapitalize="characters"></label>
    <label class="f w">Descricao<input id="s-desc" required value="${esc(s.desc||'')}" placeholder="Ex.: Filtro oleo motor"></label>
    <label class="f w">Descricao do fabricante<textarea id="s-dfab" class="ag" rows="1" placeholder="Nome completo do catalogo (opcional)">${esc(s.descFab||'')}</textarea></label>
    <label class="f w">Aplicacao<textarea id="s-aplic" class="ag" rows="1" placeholder="Ex.: Ar da cabine">${esc(s.aplic||'')}</textarea></label>
    <label class="f">Marca<input id="s-marca" value="${esc(s.marca||'')}" placeholder="Ex.: Cat, Fleetguard"></label>
    <label class="f">Tipo<select id="s-tipo">${catOpts(s.tipo||'filtro')}</select></label>
    <label class="f">Unidade<input id="s-un" value="${esc(s.un||'')}" placeholder="un, L, kg, m"></label>
    <label class="f">Saldo atual<input id="s-qtd" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(s.qtd??0)}"></label>
    <label class="f">Minimo<input id="s-min" type="number" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(s.minimo??0)}"></label>
    <label class="f">Local<input id="s-local" value="${esc(s.local||'')}" placeholder="Almoxarifado"></label></div></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Encaixe de fabrica</h3><p class="note" style="margin:0">Equipamentos da frota em que este item encaixa.</p>
      <div class="tsec-l enc-sel" id="s-enc"></div>
      <div class="search">${I.search}<input id="s-encq" type="search" placeholder="Adicionar: TAG, modelo ou nº de serie" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Buscar equipamento"></div><div class="tsec-l enc-res" id="s-encr" hidden></div></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Fotos (opcional, ate 3)</h3><div id="s-ph"></div></div>
    ${atBox('st',id?stById(id):null)}<div id="delbox"></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="fs">Salvar</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{if(id)atBind(sh,'st',id);
    if(idStep){const ft=sh.querySelector('.sh-foot');if(ft)ft.hidden=true;const iq=sh.querySelector('#s-idq'),ih=sh.querySelector('#s-idh');
      const seguir=v=>{const V=v.trim().toUpperCase();if(V)sh.querySelector(/^\d{1,7}$/.test(V)?'#s-codigo':'#s-ref').value=V;sh.querySelector('#s-idf').remove();sh.querySelector('#fs').hidden=false;if(ft)ft.hidden=false;setTimeout(()=>sh.querySelector('#s-desc').focus(),60)};
      const busca=()=>{const v=iq.value.trim(),k=nzCod(v);if(!k){ih.innerHTML='';return}
        const m=S.estoque.filter(x=>nzCod(x.codigo).includes(k)||nzCod(x.ref).includes(k)||(x.eans||[]).some(e=>nzCod(e)===k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k)).sort(byCode).slice(0,6);
        ih.innerHTML=(m.length?`<p class="note" style="margin:4px 4px 6px">Ja cadastrado${m.length>1?'s':''}: toque para ver o cadastro</p><div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-sx="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'')
          +`<button type="button" class="btn ${m.length?'':'pri '}block" id="s-go" style="margin-top:${m.length?8:0}px">Cadastrar ${esc(v.toUpperCase())}${m.length?' como novo':''}</button>`;
        ih.querySelectorAll('[data-sx]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formMov(b.dataset.sx));ih.querySelector('#s-go').onclick=()=>seguir(v)};
      iq.oninput=busca;iq.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();const b=ih.querySelector('#s-go');b&&b.click()}};setTimeout(()=>iq.focus(),60);
      const scm=sh.querySelector('#s-cam');if(scm)scm.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'raw',use:v=>{iq.value=v.text||'';busca()}})}

    const fotoOrig=[...(s.fotos||[])],fotoNow=photoEditor(sh.querySelector('#s-ph'),[...fotoOrig],3,true);
    let enc=[...(s.encaixe||[])].filter(eqById);const encBox=sh.querySelector('#s-enc'),encQ=sh.querySelector('#s-encq'),encR=sh.querySelector('#s-encr');
    const eqLine=e=>[e.tipo,[e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' '),e.serie?'serie '+e.serie:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    /* encaixe novo: escolhe o intervalo de troca deste item naquele equipamento (so neste momento) */
    const encOrig=new Set(s.encaixe||[]),encIv={};const kitSt=isKit(s);
    const drawEnc=()=>{encBox.innerHTML=enc.map(id=>{const e=eqById(id);return `<div class="tq"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc(eqLine(e))}</span><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-encrm="${esc(id)}">Tirar</button></div>`}).join('');encBox.hidden=!enc.length;
      encBox.querySelectorAll('[data-encrm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{enc=enc.filter(x=>x!==b.dataset.encrm);delete encIv[b.dataset.encrm];drawEnc()});
      encBox.querySelectorAll('[data-enciv]').forEach(v=>v.onchange=()=>{encIv[v.dataset.enciv]=v.value})};drawEnc();
    const nz=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]/g,'');
    encQ.oninput=()=>{const q=nz(encQ.value);if(!q){encR.hidden=true;return}
      const hits=S.eqCtl.filter(e=>!enc.includes(e.id)&&nz([e.codigo,e.marca,e.modelo,e.serie,e.tipo].join(' ')).includes(q)).sort(byCode).slice(0,8);
      encR.hidden=false;encR.innerHTML=hits.map(e=>`<button type="button" class="tq" data-encadd="${esc(e.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc(eqLine(e))}</span>${I.plus}</button>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">Nenhum equipamento encontrado.</div>';
      encR.querySelectorAll('[data-encadd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{enc.push(b.dataset.encadd);encQ.value='';encR.hidden=true;drawEnc()})};
    sh.querySelector('#s-tipo').onchange=ev=>{const u=sh.querySelector('#s-un');if(UNS_AUTO().includes(u.value.trim()))u.value=catUn(ev.target.value)};
    sh.querySelectorAll('textarea.ag').forEach(t=>{const g=()=>{t.style.height='auto';t.style.height=t.scrollHeight+2+'px'};t.oninput=()=>{if(/\n/.test(t.value))t.value=t.value.replace(/\s*\n\s*/g,' ');g()};requestAnimationFrame(g)});
    sh.querySelector('#fs').onsubmit=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      /* com o Ajuste ligado o saldo nao pode ser mexido pelo cadastro */
      if(S.ajuste&&(Number(sh.querySelector('#s-qtd').value)||0)!==(id?Number(s.qtd)||0:0)){
        /* nada do que foi preenchido se perde: e so desativar e tocar em Salvar de novo */
        let bx=sh.querySelector('#aj-ask');if(!bx){sh.querySelector('#s-qtd').closest('.fset').insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div class="banner aj-b" id="aj-ask" style="margin-top:12px"><div>Desative o Ajuste para alterar o saldo.</div><button type="button" class="btn sm" id="aj-off">Desativar</button></div>');bx=sh.querySelector('#aj-ask');
          bx.querySelector('#aj-off').onclick=()=>{setAjuste(false);bx.remove()}}
        bx.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});return}
      const rem=[...fotoOrig.filter(x=>!fotoNow.includes(x)).map((x,i,a)=>a.length>1?`foto ${i+1}`:'foto'),...(s.encaixe||[]).filter(x=>!enc.includes(x)&&eqById(x)).map(x=>`encaixe em ${eqById(x).codigo}`)];confirmRemovals(sh.querySelector('#delbox'),rem,saveSt)};
    const saveSt=async()=>{const g=k=>sh.querySelector('#s-'+k).value.trim();
      if(!g('codigo')&&!g('ref')){toast('Informe o codigo do almoxarifado ou a referencia.');return}
      {const tpN=g('tipo');const dup=S.estoqueAll.find(x=>x.id!==id&&!(tpN==='oleo'&&x.tipo==='oleo')&&((g('codigo')&&(nzCod(x.codigo)===nzCod(g('codigo'))||nzCod(x.ref)===nzCod(g('codigo'))))||(g('ref')&&(nzCod(x.ref)===nzCod(g('ref'))||nzCod(x.codigo)===nzCod(g('ref'))))));
       if(dup){toast(`Esse codigo ja esta cadastrado em "${dup.desc}".`);return}}
      const body={codigo:g('codigo').toUpperCase(),ref:g('ref').toUpperCase(),desc:g('desc'),descFab:g('dfab'),marca:g('marca'),aplic:g('aplic'),encaixe:enc,tipo:g('tipo'),un:g('un')||'un',local:g('local'),qtd:Number(g('qtd'))||0,minimo:Number(g('min'))||0,fotos:fotoNow.slice(0,3),exemplo:!!s.exemplo,...(s.eans&&s.eans.length?{eans:s.eans}:{}),...((id&&stById(id)||s).inativo?{inativo:true}:{})};
      const ref=id?S.db.doc('estoque/'+id):S.db.collection('estoque').doc();
      if(await safe(async()=>{await ref.set(body);if(onSaved)await onSaved(ref.id,body);
          for(const [eid,v] of Object.entries(encIv)){if(v===''||!enc.includes(eid))continue;const eq=eqById(eid);if(eq)await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+eid).update({ivEnc:{...(eq.ivEnc||{}),[ref.id]:ivVal(v)}})}},onSaved?(onSaved.msg||'Item cadastrado e entrada lancada'):'Item salvo')){commitPhotos(fotoOrig,fotoNow);id||onSaved?goBack():closeSheet()}}}});
}
function movLine(m,un){const e=m.equipId&&eqById(m.equipId);const ent=m.tipo==='entrada';
  return `<div class="r"><span>${sdate(m.data)} · ${ent?(m.origem==='estorno'?'Devolucao':'Entrada'):m.origem==='preventiva'?'Preventiva':'Saida'}${e?' · '+esc(e.codigo):''}${m.ajuste?AJS:''}${m.obs?`<small class="mv-o">${esc(m.obs)}</small>`:''}</span><b class="num ${ent?'mv-in':'mv-out'}">${ent?'+':'−'}${nf(m.qtd,1)} ${esc(un)}</b></div>`}
function formMov(id){
  nav('mv:'+id,()=>formMov(id));
  const s=stById(id);if(!s)return;if(isKit(s))return kitView(s);const c=stockCls(s);
  const inKits=S.estoque.filter(k=>isKit(k)&&(k.comps||[]).some(x=>x.id===id)).sort(byCode).map(k=>stCod(k));
  const used=S.equips.filter(e=>(e.itens||[]).some(i=>i.estoqueId===id)).sort(byCode).map(e=>e.codigo);
  const enc=(s.encaixe||[]).map(eqById).filter(Boolean).sort(byCode);
  openSheet({title:esc(s.desc),sub:`<span class="num">${esc(stCod(s))}</span> · ${esc(catNm(s.tipo))}${s.marca?` · ${esc(s.marca)}`:''}${s.local?` · ${esc(s.local)}`:''}`,
  body:`${s.inativo?`<div class="nv-b on" style="margin:0 0 12px"><div><b>Item inativo</b><span>Fora das listas, buscas e dos alertas de estoque. Para reativar: + › Editar.</span></div></div>`:''}<div class="hmcard"><div><div class="k">Saldo</div><div class="v">${nf(s.qtd,1)}<small>${esc(s.un)}</small></div><div class="d">Minimo ${nf(s.minimo,1)} ${esc(s.un)}</div></div><span class="pill ${c}">${c==='bad'?'Zerado':c==='warn'?'Repor':'Ok'}</span></div>
  <div class="lbl">Dados do item</div><div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Codigo almoxarifado</span><b class="num">${s.codigo?esc(s.codigo):'<span style="color:var(--faint)">—</span>'}</b></div>${s.ref?`<div class="r"><span>Referencia</span><b class="num">${esc(s.ref)}</b></div>`:''}${s.descFab?`<div class="r"><span>Descricao do fabricante</span><b>${esc(s.descFab)}</b></div>`:''}${s.marca?`<div class="r"><span>Marca</span><b>${esc(s.marca)}</b></div>`:''}${s.aplic?`<div class="r"><span>Aplicacao</span><b>${esc(s.aplic)}</b></div>`:''}${s.local?`<div class="r"><span>Local</span><b>${esc(s.local)}</b></div>`:''}</div>
  ${enc.length?`<div class="lbl">Encaixe de fabrica <span class="c">${enc.length}</span></div><div class="spec">${enc.map(e=>`<div class="r"><span><b class="num">${esc(e.codigo)}</b></span><b>${esc([e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' '))}${e.serie?` · <span class="num">${esc(e.serie)}</span>`:''}</b></div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}
  <div class="lbl">Usado em</div><div class="spec">${used.length?specList('Planos',used.map(u=>[u])):'<div class="r"><span>Planos</span><b>Nenhum ligado</b></div>'}${inKits.length?specList('Kits',inKits.map(k=>[k])):''}</div>
  <div class="lbl">Movimentacoes</div><div id="mv-box"><div class="note" style="padding:6px 2px">Carregando…</div></div>
  ${fotoStHTML(s)}`,
  foot:avisarFoot('item',id),
  mount:sh=>{bindFotoSt(sh);
    S.db.collection('movs').where('estoqueId','==',id).get().then(q=>{const b=sh.querySelector('#mv-box');if(!b||!b.isConnected)return;
      const l=q.docs.map(d=>d.data()).sort((a,b)=>(b.data||'').localeCompare(a.data||'')||(b.ts||0)-(a.ts||0)).slice(0,6);
      b.innerHTML=l.length?`<div class="spec">${l.map(m=>movLine(m,s.un)).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty">Nenhuma movimentacao registrada.</div>'}).catch(()=>{const b=sh.querySelector('#mv-box');if(b)b.innerHTML='<div class="note">Nao foi possivel carregar.</div>'})}});
}
/* + › Entrada / Saida: informa so o codigo ou a referencia; o item e localizado sozinho */
const nzCod=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s.\-\/]/g,'');
const findSt=v=>{const k=nzCod(v);return k?S.estoque.find(x=>!isKit(x)&&(nzCod(x.codigo)===k||nzCod(x.ref)===k)):null};
function formMovEst(dir){
  nav('mve:'+dir,()=>formMovEst(dir));
  const ent=dir==='entrada',D=formMovEst.d=formMovEst.d||{};D.dt=D.dt||today();
  const done=formMovEst.done&&formMovEst.done.dir===dir?formMovEst.done:null;
  if(done){const x=stById(done.id);
    openSheet({title:ent?'Entrada lancada':'Saida lancada',sub:fdate(done.dt),
      body:`<div class="mv-done"><span class="mv-ok"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg></span><div class="mv-dq num">${ent?'+':'−'}${nf(done.n,1)} <small>${esc(x?x.un:'')}</small></div>
        <b>${esc(x?x.desc:'Item')}</b><span class="num">${esc(x?stCod(x):'')}</span>
        <div class="mv-ds"><span>Saldo anterior <b class="num">${nf(done.antes,1)}</b></span><span>Novo saldo <b class="num">${nf(done.saldo,1)}</b></span></div></div>`,
      foot:`<button type="button" class="btn" id="mv-rel">Relatorio</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" id="mv-new">${ent?'Nova entrada':'Nova saida'}</button>`,
      mount:sh=>{sh.querySelector('#mv-rel').onclick=()=>openRelEstoque(done.id);
        sh.querySelector('#mv-new').onclick=()=>{formMovEst.done=null;NAV.pop();formMovEst(dir)}}});
    return}
  const eqs=S.eqCtl.slice().sort(byCode);
  openSheet({title:ent?'Entrada no estoque':'Saida do estoque',sub:'Codigo do almoxarifado ou referencia',
  body:`${S.ajuste?'<div class="banner aj-b" id="aj-ask"><div>Desative o Ajuste para lancar.</div><button type="button" class="btn sm" id="aj-off">Desativar</button></div>':''}<form id="mv-f"><div class="fset"><div class="grid">
    <div class="w sug-w"><label class="f" id="mv-cl">Codigo ou referencia<input id="mv-c" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="Ex.: 353-5058" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:19px;min-height:52px"></label>
    <div class="sug" id="mv-hit" aria-live="polite"></div></div>
    ${CAM_OK?`<div class="w" id="mv-camw">${rdBtn('mv-cam')}</div>`:''}
    <div class="w" id="mv-sel" hidden></div>
    <label class="f" data-mvx hidden>${ent?'Quantidade a adicionar':'Quantidade a retirar'}<input id="mv-q" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" inputmode="decimal" required style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:19px;min-height:52px"></label>
    <label class="f" data-mvx hidden>Data<input id="mv-dt" type="date" required value="${esc(D.dt)}"></label>
    <div class="w note" id="mv-prev" data-mvx hidden></div>
    ${ent?`<label class="f w" data-mvx hidden>Fornecedor<input id="mv-obs" placeholder="Opcional" value="${esc(D.forn||'')}"></label>`
      :`<label class="f" data-mvx hidden>Equipamento<select id="mv-eq" data-eqs><option value="">Nenhum (opcional)</option>${eqs.map(e=>`<option value="${esc(e.id)}">${esc(e.codigo)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label class="f" data-mvx hidden>Motivo<input id="mv-obs" placeholder="Opcional"></label>`}
  </div></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="mv-f" id="mv-go" style="flex:1" disabled>${ent?'Lancar entrada':'Lancar saida'}</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{
    const c=sh.querySelector('#mv-c'),q=sh.querySelector('#mv-q'),hit=sh.querySelector('#mv-hit'),go=sh.querySelector('#mv-go'),pv=sh.querySelector('#mv-prev');let cur=null;
    const prev=()=>{const n=Number(q.value)||0;pv.innerHTML=cur&&n>0?`Saldo ${nf(cur.qtd,1)} ${esc(cur.un)} → <b>${nf(Math.round(((Number(cur.qtd)||0)+(ent?n:-n))*10)/10,1)} ${esc(cur.un)}</b>`:''};
    /* primeiro identifica o item; so depois aparecem quantidade, data e o resto */
    const cl=sh.querySelector('#mv-cl'),camw=sh.querySelector('#mv-camw'),selb=sh.querySelector('#mv-sel'),mvx=[...sh.querySelectorAll('[data-mvx]')];
    const step=on=>{cl.hidden=on;if(camw)camw.hidden=on;hit.hidden=on;selb.hidden=!on;mvx.forEach(x=>x.hidden=!on);go.disabled=!on};
    const pick=(x,keepQ)=>{cur=x;D.cur=x.id;D.curDir=dir;c.value=stCod(x);hit.innerHTML='';
      selb.innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="mv-card" id="mv-card"><b>${esc(x.desc)}</b><span><span class="num">${esc(stCod(x))}</span>${x.marca?' · '+esc(x.marca):''} · saldo ${nf(x.qtd,1)} ${esc(x.un)}</span><em>Toque para ver o cadastro ›</em></button><div class="mv-tr"><button type="button" class="lnk" id="mv-tr">Trocar item</button></div>`;
      selb.querySelector('#mv-card').onclick=()=>formMov(x.id);
      selb.querySelector('#mv-tr').onclick=()=>{cur=null;D.cur=null;c.value='';selb.innerHTML='';step(false);prev();c.focus()};
      step(true);go.textContent=ent?'Lancar entrada':'Lancar saida';q.value=keepQ?(D.q||''):'';if(!keepQ)D.q='';prev();if(!keepQ)setTimeout(()=>q.focus(),60)};
    const search=()=>{cur=null;prev();const k=nzCod(c.value);go.textContent=ent?'Lancar entrada':'Lancar saida';
      if(!k){hit.innerHTML='';return}
      const its=S.estoque.filter(x=>!isKit(x)),score=x=>{const a=nzCod(x.codigo),b=nzCod(x.ref);return a===k||b===k?0:a.startsWith(k)||b.startsWith(k)?1:a.includes(k)||b.includes(k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k)?2:9};
      const m=its.map(x=>[score(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,5).map(a=>a[1]);
      if(m.length){hit.innerHTML=`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-pk="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`;
        hit.querySelectorAll('[data-pk]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>pick(stById(b.dataset.pk)))}
      else{const v=c.value.trim().toUpperCase();hit.innerHTML=`<div class="mv-hit no"><b>Item nao cadastrado</b><span>${ent?'Cadastre o item para lancar a entrada.':'Confira o codigo: so da saida de item cadastrado.'}</span>${ent?`<button type="button" class="btn sm" id="mv-cad" style="margin-top:8px">Cadastrar ${esc(v)}</button>`:''}</div>`;
        const nb=hit.querySelector('#mv-cad');if(nb)nb.onclick=()=>{const pre=/^\d{1,7}$/.test(v)?{codigo:v}:{ref:v};const cb=async sid=>{D.cur=sid;D.curDir=dir};cb.msg='Item cadastrado';formStock(null,pre,cb)}}};
    c.oninput=search;q.oninput=()=>{D.q=q.value;prev()};
    const cb2=sh.querySelector('#mv-cam');cb2&&(cb2.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item)pick(v.item);else if(v.text){c.value=v.text;search()}}}));
    if(D.cur&&D.curDir===dir&&stById(D.cur))pick(stById(D.cur),true);else{D.cur=null;setTimeout(()=>c.focus(),60)}
    c.onkeydown=ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();const f=hit.querySelector('[data-pk]');if(f)f.click()}};
    sh.querySelector('#mv-dt').onchange=ev=>D.dt=ev.target.value;
    ent&&(sh.querySelector('#mv-obs').oninput=ev=>D.forn=ev.target.value);
    /* entrada e saida mexem no saldo: com o Ajuste ligado nao lanca; o que foi digitado continua na tela */
    const ajb=sh.querySelector('#aj-off');ajb&&(ajb.onclick=()=>{setAjuste(false);sh.querySelector('#aj-ask').remove();prev()});
    sh.querySelector('#mv-f').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      if(S.ajuste){const a=sh.querySelector('#aj-ask');if(a){a.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});a.animate([{transform:'scale(1)'},{transform:'scale(1.03)'},{transform:'scale(1)'}],{duration:350})}else toast('Desative o Ajuste para lancar.','aj');return}
      if(!cur&&hit.querySelector('[data-pk]')){toast('Toque no item para selecionar.');return}
      const n=Number(q.value)||0;if(n<=0){toast('Informe a quantidade.');q.focus();return}
      const dt=sh.querySelector('#mv-dt').value||today(),obs=sh.querySelector('#mv-obs').value.trim(),eqId=ent?'':sh.querySelector('#mv-eq').value;
      const log=(sid,saldo)=>S.db.collection('movs').add({estoqueId:sid,tipo:dir,qtd:n,data:dt,obs,equipId:eqId,origem:'manual',saldo,ts:Date.now()});
      if(!cur){if(!ent){toast('Item nao encontrado.');return}
        const v=c.value.trim().toUpperCase(),pre=/^\d{1,7}$/.test(v)?{codigo:v}:{ref:v};
        formStock(null,{...pre,qtd:n},async sid=>{await log(sid,n);await S.db.doc('estoque/'+sid).update({ultEnt:{data:dt,qtd:n}});formMovEst.done={dir,id:sid,n,antes:0,saldo:n,dt}});return}
      const x=cur,antes=Number(x.qtd)||0,saldo=Math.round((antes+(ent?n:-n))*10)/10;
      if(!ent&&saldo<0){toast(`Saida maior que o saldo (${nf(x.qtd,1)} ${x.un}).`);return}
      if(await safe(async()=>{await S.db.doc('estoque/'+x.id).update({qtd:saldo,...(ent?{ultEnt:{data:dt,qtd:n}}:{})});await log(x.id,saldo)},`${ent?'Entrada':'Saida'} lancada`)){
        formMovEst.done={dir,id:x.id,n,antes,saldo,dt};D.cur=null;D.q='';NAV.pop();formMovEst(dir)}};
  }});
}
/* ---------- lista de compra ---------- */
const cartKeyIt=it=>{const st=stOfIt(it);return st?'s:'+st.id:'f:'+nzCod(it.espec||'')+'|'+nzCod(it.desc||'')};
const inCart=k=>S.cart.some(c=>c.k===k);
/* kit na lista de compra vira os itens que o compoem (qtd do kit × qtd de cada item) */
const kitExpand=list=>{const out=[];list.forEach(c=>{const k=c.estoqueId&&stById(c.estoqueId);
  if(k&&isKit(k)){const n=Math.max(1,Number(c.qtd)||1);kitComps(k).forEach(({c:cc,s:x})=>{const q=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(n*(Number(cc.qtd)||1)));const j=out.findIndex(o=>o.k==='s:'+x.id);
    if(j>=0)out[j]={...out[j],qtd:Math.max(Number(out[j].qtd)||0,q)};else out.push({...c,k:'s:'+x.id,estoqueId:x.id,qtd:q,un:x.un||'un',kit:k.id})})}
  else out.push(c)});return out};
const inCartSt=x=>isKit(x)?kitComps(x).length>0&&kitComps(x).every(({s:y})=>inCart('s:'+y.id)):inCart('s:'+x.id);
const inCartIt=it=>{const st=stOfIt(it);return st?inCartSt(st):inCart(cartKeyIt(it))};
const cartFromSt=x=>({k:'s:'+x.id,estoqueId:x.id,qtd:Math.max(1,Math.ceil((Number(x.minimo)||0)-(Number(x.qtd)||0))),un:x.un||'un'});
const cartFromIt=(it,e)=>{const st=stOfIt(it);return st?{...cartFromSt(st),qtd:Math.max(1,Number(it.qtd)||1),equipId:e.id}:{k:cartKeyIt(it),cod:it.espec||'',desc:it.desc||'',qtd:Math.max(1,Number(it.qtd)||1),un:it.un||'un',equipId:e.id}};
const cartNome=c=>{const x=c.estoqueId&&stById(c.estoqueId);return x?x.desc:c.desc||'Item'};
const cartMarca=c=>{const x=c.estoqueId&&stById(c.estoqueId);return (x&&x.marca)||c.marca||''};
const liMarca=i=>{const x=i.estoqueId&&stById(i.estoqueId);return (x&&x.marca)||i.marca||''};
const cartCod=c=>{const x=c.estoqueId&&stById(c.estoqueId);return x?stCod(x):c.cod||'—'};
function cartBtnHTML(kind,id,on){return on?`<button type="button" class="btn sm cart-on" disabled>✓ Na lista de compra</button>`:`<button type="button" class="btn sm" data-cartadd="${kind}:${esc(id)}">Lista de compra</button>`}
async function saveCart(itens){S.cart=itens;cartBtn();return safe(()=>S.db.doc('config/compras').set({itens}),'')}
async function addCart(list){list=kitExpand(list);const cur=[...S.cart];let n=0;list.forEach(c=>{if(!cur.some(x=>x.k===c.k)){cur.push({...c,add:today()});n++}});
  if(!n){toast('Ja esta na lista de compra.');return}
  if(await saveCart(cur)){toast(n===1?'Adicionado a lista de compra':`${n} itens adicionados a lista de compra`);const b=$('#cartb');b.classList.remove('pop');void b.offsetWidth;b.classList.add('pop');if(!sheetOpen)render()}}
function cartBtn(){const b=$('#cartb');if(!b)return;b.hidden=!S.cart.length;$('#cartn').textContent=S.cart.length}
function openCart(){
  nav('cart:',()=>openCart());
  if(S.cart.some(c=>{const k=c.estoqueId&&stById(c.estoqueId);return k&&isKit(k)})){const m=[];kitExpand(S.cart).forEach(c=>{const j=m.findIndex(o=>o.k===c.k);if(j>=0)m[j]={...m[j],qtd:Math.max(Number(m[j].qtd)||0,Number(c.qtd)||0)};else m.push(c)});saveCart(m)}
  const its=S.cart;
  const row=(c,i)=>`<div class="ct-r" data-i="${i}"><div class="ct-i"><b>${esc(cartNome(c))}</b><span><span class="num">${esc(cartCod(c)==='—'?'':cartCod(c))}</span>${cartCod(c)==='—'?'':' · '}${cartMarca(c)?esc(cartMarca(c))+' · ':''}${c.equipId&&eqById(c.equipId)?'para '+esc(eqById(c.equipId).codigo):c.estoqueId?'reposicao de estoque':'item avulso'}</span></div>
    <div class="ct-st"><button type="button" class="ct-b" data-d="-1" aria-label="Diminuir"${(Number(c.qtd)||0)<=1?' disabled':''}>−</button><span class="ct-v num">${nf(c.qtd,1)}<small>${esc(c.un||'')}</small></span><button type="button" class="ct-b" data-d="1" aria-label="Aumentar">+</button></div><button type="button" class="ct-x" data-rm="${i}" aria-label="Tirar da lista">✕</button></div>`;
  openSheet({title:'Lista de compra',sub:its.length?`${its.length} ${its.length===1?'item':'itens'} a comprar`:'Nenhum item na lista',
  body:`${its.length?'':'<div class="empty">Nenhum item na lista.</div>'}
    ${its.length?(()=>{const ix=its.map((c,i)=>({c,i})),solo=ix.filter(x=>!(x.c.kit&&stById(x.c.kit))),kids=[...new Set(ix.filter(x=>x.c.kit&&stById(x.c.kit)).map(x=>x.c.kit))];
      return (solo.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${solo.map(x=>row(x.c,x.i)).join('')}</div>`:'')+kids.map(kid=>{const k=stById(kid);return `<div class="ct-kit"><div class="ct-kh"><span>Kit</span>${esc(k.desc)}<small class="num">${esc(stCod(k))}</small></div>${ix.filter(x=>x.c.kit===kid).map(x=>row(x.c,x.i)).join('')}</div>`}).join('')})()+'<div id="ct-pick"></div>':''}
`,
  foot:its.length?`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="ct-ok" style="flex:1">Concluir lista</button>`:'',
  mount:sh=>{
    sh.querySelectorAll('.ct-b').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const r=b.closest('.ct-r'),i=+r.dataset.i;const c=[...S.cart];const v=Math.max(1,(Number(c[i].qtd)||0)+Number(b.dataset.d));c[i]={...c[i],qtd:v};
      r.querySelector('.ct-v').firstChild.textContent=nf(v,1);r.querySelector('[data-d="-1"]').disabled=v<=1;saveCart(c)});
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-rm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{const c=S.cart.filter((x,i)=>i!==+b.dataset.rm);await saveCart(c);if(!c.length){toast('Lista vazia');closeSheet();render()}else openCartRe()});
    const pk=sh.querySelector('#ct-pick');pk&&addPicker(pk,x=>inCartSt(x),async x=>{await addCart([cartFromSt(x)]);openCartRe()},async v=>{await addCart([{k:'f:|'+nzCod(v),cod:'',desc:v,qtd:1,un:'un'}]);openCartRe()});
    const ok=sh.querySelector('#ct-ok');ok&&(ok.onclick=async()=>{const itens=S.cart.map(c=>({cod:c.estoqueId?cartCod(c):(c.cod||''),marca:cartMarca(c),desc:cartNome(c),qtd:c.qtd,un:c.un||'',estoqueId:c.estoqueId||'',equipId:c.equipId||''}));
      const lista={data:today(),ts:Date.now(),itens};
      if(await safe(async()=>{await S.db.collection('listas').add(lista);await S.db.doc('config/compras').set({itens:[]})},'')){S.cart=[];cartBtn();render();NAV.pop();openListaFeita(lista)}});
  }});
}
function openCartRe(){NAV.pop();openCart()}
/* texto da lista para compartilhar */
/* DIRETRIZ DE COMPARTILHAMENTO (vale para tudo que o app compartilha, hoje e no futuro):
   - sempre como IMAGEM, gerada pelo app no padrao visual do cartao (faixa escura, titulo, data, rodape);
   - nunca texto, link ou atalho de aplicativo (ex.: links de WhatsApp);
   - botao unico chamado "Compartilhar", que abre o menu do celular;
   - sem o menu do celular, oferece salvar a imagem no aparelho. */
async function shareImg(cv,fname,title){
  const blob=await new Promise(r=>cv.toBlob(r,'image/png'));
  try{const f=new File([blob],fname,{type:'image/png'});if(navigator.canShare&&navigator.canShare({files:[f]})){await navigator.share({files:[f],title});return}}catch(e){if(e&&e.name==='AbortError')return}
  const dl=await claude.use('downloads').catch(()=>null);
  if(dl){try{await dl.save({filename:fname,data:blob});toast('Imagem salva');return}catch(e){if(e&&e.code==='declined')return}}
  toast('Nao foi possivel compartilhar a imagem.')}
/* desenha a lista como imagem, no mesmo estilo do cartao preventiva */
async function drawLista(l){
  try{await Promise.all(['700 64px "Barlow Condensed"','600 30px "IBM Plex Sans"','400 30px "IBM Plex Sans"','600 30px "IBM Plex Mono"'].map(f=>document.fonts.load(f)))}catch(_){}
  const C={bg:'#ffffff',ink:'#0b2470',inkFg:'#ffffff',inkMuted:'#a9b8e6',acc:'#0033a0',accInk:'#ffffff',fg:'#121a2c',mut:'#4f5a70',line:'#dfe4ec',sunk:'#f3f5f9'};
  const DSP='"Barlow Condensed","Arial Narrow",Arial,sans-serif',SAN='"IBM Plex Sans",Arial,sans-serif',MON='"IBM Plex Mono",Menlo,monospace';
  const W=1080,P=64,its=l.itens||[],cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=W;cv.height=420+its.length*150+200;const x=cv.getContext('2d');
  x.fillStyle=C.bg;x.fillRect(0,0,W,cv.height);const font=(w,sz,f)=>x.font=`${w} ${sz}px ${f}`;
  const rr=(X,Y,w,h,r,f)=>{x.beginPath();x.roundRect?x.roundRect(X,Y,w,h,r):x.rect(X,Y,w,h);x.fillStyle=f;x.fill()};
  const fit=(t,mw)=>{let s2=String(t);if(x.measureText(s2).width<=mw)return s2;while(s2.length>1&&x.measureText(s2+'…').width>mw)s2=s2.slice(0,-1);return s2+'…'};
  x.fillStyle=C.ink;x.fillRect(0,0,W,190);x.fillStyle='#e30613';x.fillRect(P,58,10,74);
  font(600,34,MON);const dt=fdate(l.data),dw=x.measureText(dt).width+40;rr(W-P-dw,64,dw,62,31,'#ffffff');x.fillStyle=C.ink;x.fillText(dt,W-P-dw+20,107);
  font(700,70,DSP);x.fillStyle=C.inkFg;x.fillText('LISTA DE COMPRA',P+34,118);
  font(500,28,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.inkMuted;x.fillText('Rondaflex Ltda · Controle de Frota',P+36,158);
  let y=190+80;font(700,38,DSP);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText('ITENS',P,y);y+=20;x.fillStyle=C.acc;x.fillRect(P,y,W-2*P,4);y+=4;
  its.forEach((i,k)=>{const q=`${nf(i.qtd,1)} ${i.un||''}`.trim();font(600,34,MON);const qw=x.measureText(q).width;
    const yy=y+58;font(600,34,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText(fit(i.desc||'Item',W-2*P-qw-40),P,yy);font(600,34,MON);x.fillText(q,W-P-qw,yy);
    let h=80;const sl=[i.cod&&i.cod!=='—'?i.cod:'',liMarca(i)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');if(sl){font(500,28,MON);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText(fit(sl,W-2*P-(i.equipId?220:0)),P,yy+44);h=120}
    const e=i.equipId&&eqById(i.equipId);if(e){font(400,26,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.mut;const t='para '+e.codigo;const tw=x.measureText(t).width;x.fillText(t,W-P-tw,yy+44);if(h<120)h=120}
    y+=h;if(k<its.length-1){x.fillStyle=C.line;x.fillRect(P,y,W-2*P,2)}});
  y+=40;x.fillStyle=C.line;x.fillRect(P,y,W-2*P,2);y+=48;font(400,24,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText(`Gerado em ${fdate(today())} pelo Controle de Frota`,P,y);y+=P-8;
  const out=document.createElement('canvas');out.width=W;out.height=Math.ceil(y);out.getContext('2d').drawImage(cv,0,0);return out;
}
async function shareLista(l){shareImg(await drawLista(l),`Lista de compra ${l.data||''}.png`,'Lista de compra')}
/* tela seguinte a conclusao: aviso + compartilhar */
function openListaFeita(l){
  nav('ldone',()=>openListaFeita(l));
  openSheet({title:'Lista concluida',sub:fdate(l.data),
  body:`<div class="mv-done" style="padding-bottom:4px"><span class="mv-ok"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg></span><b>Lista de compra concluida</b><span class="note">Ela fica guardada em + › Listas de compra.</span></div>
    <div id="ld-img" class="card-wait" style="min-height:160px">Montando a imagem…</div>`,
  foot:`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="ld-sh" style="flex:1">${I.share}Compartilhar</button>`,
  mount:async sh=>{sh.querySelector('#ld-sh').onclick=()=>shareLista(l);const cv=await drawLista(l),b=sh.querySelector('#ld-img');if(b&&b.isConnected){b.className='';b.style.minHeight='';b.innerHTML=`<img class="card-prev" style="margin-top:14px" alt="Imagem da lista de compra" src="${cv.toDataURL('image/png')}">`}}});
}
/* botao "Adicionar item" que so mostra a busca quando tocado */
function addPicker(box,has,onPick,onAvulso){
  box.innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="ct-add">${I.plus}Adicionar item</button>`;
  box.querySelector('.ct-add').onclick=ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();box.innerHTML=`<div class="ct-pk"><div class="sug-w"><input class="ct-c" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="TAG, codigo, referencia ou nome"><div class="ct-hit sug"></div></div>${rdBtn('ct-cam')}</div>`;
    const c=box.querySelector('.ct-c'),hit=box.querySelector('.ct-hit');c.focus();
    const cc=box.querySelector('#ct-cam');if(cc)cc.onclick=e2=>{e2.stopPropagation();rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(v.item)onPick(v.item);else if(v.text){c.value=v.text;c.oninput()}}})};
    c.oninput=()=>{const k=nzCod(c.value);if(!k){hit.innerHTML='';return}
      /* TAG de equipamento: mostra o kit dele e os filtros compativeis */
      const eq=S.equips.find(e=>nzCod(e.codigo)===k),cp=eq?eqCompat(eq):{kits:[],its:[]},shown=new Set([...cp.kits,...cp.its].map(x=>x.id));
      const m=S.estoque.filter(x=>!shown.has(x.id)&&(nzCod(x.codigo).includes(k)||nzCod(x.ref).includes(k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k))).slice(0,5);
      const rows=l=>`<div class="tsec-l">${l.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-ad="${esc(x.id)}"`,end:has(x)?'<span class="es-e">na lista</span>':`<span class="es-e">${I.plus}</span>`})).join('')}</div>`;
      hit.innerHTML=(eq?`<div class="ct-eq"><b class="num">${esc(eq.codigo)}</b> ${esc(eq.tipo||'')}</div>${cp.kits.length?`<div class="ig-h">${cp.kits.length>1?'Kits':'Kit'}</div>${rows(cp.kits)}`:''}${cp.its.length?`<div class="ig-h">Filtros compativeis</div>${rows(cp.its)}`:''}${!cp.kits.length&&!cp.its.length?'<p class="note" style="margin:0 2px">Nenhum kit ou filtro ligado a este equipamento.</p>':''}`:'')
        +(m.length?(eq?'<div class="ig-h">Outros resultados</div>':'')+rows(m):'')
        +`<div class="ct-av"><span>${m.length||eq?'Nao e nenhum desses?':'Nao cadastrado.'}</span><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-av>Adicionar "${esc(c.value.trim())}" avulso</button></div>`;
      hit.querySelectorAll('[data-ad]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>onPick(stById(b.dataset.ad)));
      hit.querySelector('[data-av]').onclick=()=>{const v=c.value.trim();if(v)onAvulso(v)}}};
}
function openListas(fresh,sel0){
  nav('listas:'+(fresh?1:0),()=>openListas(fresh,[...(openListas.sel||[])]));
  const ls=S.listas.slice().sort((a,b)=>(b.ts||0)-(a.ts||0));
  const n=l=>`${(l.itens||[]).length} ${(l.itens||[]).length===1?'item':'itens'}`;
  const sel=new Set((sel0||[]).filter(id=>ls.some(l=>l.id===id)));openListas.sel=sel;
  const CK='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg>';
  const body=!ls.length?'<div class="empty">Nenhuma lista concluida ainda.</div>'
    :`<div class="ilist">${ls.map((l,j)=>`<div class="ia" data-li="${esc(l.id)}"><button type="button" class="ia-h lp" data-ia data-lp="${esc(l.id)}" aria-expanded="${!!(fresh&&j===0)}"><span class="lp-ck">${CK}</span><span class="ia-t">${fdate(l.data)}${l.mesclou?' <span class="note">· mesclada</span>':''}</span><span class="ist">${n(l)}</span>${I.chev}</button>
    <div class="ia-b"${fresh&&j===0?'':' hidden'}><div class="hh-its">${(l.itens||[]).map(i=>`<div class="hh-i"><span>${esc(i.desc||'Item')}${(i.cod&&i.cod!=='—')||liMarca(i)?`<small>${esc([i.cod&&i.cod!=='—'?i.cod:'',liMarca(i)].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small>`:''}</span><b class="num">${nf(i.qtd,1)} ${esc(i.un||'')}</b></div>`).join('')}</div>
    <button type="button" class="btn sm" data-share="${j}">${I.share}Compartilhar</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div><p class="lp-tip">segure uma lista para selecionar</p>`;
  openSheet({title:'Listas de compra',sub:'Concluidas, da mais recente',
  body:body+'<div id="lp-conf"></div>',
  foot:`<div class="lp-bar" hidden><button type="button" class="btn danger" id="lp-del">Excluir</button><button type="button" class="btn pri" id="lp-mer" hidden>Mesclar listas</button></div>`,
  mount:sh=>{
    const foot=sh.querySelector('.sh-foot'),bar=sh.querySelector('.lp-bar'),conf=sh.querySelector('#lp-conf'),sub=sh.querySelector('.sh-head p,.sh-head .sub');
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-share]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>shareLista(ls[+b.dataset.share]));
    /* ao abrir uma lista, as demais somem; ao fechar, voltam */
    const solo=()=>{if(sel.size){sh.querySelectorAll('.ilist>.ia').forEach(x=>x.hidden=false);return}
      const all=[...sh.querySelectorAll('.ilist>.ia')],open=all.find(x=>x.querySelector('.ia-h').getAttribute('aria-expanded')==='true');
      all.forEach(x=>x.hidden=!!open&&x!==open);const tip=sh.querySelector('.lp-tip');tip&&(tip.hidden=!!open)};
    /* modo selecao: segurar seleciona; com selecao ativa, um toque marca ou desmarca */
    const paint=()=>{openListas.sel=sel;const on=sel.size>0;sh.classList.toggle('lp-mode',on);
      sh.querySelectorAll('.ilist>.ia').forEach(x=>{const s1=sel.has(x.dataset.li);x.classList.toggle('lp-sel',s1);if(on){const h=x.querySelector('.ia-h');h.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');x.querySelector('.ia-b').hidden=true}});
      if(foot)foot.style.display=on?'':'none';bar.hidden=!on;sh.querySelector('#lp-mer').hidden=sel.size<2;conf.innerHTML='';
      const tip=sh.querySelector('.lp-tip');tip&&(tip.textContent=on?'toque nas listas para marcar ou desmarcar':'segure uma lista para selecionar',tip.hidden=false);solo()};
    sh.querySelectorAll('[data-lp]').forEach(h=>{let tm=null,fired=false,x0=0,y0=0;
      const stop=()=>{clearTimeout(tm);tm=null;h.classList.remove('lp-hold')};
      h.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{fired=false;x0=ev.clientX;y0=ev.clientY;if(sel.size)return;h.classList.add('lp-hold');tm=setTimeout(()=>{fired=true;stop();try{navigator.vibrate&&navigator.vibrate(25)}catch(_){}sel.add(h.dataset.lp);paint()},600)});
      h.addEventListener('pointermove',ev=>{if(tm&&(Math.abs(ev.clientX-x0)>8||Math.abs(ev.clientY-y0)>8))stop()});
      ['pointerup','pointercancel','pointerleave'].forEach(t=>h.addEventListener(t,stop));
      h.addEventListener('click',ev=>{if(fired){ev.stopPropagation();ev.preventDefault();fired=false;return}
        if(sel.size){ev.stopPropagation();ev.preventDefault();const id=h.dataset.lp;sel.has(id)?sel.delete(id):sel.add(id);paint();return}
        setTimeout(solo,0)});
      h.addEventListener('contextmenu',ev=>ev.preventDefault())});
    sh.querySelector('#lp-del').onclick=()=>askDelete(conf,sel.size>1?'Excluir listas selecionadas? Nao sera possivel desfazer.':'Excluir lista selecionada? Nao sera possivel desfazer.',async()=>{const ids=[...sel];
      if(await safe(async()=>{for(const id of ids)await S.db.doc('listas/'+id).delete()},ids.length>1?'Listas excluidas':'Lista excluida')){NAV.pop();openListas()}});
    sh.querySelector('#lp-mer').onclick=()=>{if(sel.size<2)return;const ids=[...sel];
      askConfirm(conf,'Mesclar','Deseja mesclar listas selecionadas?',async()=>{if(await mergeListas(ids)){NAV.pop();openListas(true)}})};
    paint();
  }});
}
/* mescla listas concluidas numa so, somando itens iguais; as originais deixam de existir */
async function mergeListas(ids){
  const m=new Map();ids.map(id=>S.listas.find(l=>l.id===id)).filter(Boolean).forEach(l=>(l.itens||[]).forEach(i=>{const k=(i.estoqueId?'s:'+i.estoqueId:'f:'+nzCod(i.cod)+'|'+nzCod(i.desc))+'|'+(i.un||'');const c=m.get(k);
    if(c){c.qtd=Math.max(Number(c.qtd)||0,Number(i.qtd)||0);if(i.equipId&&c.equipId!==i.equipId)c.equipId=''}else m.set(k,{...i})}));
  return safe(async()=>{await S.db.collection('listas').add({data:today(),ts:Date.now(),itens:[...m.values()],mesclou:ids.length});for(const id of ids)await S.db.doc('listas/'+id).delete()},'Listas mescladas');
}
/* relatorio do estoque (somente leitura), com destaque para um item */
function openRelEstoque(hl){
  nav('rel:'+(hl||''),()=>openRelEstoque(hl));
  const its=S.estoque.filter(x=>!isKit(x));
  const G=CATS.map(c=>[catKey(c),catPl(c)]),grp=catGrp;
  const lastIn=id=>{const x=stById(id);return x&&x.ultEnt||null};
  const row=x=>{const m=lastIn(x.id);return esRow(x,{tag:'div',hl:x.id===hl,attr:x.id===hl?'id="rel-hl"':'',sub:`${esc(stCod(x))} · min ${nf(x.minimo,1)} · ${m?`entrada ${sdate(m.data)} +${nf(m.qtd,1)}`:'sem entradas'}`})};
  const tot=its.length,falta=its.filter(x=>stockCls(x)!=='ok').length;
  openSheet({title:'Relatorio do estoque',sub:`${tot} itens · ${falta} abaixo do minimo · ${fdate(today())}`,
  body:G.map(([k,lab])=>{const l=its.filter(x=>grp(x)===k).sort(byCode);return l.length?`<div class="es-g"><span>${lab}</span><em>${l.length}</em></div><div class="es-l">${l.map(row).join('')}</div>`:''}).join('')||'<div class="empty">Nenhum item cadastrado.</div>',
  mount:sh=>{const h=sh.querySelector('#rel-hl');h&&setTimeout(()=>h.scrollIntoView({block:'center'}),80)}});
}
/* registra no historico do item as baixas feitas por preventiva */
async function logBaixa(sid,q,dt,eqId){try{const x=stById(sid);await S.db.collection('movs').add({estoqueId:sid,tipo:'saida',qtd:q,data:dt,obs:'',equipId:eqId||'',origem:'preventiva',saldo:Math.round(((Number(x&&x.qtd)||0)-q)*10)/10,ts:Date.now()})}catch(e){console.error(e)}}

/* ---------- cartao preventiva (imagem para compartilhar) ---------- */
function cartaoDados(t){
  /* Horimetro: como foi lido no dia. Proxima: SEMPRE no relogio que esta hoje no equipamento (o que o mecanico vai ler).
     Se o relogio foi trocado depois deste lancamento, o cartao informa a troca. */
  const e=eqById(t.equipId)||{};const h0=Number(t.horimetro)||0,hS=t.hmConv!=null?Number(t.hmConv):h0;
  let pS=t.proximaH!==''&&t.proximaH!=null?Number(t.proximaH):null;
  if(pS==null&&t.tipo!=='filtro_ar'){const ivs=(t.itens||[]).map(i=>(e.itens||[]).find(p=>p.pid===i.pid)).filter(Boolean).map(p=>itIv(e,p)).filter(Boolean);if(ivs.length)pS=hS+Math.min(...ivs)}
  const dA=e.id?hmDeltaApos(e.id,t.data):0,dIn=Math.round((hS-h0)*10)/10,dNow=Math.round((dA-dIn)*10)/10;
  const prox=pS!=null?Math.round((pS+dNow)*10)/10:null,iv=t.intervalo||(pS!=null?Math.round((pS-hS)*10)/10:null);
  const cur=Number(e.horimetro)||0,last=!S.trocas.some(x=>x.equipId===t.equipId&&x.tipo===t.tipo&&(x.data||'')>(t.data||''));
  const trocasHm=e.id?hmTrocas(e.id).filter(x=>(x.data||'')>(t.data||'')).sort((a,b)=>(a.data||'').localeCompare(b.data||'')):[];
  return {e,h:h0,prox,iv,falta:prox!=null&&last?Math.round((prox-cur)*10)/10:null,cur,trocasHm};
}
async function drawCartao(t){
  try{await Promise.all(['700 64px "Barlow Condensed"','600 30px "IBM Plex Sans"','400 30px "IBM Plex Sans"','600 30px "IBM Plex Mono"'].map(f=>document.fonts.load(f)))}catch(_){}
  const {e,h,prox,iv,falta,cur,trocasHm}=cartaoDados(t);
  const C={bg:'#ffffff',ink:'#0b2470',inkFg:'#ffffff',inkMuted:'#a9b8e6',acc:'#0033a0',accInk:'#ffffff',fg:'#121a2c',mut:'#4f5a70',line:'#dfe4ec',sunk:'#f3f5f9',bad:'#c62f22',ok:'#1f7a4a'};
  const DSP='"Barlow Condensed","Arial Narrow",Arial,sans-serif',SAN='"IBM Plex Sans",Arial,sans-serif',MON='"IBM Plex Mono",Menlo,monospace';
  const W=1080,P=64,cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=W;cv.height=4200;const x=cv.getContext('2d');
  x.fillStyle=C.bg;x.fillRect(0,0,W,cv.height);x.textBaseline='alphabetic';
  const font=(w,sz,f)=>x.font=`${w} ${sz}px ${f}`;
  const wrap=(txt,maxW)=>{const out=[];String(txt||'').split('\n').forEach(par=>{let ln='';par.split(/\s+/).forEach(w=>{const tt=ln?ln+' '+w:w;if(x.measureText(tt).width>maxW&&ln){out.push(ln);ln=w}else ln=tt});out.push(ln)});return out};
  const rr=(X,Y,w,hh,r,fill)=>{x.beginPath();x.roundRect?x.roundRect(X,Y,w,hh,r):x.rect(X,Y,w,hh);x.fillStyle=fill;x.fill()};
  const fit=(txt,maxW)=>{let s2=String(txt);if(x.measureText(s2).width<=maxW)return s2;while(s2.length>1&&x.measureText(s2+'…').width>maxW)s2=s2.slice(0,-1);return s2+'…'};
  // faixa superior
  x.fillStyle=C.ink;x.fillRect(0,0,W,190);x.fillStyle='#e30613';x.fillRect(P,58,10,74);
  font(600,34,MON);const dt=fdate(t.data),dw=x.measureText(dt).width+40;rr(W-P-dw,64,dw,62,31,'#ffffff');x.fillStyle=C.ink;x.fillText(dt,W-P-dw+20,107);
  let ts=70;font(700,ts,DSP);while(ts>40&&x.measureText('CARTAO PREVENTIVA').width>W-2*P-36-dw-24){ts-=2;font(700,ts,DSP)}
  x.fillStyle=C.inkFg;x.fillText('CARTAO PREVENTIVA',P+34,118);
  font(500,28,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.inkMuted;x.fillText('Rondaflex Ltda · Controle de Frota',P+36,158);
  let y=190+118;
  // identificacao
  font(600,92,MON);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText(e.codigo||'—',P,y);
  y+=58;font(600,36,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText(fit(e.tipo||'—',W-2*P),P,y);
  const mm=[e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' ');if(mm){y+=46;font(400,32,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText(fit(mm,W-2*P),P,y)}
  const loc=e.alocacao?alocTxt(e):'';if(loc){y+=46;font(400,30,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText('Alocado em '+loc,P,y)}
  // servico
  y+=44;const sv=t.tipo==='filtro_ar'?`Filtro de ar · ${t.sub||''} · ${t.servico||''}`:'Preventiva';
  font(700,40,DSP);const sw=x.measureText(sv.toUpperCase()).width+36;rr(P,y,sw,58,12,C.sunk);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText(sv.toUpperCase(),P+18,y+42);
  y+=58+28;
  // numeros
  const boxes=[['Horimetro',nf(h)+' h']];
  if(prox!=null)boxes.push(['Proxima',nf(prox)+' h']);
  if(iv)boxes.push(['Intervalo',nf(iv)+' h']);
  const gap=18,bw=(W-2*P-gap*(boxes.length-1))/boxes.length,bh=150;
  boxes.forEach(([k,v],i)=>{const X=P+i*(bw+gap);rr(X,y,bw,bh,20,i===1?C.ink:C.sunk);
    font(600,24,SAN);x.fillStyle=i===1?C.inkMuted:C.mut;x.fillText(fit(k.toUpperCase(),bw-40),X+22,y+46);
    font(700,64,DSP);x.fillStyle=i===1?'#ffffff':C.fg;x.fillText(fit(v,bw-40),X+22,y+118)});
  y+=bh;
  if(trocasHm.length){y+=24;const ln=trocasHm.map(x=>`Horimetro trocado em ${fdate(x.data)}: relogio antigo ${nf(x.antigo)} h → novo ${nf(x.horimetro)} h`);
    const hh=30+ln.length*40+40;rr(P,y,W-2*P,hh,18,'#fff4cc');x.fillStyle='#8a5a00';font(600,27,SAN);
    ln.forEach((l,i)=>x.fillText(fit(l,W-2*P-44),P+22,y+44+i*40));font(400,25,SAN);x.fillText(fit('A proxima esta no relogio atual do equipamento.',W-2*P-44),P+22,y+44+ln.length*40);y+=hh}
  if(falta!=null){y+=26;const txt=falta>0?`Faltam ${nf(falta)} h · horimetro atual ${nf(cur)} h`:`Vencida ha ${nf(-falta)} h · horimetro atual ${nf(cur)} h`;
    font(600,30,SAN);const tw=x.measureText(txt).width+44;rr(P,y,tw,56,28,falta>0?'#e5f3ea':'#fbe4e1');x.fillStyle=falta>0?C.ok:C.bad;x.fillText(txt,P+22,y+38);y+=56}
  // itens
  const its=itensInforme(t);
  y+=64;font(700,38,DSP);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText('MATERIAL APLICADO',P,y);
  font(600,30,MON);x.fillStyle=C.mut;const nIt=String(its.length);x.fillText(nIt,W-P-x.measureText(nIt).width,y);
  y+=20;x.fillStyle=C.acc;x.fillRect(P,y,W-2*P,4);y+=4;
  if(!its.length){y+=60;font(400,30,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText('Nenhum item informado.',P,y);y+=24}
  its.forEach((it,i)=>{const q=it.qtd!==''&&it.qtd!=null&&Number(it.qtd)?`${nf(it.qtd,1)} ${it.un||''}`.trim():'';
    font(600,30,MON);const qw=q?x.measureText(q).width:0;
    font(600,34,SAN);const dl=wrap(itNome(it),W-2*P-qw-40);
    let yy=y+56;dl.forEach((l,j)=>{x.fillStyle=C.fg;font(600,34,SAN);x.fillText(l,P,yy);if(j===0&&q){font(600,30,MON);x.fillStyle=C.fg;x.fillText(q,W-P-qw,yy)}yy+=44});
    if(itCod(it)){font(500,28,MON);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText(fit(itCod(it),W-2*P),P,yy-4);yy+=38}
    y=yy-10;if(i<its.length-1){x.fillStyle=C.line;x.fillRect(P,y,W-2*P,2)}});
  // observacoes e responsavel
  const extra=[];if(t.obs)extra.push(['Observacoes',t.obs]);if(t.resp)extra.push(['Responsavel',t.resp]);
  extra.forEach(([k,v])=>{y+=58;font(700,32,DSP);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText(k.toUpperCase(),P,y);font(400,32,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.fg;wrap(v,W-2*P).forEach(l=>{y+=46;x.fillText(l,P,y)})});
  // rodape
  y+=64;x.fillStyle=C.line;x.fillRect(P,y,W-2*P,2);y+=48;font(400,24,SAN);x.fillStyle=C.mut;x.fillText(`Gerado em ${fdate(today())} pelo Controle de Frota`,P,y);y+=P-8;
  const out=document.createElement('canvas');out.width=W;out.height=Math.ceil(y);out.getContext('2d').drawImage(cv,0,0);
  return out;
}
function openCartao(id){
  nav('card:'+id,()=>openCartao(id));
  const t=S.trocas.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!t){goBack();return}const e=eqById(t.equipId);
  const fname=`Cartao preventiva ${e?e.codigo:''} ${t.data||''}.png`.replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  openSheet({title:'Cartao preventiva',sub:`${e?esc(e.codigo)+' · ':''}${fdate(t.data)}`,
  body:`<div id="cd-box" class="card-wait">Montando o cartao…</div><p class="note" style="margin:10px 2px 0">Imagem pronta para enviar no WhatsApp, e-mail ou grupo da equipe.</p>`,
  foot:`<button type="button" class="btn pri" id="cd-go" disabled>${I.share}Compartilhar</button>`,
  mount:async sh=>{
    const cv=await drawCartao(t);const box=sh.querySelector('#cd-box');if(!box.isConnected)return;
    box.className='';box.innerHTML=`<img class="card-prev" alt="Cartao preventiva de ${esc(e?e.codigo:'')}" src="${cv.toDataURL('image/png')}">`;
    const blob=await new Promise(r=>cv.toBlob(r,'image/png'));const go=sh.querySelector('#cd-go');go.disabled=false;
    go.onclick=()=>shareImg(cv,fname,'Cartao preventiva');
  }});
}

/* ---------- kits ---------- */
function kitView(k){
  const id=k.id,c=stockCls(k),d=kitDisp(k),cs=kitComps(k);
  const lim=cs.filter(x=>Number(x.c.qtd)>0&&Math.floor((Number(x.s.qtd)||0)/Number(x.c.qtd)+1e-9)===d).map(x=>stCod(x.s));
  const used=S.equips.filter(e=>(e.itens||[]).some(i=>i.estoqueId===id)).sort(byCode).map(e=>e.codigo);
  openSheet({title:esc(k.desc),sub:`<span class="num">${esc(stCod(k))}</span> · Kit · ${cs.length} ${cs.length===1?'item':'itens'}`,
