  body:`<div class="hmcard"><div><div class="k">Kits disponiveis</div><div class="v">${nf(d)}<small>${d===1?'kit':'kits'}</small></div><div class="d">${cs.length?`Limitado por ${esc(lim.join(', '))}`:'Kit sem itens'} · minimo ${nf(k.minimo,1)}</div></div><span class="pill ${c}">${c==='bad'?'Falta item':c==='warn'?'Repor':'Ok'}</span></div>
  <div class="lbl">Itens do kit</div><div class="es-l">${cs.map(({c:x,s})=>{const falta=(Number(s.qtd)||0)<Number(x.qtd);return esRow(s,{tag:'div',cls:falta?'bad':undefined,sub:`${esc(stCod(s))} · ${nf(x.qtd,1)} ${esc(s.un||'')} por kit`})}).join('')||'<div class="empty">Nenhum item no kit.</div>'}</div>
  <p class="note" style="margin:8px 2px 0">O saldo fica nos itens. Cada kit usado numa preventiva da baixa nessas quantidades.</p>
  <div class="lbl">Usado em</div><div class="spec"><div class="r"><span>Equipamentos</span><b>${esc(used.join(', ')||'Nenhum ligado')}</b></div></div>
  ${fotoStHTML(k)}`,
  mount:sh=>bindFotoSt(sh)});
}
function formKit(id){
  nav('fk:'+(id||'novo'),()=>formKit(id));
  const k=id?stById(id):{kit:true,comps:[],minimo:1};
  const its=S.estoque.filter(s=>!isKit(s)).sort(byCode);
  if(!its.length){NAV.pop();toast('Cadastre os filtros e oleos antes de montar um kit.');return}
  /* itens do kit: busca por codigo ou referencia (ou camera); rascunho guardado ao sair para cadastrar um item novo */
  const dr=formKit.draft&&formKit.draft.key===(id||'novo')?formKit.draft:null;formKit.draft=null;
  const C=(dr?dr.comps:(k.comps||[])).map(c=>({id:c.id,qtd:c.qtd??1}));
  openSheet({title:id?'Editar kit':'Novo kit',sub:id?esc(k.codigo):'Junte varios itens do estoque em um so',
  body:`<form id="fk"><div class="fset"><div class="grid">
    <label class="f">Codigo do kit<input id="k-codigo" required value="${esc(k.codigo||'')}" placeholder="Ex.: KIT-250" autocapitalize="characters"></label>
    <label class="f">Minimo de kits<input id="k-min" type="number" step="1" min="0" inputmode="numeric" value="${esc(k.minimo??1)}"></label>
    <label class="f w">Descricao<input id="k-desc" required value="${esc(k.desc||'')}" placeholder="Ex.: Preventiva 250 h · Caminhao"></label></div></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Encaixe de fabrica</h3><p class="note" style="margin:0">Equipamentos em que este kit encaixa. Ele aparece sozinho no Registrar preventiva e na ficha de cada um.</p>
      <div class="tsec-l enc-sel" id="k-enc"></div>
      <div class="search">${I.search}<input id="k-encq" type="search" placeholder="Adicionar: TAG, modelo ou nº de serie" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Buscar equipamento"></div><div class="tsec-l enc-res" id="k-encr" hidden></div></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Itens do kit</h3><div class="sug-w t-add"><div class="search">${I.search}<input id="k-q" type="search" placeholder="Codigo ou referencia do item" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" aria-label="Adicionar item"></div><div class="sug" id="k-qh"></div></div>
      ${rdBtn('k-cam','Adicionar item pela camera')}<div id="k-comps"></div></div>
    <div class="fset" style="margin-top:12px"><h3>Fotos (opcional, ate 3)</h3><div id="k-ph"></div></div>
    ${atBox('kit',id?stById(id):null)}<div id="delbox"></div></form>`,
  foot:`<button class="btn pri" form="fk">Salvar kit</button>`,
  mount:sh=>{if(id)atBind(sh,'kit',id);
    const fotoOrig=[...(k.fotos||[])],fotoNow=photoEditor(sh.querySelector('#k-ph'),dr?dr.fotos.slice():[...fotoOrig],3,true);
    if(dr){sh.querySelector('#k-codigo').value=dr.codigo;sh.querySelector('#k-min').value=dr.min;sh.querySelector('#k-desc').value=dr.desc}
    /* encaixe de fabrica do kit: um ou varios equipamentos */
    let enc=[...(dr&&dr.enc?dr.enc:(k.encaixe||[]))].filter(eqById);const encBox=sh.querySelector('#k-enc'),encQ=sh.querySelector('#k-encq'),encR=sh.querySelector('#k-encr');
    const eqLine=e=>[e.tipo,[e.marca,e.modelo].filter(Boolean).join(' '),e.serie?'serie '+e.serie:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    const drawEnc=()=>{encBox.innerHTML=enc.slice().sort((a,b)=>byCode(eqById(a),eqById(b))).map(i=>{const e=eqById(i);return `<div class="tq"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc(eqLine(e))}</span><button type="button" class="btn sm" data-encrm="${esc(i)}">Tirar</button></div>`}).join('');encBox.hidden=!enc.length;
      encBox.querySelectorAll('[data-encrm]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{enc=enc.filter(x=>x!==b.dataset.encrm);drawEnc()})};drawEnc();
    const nzE=v=>String(v||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]/g,'');
    encQ.oninput=()=>{const qq=nzE(encQ.value);if(!qq){encR.hidden=true;return}
      const hits=S.eqCtl.filter(e=>!enc.includes(e.id)&&nzE([e.codigo,e.marca,e.modelo,e.serie,e.tipo].join(' ')).includes(qq)).sort(byCode).slice(0,8);
      encR.hidden=false;encR.innerHTML=hits.map(e=>`<button type="button" class="tq" data-encadd="${esc(e.id)}"><span class="tq-c">${esc(e.codigo)}</span><span class="tq-m">${esc(eqLine(e))}</span>${I.plus}</button>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">Nenhum equipamento encontrado.</div>';
      encR.querySelectorAll('[data-encadd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{enc.push(b.dataset.encadd);encQ.value='';encR.hidden=true;drawEnc()})};
    const box=sh.querySelector('#k-comps');
    const draw=()=>{box.innerHTML=C.length?`<div class="kl">${C.map((c,i)=>{const x=stById(c.id);return `<div class="kr"><span class="n"><b>${esc(x?x.desc:'Item removido')}</b><small class="num">${esc(x?stCod(x):'')}</small></span><input type="number" step="0.1" min="0" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(c.qtd)}" data-kq="${i}" aria-label="Quantidade por kit"><span class="u">${esc(x?stUn(x):'')}</span><button type="button" class="x" data-kx="${i}" aria-label="Tirar do kit">✕</button></div>`}).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="note" style="margin:10px 2px 0">Adicione pelo menos 2 itens.</p>';
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-kq]').forEach(q=>q.oninput=()=>{C[Number(q.dataset.kq)].qtd=q.value});
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-kx]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{C.splice(Number(b.dataset.kx),1);draw()});
      /* item recem-cadastrado ainda chegando: redesenha quando aparecer */
      if(C.some(c=>!stById(c.id))&&(draw.n=(draw.n||0)+1)<10)setTimeout(()=>{if(document.body.contains(box))draw()},500)};
    const add=x=>{if(!x||isKit(x)){toast('Escolha um filtro ou oleo do estoque.');return}if(C.some(c=>c.id===x.id)){toast(`${x.desc} ja esta no kit`);return}C.push({id:x.id,qtd:1});draw()};
    const q=sh.querySelector('#k-q'),qh=sh.querySelector('#k-qh');
    q.oninput=()=>{const kk=nzCod(q.value);if(!kk){qh.innerHTML='';return}
      /* TAG de equipamento: mostra os itens com encaixe de fabrica nele */
      const eqT=S.equips.find(e=>nzCod(e.codigo)===kk);
      if(eqT){const l=encaixeDe(eqT.id).filter(x=>!isKit(x));
        qh.innerHTML=`<div class="ti-g k-tg">Encaixe em ${esc(eqT.codigo)} · ${l.length} ${l.length===1?'item':'itens'}</div>`+(l.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${l.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-kp="${esc(x.id)}"`,cls2:C.some(c=>c.id===x.id)?'k-in':'',end:C.some(c=>c.id===x.id)?'<span class="k-ok">no kit</span>':''})).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum item com encaixe neste equipamento.</div>');
        qh.querySelectorAll('[data-kp]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{add(stById(b.dataset.kp));q.oninput()});return}
      const sc=x=>{const a=nzCod(stCod(x)),b=nzCod(x.ref),c2=nzCod(x.codigo);return a===kk||b===kk||c2===kk?0:a.startsWith(kk)||b.startsWith(kk)||c2.startsWith(kk)?1:a.includes(kk)||b.includes(kk)||c2.includes(kk)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(kk)?2:9};
      const m=its.map(x=>[sc(x),x]).filter(a=>a[0]<9).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||byCode(a[1],b[1])).slice(0,6).map(a=>a[1]),v=q.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      qh.innerHTML=(m.length?`<div class="tsec-l">${m.map(x=>esRow(x,{attr:`data-kp="${esc(x.id)}"`})).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="eqs-v">Nenhum item encontrado no estoque.</div>')+`<button type="button" class="et-fora" id="k-cad" style="color:var(--accent,#0033a0)">Cadastrar item ${esc(v)}</button>`;
      qh.querySelectorAll('[data-kp]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{q.value='';qh.innerHTML='';add(stById(b.dataset.kp))});
      qh.querySelector('#k-cad').onclick=()=>{const d={key:id||'novo',comps:C.map(c=>({...c})),enc:enc.slice(),fotos:fotoNow.slice(),codigo:sh.querySelector('#k-codigo').value,min:sh.querySelector('#k-min').value,desc:sh.querySelector('#k-desc').value};
        q.value='';qh.innerHTML='';formKit.draft=d;const cb=async sid=>{d.comps.push({id:sid,qtd:1})};cb.msg='Item cadastrado';formStock(null,{ref:v},cb)}};
    draw();
    const kc=sh.querySelector('#k-cam');if(kc)kc.onclick=()=>rdOpen({mode:'item',use:v=>{if(!v.item){toast('Item nao encontrado no estoque.');return}add(v.item)}});
    sh.querySelector('#fk').onsubmit=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();
      const m={};C.forEach(c=>{const qq=Number(c.qtd)||0;if(c.id&&qq>0&&stById(c.id))m[c.id]=(m[c.id]||0)+qq});
      const comps=Object.entries(m).map(([cid,q])=>({id:cid,qtd:Math.round(q*10)/10}));
      if(comps.length<2){toast('Um kit precisa de pelo menos 2 itens com quantidade.');return}
      const keep=new Set(comps.map(c=>c.id));
      const rem=[...(k.comps||[]).filter(c=>!keep.has(c.id)).map(c=>{const s=stById(c.id);return s?`${stCod(s)} do kit`:'item do kit'}),...fotoOrig.filter(x=>!fotoNow.includes(x)).map(()=>'foto'),...(k.encaixe||[]).filter(x=>!enc.includes(x)&&eqById(x)).map(x=>`encaixe em ${eqById(x).codigo}`)];
      confirmRemovals(sh.querySelector('#delbox'),rem,()=>save(comps))};
    const save=async comps=>{const g=n=>sh.querySelector('#k-'+n).value.trim();
      const body={kit:true,tipo:'kit',codigo:g('codigo').toUpperCase(),desc:g('desc'),minimo:Number(g('min'))||0,un:'kit',comps,encaixe:enc,fotos:fotoNow.slice(0,3),...(k.inativo?{inativo:true}:{})};
      if(S.estoque.some(x=>x.id!==id&&String(x.codigo).toLowerCase()===body.codigo.toLowerCase())){toast('Ja existe um item ou kit com esse codigo.');return}
      const ref=id?S.db.doc('estoque/'+id):S.db.collection('estoque').doc();
      if(await safe(()=>ref.set(body),id?'Kit atualizado':'Kit montado')){commitPhotos(fotoOrig,fotoNow);id?goBack():closeSheet()}}}});
}

/* ---------- exemplos ---------- */
async function clearExamples(){
  await safe(async()=>{
    for(const t of S.trocas.filter(x=>x.exemplo))await S.db.doc('trocas/'+t.id).delete();
    for(const e of S.equips.filter(x=>x.exemplo))await S.db.doc('equipamentos/'+e.id).delete();
    for(const s of S.estoque.filter(x=>x.exemplo))await S.db.doc('estoque/'+s.id).delete();
  },'Exemplos apagados');
}

/* com o teclado aberto, o campo de busca sobe para o topo e as sugestoes ficam visiveis logo abaixo */
document.addEventListener('focusin',ev=>{const t=ev.target;if(!t.matches||!t.matches('#mv-c,#s-idq,.ct-c,#esq,#pe-q,#pd-q,#pq,.eqs-in,#t-q,#k-q'))return;
  setTimeout(()=>{if(document.activeElement===t)t.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'})},320)});
/* ---------- leitor pela camera (so no app baixavel): codigo de barras, QR ou texto ----------
   Um botao de camera aparece dentro dos campos onde se informa um item do estoque.
   Codigos sao lidos na hora; texto e lido sozinho depois de ~2 s sem codigo, ou em "Ler texto agora". */
const CAM_OK=!!(window.__PWA&&navigator.mediaDevices&&navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia);
const RD_IC='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 8h3l2-3h6l2 3h3v11H4z"/><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="3.5"/></svg>';
/* botao "Ler com a camera" numa linha propria, logo abaixo do campo de identificacao */
const rdBtn=(id,txt)=>CAM_OK?`<button type="button" class="btn block rd-row" id="${id}">${RD_IC}${txt||'Ler com a camera'}</button>`:'';
const rdCanon=s=>String(s||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g,'').replace(/O/g,'0').replace(/[IL]/g,'1').replace(/S/g,'5').replace(/B/g,'8').replace(/Z/g,'2');
const rdQuase=(t,k)=>{if(k.length<7||t.length<k.length)return false;for(let i=0;i+k.length<=t.length;i++){let d=0;for(let j=0;j<k.length&&d<2;j++)if(t[i+j]!==k[j])d++;if(d<=1)return true}return false};
function rdKeys(){return S.estoque.map(s=>({s,keys:[s.codigo,s.ref,...(s.eans||[])].filter(Boolean).map(rdCanon).filter(k=>k.length>=4)}))}
function rdFind1(txt){const t=rdCanon(txt);if(t.length<4)return {lista:[],aprox:false};const its=rdKeys();
  const ex=its.filter(x=>x.keys.some(k=>t.includes(k)||(k.includes(t)&&t.length>=6)));if(ex.length)return {lista:ex.map(x=>x.s),aprox:false};
  const ap=its.filter(x=>x.keys.some(k=>rdQuase(t,k)));return {lista:ap.map(x=>x.s),aprox:ap.length>0}}
function rdFind(txt){const ps=String(txt).split(/[|;\n]/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if(ps.length>1){for(const p of ps){const r=rdFind1(p);if(r.lista.length&&!r.aprox)return r}for(const p of ps){const r=rdFind1(p);if(r.lista.length)return r}return {lista:[],aprox:false}}
  return rdFind1(txt)}
/* referencia de peca dentro do que foi lido (ex.: CAT 222-9021, ou "2229021.000" dentro do QR) */
function rdRef(txt){const t=String(txt);let m=t.match(/\b(\d{3})-?(\d{4})\b/);if(m)return m[1]+'-'+m[2];
  for(const p of t.split(/[|;\n]/)){const q=p.trim().match(/^(\d{3})(\d{4})(\.0+)?$/);if(q)return q[1]+'-'+q[2]}
  if(/^\d{8,}$/.test(t.trim()))return '';
  const tk=t.toUpperCase().split(/[\s|;]+/).map(x=>x.replace(/[^A-Z0-9.\-\/]/g,'')).filter(x=>x.length>=4&&/\d/.test(x)&&!/^\d{8,}$/.test(x));return tk[0]||''}
let RD=null;
function rdOpen(o){if(RD)return;RD={...o,last:'',lastCode:0,busy:false,ocrBusy:false,stopped:false};
  const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='rdr';d.setAttribute('role','dialog');d.setAttribute('aria-label','Ler item');
  d.innerHTML=`<div class="rdr-h"><button type="button" class="backbtn" data-x aria-label="Voltar">${I.back}</button><div class="sh-tt"><h2>Ler item</h2><div class="sub">Codigo de barras, QR ou texto</div></div></div>
    <div class="rdr-b"><div class="rdr-cam"><video playsinline muted></video><div class="rdr-fr"></div><button type="button" class="rdr-lz" hidden aria-label="Lanterna"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M9 2h6l-1 6h-4zM10 8h4v12a2 2 0 0 1-4 0z"/></svg></button><div class="rdr-st">Abrindo a camera…</div></div>
    <div class="rdr-r" hidden></div><div class="rdr-a"><button type="button" class="btn" data-now>Ler texto agora</button><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-x>Digitar em vez de ler</button></div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(d);RD.el=d;d.querySelectorAll('[data-x]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=rdClose);d.querySelector('[data-now]').onclick=()=>rdOcr(true);
  rdStart()}
function rdClose(){if(!RD)return;RD.stopped=true;cancelAnimationFrame(RD.raf);clearTimeout(RD.t);if(RD.stream)RD.stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());RD.el.remove();RD=null}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden&&RD)rdClose()});
async function rdStart(){const r=RD,v=r.el.querySelector('video'),st=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-st');
  try{r.stream=await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({video:{facingMode:{ideal:'environment'},width:{ideal:1920},height:{ideal:1080}},audio:false})}
  catch(e){st.textContent='Sem acesso a camera. Libere a camera para o app nas configuracoes do celular.';return}
  if(r.stopped){r.stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());return}
  const tr=r.stream.getVideoTracks()[0];r.track=tr;
  try{const c=tr.getCapabilities?tr.getCapabilities():{};if(c.focusMode&&c.focusMode.includes('continuous'))await tr.applyConstraints({advanced:[{focusMode:'continuous'}]});
    if(c.torch){const lz=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-lz');lz.hidden=false;let on=false;lz.onclick=async()=>{on=!on;try{await tr.applyConstraints({advanced:[{torch:on}]})}catch(e){}}}}catch(e){}
  v.srcObject=r.stream;await v.play().catch(()=>{});st.textContent='Aponte para o codigo ou enquadre o texto';r.lastCode=performance.now();
  try{if('BarcodeDetector' in window)r.det=new BarcodeDetector({formats:await BarcodeDetector.getSupportedFormats()})}catch(e){}
  let lt=0;const loop=()=>{r.raf=requestAnimationFrame(async ts=>{if(r.stopped)return;loop();if(!r.det||r.busy||r.paused||ts-lt<180||v.readyState<2)return;r.busy=true;lt=ts;
    try{const c=(await r.det.detect(v))[0];if(c){r.lastCode=performance.now();if(c.rawValue!==r.last){r.last=c.rawValue;rdShow(c.rawValue,c.format==='qr_code'?'QR code':'codigo de barras')}}}catch(e){}r.busy=false})};loop();
  r.t=setTimeout(()=>rdOcr(false),2000)}
function rdCrop(v){const vw=v.videoWidth,vh=v.videoHeight,cw=v.clientWidth,ch=v.clientHeight;if(!vw)return null;const s=Math.max(cw/vw,ch/vh),ox=(vw*s-cw)/2,oy=(vh*s-ch)/2;
  const fx=(cw*.08+ox)/s,fy=(ch*.40+oy)/s,fw=cw*.84/s,fh=ch*.20/s,k=Math.min(2.2,1400/fw),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round(fw*k);c.height=Math.round(fh*k);
  const g=c.getContext('2d');g.filter='grayscale(1) contrast(1.6)';g.drawImage(v,fx,fy,fw,fh,0,0,c.width,c.height);return c}
let rdWorker=null;
async function rdOcrWorker(){if(rdWorker)return rdWorker;
  await new Promise((ok,ko)=>{if(window.Tesseract)return ok();const s=document.createElement('script');s.src='https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/tesseract.js@5.1.1/dist/tesseract.min.js';s.onload=ok;s.onerror=()=>ko(new Error('sem internet'));document.head.appendChild(s)});
  const w=await Tesseract.createWorker('eng');await w.setParameters({tessedit_char_whitelist:'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789-./ ',tessedit_pageseg_mode:'6'});return rdWorker=w}
async function rdOcr(forced){const r=RD;if(!r||r.stopped)return;clearTimeout(r.t);
  if(r.paused&&!forced){r.t=setTimeout(()=>rdOcr(false),2500);return}
  if(!forced&&performance.now()-r.lastCode<2000){r.t=setTimeout(()=>rdOcr(false),1200);return}
  if(r.ocrBusy)return;r.ocrBusy=true;const st=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-st'),nb=r.el.querySelector('[data-now]');nb.disabled=true;st.textContent=rdWorker?'Lendo texto…':'Preparando a leitura de texto…';
  try{const w=await rdOcrWorker();if(!RD||r.stopped)return;const c=rdCrop(r.el.querySelector('video'));
    if(c){const {data}=await w.recognize(c);if(!RD||r.stopped)return;
      const ls=(data.text||'').split('\n').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(x=>x.replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/gi,'').length>=4);let ok=false;
      for(const l of ls){if(rdFind(l).lista.length){rdShow(l,'texto');ok=true;break}}
      if(!ok&&ls.length&&(forced||(data.confidence||0)>=60))rdShow(ls[0],'texto');
      else if(!ok)st.textContent=ls.length?'Texto lido, sem item igual no estoque':'Nenhum texto no retangulo'}}
  catch(e){st.textContent='Leitura de texto indisponivel sem internet'}
  r.ocrBusy=false;if(RD&&!r.stopped){nb.disabled=false;r.t=setTimeout(()=>rdOcr(false),2500)}}
function rdShow(txt,tipo){const r=RD;if(!r)return;r.paused=true;const fr=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-fr'),st=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-st'),box=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-r'),act=r.el.querySelector('.rdr-a');
  try{navigator.vibrate&&navigator.vibrate(40)}catch(e){}
  const f=rdFind(txt),ref=rdRef(txt),lido=f.lista.length===1&&!f.aprox?stCod(f.lista[0]):(ref||txt);fr.classList.add('ok');
  const again=()=>{r.paused=false;r.last='';box.hidden=true;act.hidden=false;fr.classList.remove('ok');st.textContent='Aponte para o codigo ou enquadre o texto';r.lastCode=performance.now()};
  const done=v=>{const u=r.use;rdClose();u(v)};
  box.hidden=false;act.hidden=true;
  const row=s=>`<button type="button" class="rdr-it" data-rit="${esc(s.id)}">${esRow(s,{tag:'div'})}</button>`;
  if(r.mode==='raw'){st.textContent=tipo==='texto'?'Texto lido':'Codigo lido';
    box.innerHTML=`<div class="rdr-c"><span class="rdr-k">Lido · ${tipo}</span><b class="rdr-v num">${esc(lido)}</b>${f.lista.length?`<span class="rdr-k">${f.aprox?'Parecido com':'No estoque'}: ${f.lista.map(x=>esc(stCod(x)+' · '+x.desc)).join(', ')}</span>`:''}</div>
      <button type="button" class="btn pri" data-u>Usar ${esc(lido)}</button><button type="button" class="btn" data-a>Ler outro</button>`;
    box.querySelector('[data-u]').onclick=()=>done({text:lido});box.querySelector('[data-a]').onclick=again;return}
  if(f.lista.length){st.textContent='Item encontrado';
    box.innerHTML=`<div class="rdr-c"><span class="rdr-k">Lido · ${tipo}${f.aprox?' · leitura parecida':''}</span><b class="rdr-v num">${esc(txt.length>40?txt.slice(0,40)+'…':txt)}</b>${f.lista.length>1||f.aprox?'<span class="rdr-k">Toque no item certo</span>':''}<div class="rdr-l">${f.lista.map(row).join('')}</div></div>
      ${f.lista.length===1&&!f.aprox?'<button type="button" class="btn pri" data-u>Usar este item</button>':''}<button type="button" class="btn" data-a>Ler outro</button>`;
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-rit]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>done({item:stById(b.dataset.rit)}));const u=box.querySelector('[data-u]');if(u)u.onclick=()=>done({item:f.lista[0]});
    box.querySelector('[data-a]').onclick=again;return}
  /* nao esta no estoque: referencia → cadastrar em destaque; numero sem referencia → ligar em destaque */
  st.textContent=tipo==='texto'?'Texto lido':'Codigo lido';const code=tipo!=='texto'?txt:'';
  const bC=`<button type="button" class="btn ${ref?'pri':''}" data-c>Cadastrar item${ref?' '+esc(ref):''}</button>`,bL=code?`<button type="button" class="btn ${ref?'':'pri'}" data-l>Ligar a um item existente</button>`:'';
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="rdr-c"><span class="rdr-k">Lido · ${tipo}</span><b class="rdr-v num">${esc(ref||(txt.length>40?txt.slice(0,40)+'…':txt))}</b><div class="rdr-no">${ref?'Esta peca nao esta no estoque.':'Codigo ainda nao ligado a nenhum item.'}</div></div>
    ${ref?bC+bL:bL+bC}<button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-a>Ler outro</button>`;
  box.querySelector('[data-a]').onclick=again;
  box.querySelector('[data-c]').onclick=()=>{const u=r.use,pre={ref:ref||''};if(code&&!ref)pre.eans=[code];else if(code)pre.eans=[code];rdClose();
    const cb=async id=>{setTimeout(()=>{const x=stById(id);if(x)u({item:x})},350)};cb.msg='Item cadastrado';formStock(null,pre,cb)};
  const l=box.querySelector('[data-l]');if(l)l.onclick=()=>{
    box.innerHTML=`<div class="rdr-c"><span class="rdr-k">Ligar ${esc(code.length>24?code.slice(0,24)+'…':code)} a qual item?</span><div class="search">${I.search}<input type="search" data-q placeholder="Codigo, referencia ou descricao" autocomplete="off"></div><div class="rdr-l" data-ll></div></div><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-a>Cancelar</button>`;
    const q=box.querySelector('[data-q]'),ll=box.querySelector('[data-ll]');const draw=()=>{const k=nzCod(q.value);ll.innerHTML=S.estoque.filter(x=>!isKit(x)&&(!k||nzCod(x.codigo).includes(k)||nzCod(x.ref).includes(k)||nzCod(x.desc).includes(k))).sort(byCode).slice(0,30).map(row).join('')||'<p class="note" style="margin:6px 2px">Nenhum item.</p>';
      ll.querySelectorAll('[data-rit]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{const x=stById(b.dataset.rit);const eans=[...new Set([...(x.eans||[]),code])];
        if(await safe(()=>S.db.doc('estoque/'+x.id).update({eans}),'Codigo ligado a '+stCod(x)))done({item:{...x,eans}})})};
    q.oninput=draw;draw();box.querySelector('[data-a]').onclick=again}}

/* ---------- dados ---------- */
(async()=>{
  const db=window.claude&&window.claude.use?await window.claude.use('db'):null;
  window.claude&&window.claude.use&&window.claude.use('assets').then(a=>{S.assets=a;setTimeout(idLimpa,4000);if(sheetOpen)refreshSheet()}).catch(()=>{});
  S.db=db;const sy=$('#sync');
  if(!db){sy.textContent='Offline';sy.className='sync off';render();return}
  const sub=(col,key,lk)=>db.collection(col).onSnapshot(snap=>{const L0=snap.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()}));if(key==='equips'){S.equipsAll=L0;S[key]=L0.filter(e=>e.status!=='inativo');S.eqCtl=S[key].filter(e=>!soCad(e))}else if(key==='estoque'){S.estoqueAll=L0;S[key]=L0.filter(x=>!x.inativo)}else S[key]=L0;S.loaded[lk]=1;
      sy.textContent='Sincronizado';sy.className='sync on';if(!sheetOpen)render();else refreshSheet()},
    err=>{sy.textContent='Sem sincronia';sy.className='sync off';console.error(err)});
  db.doc('config/equivalencias').onSnapshot(d=>{S.equiv=(d.exists&&d.data().pares)||[];if(!sheetOpen)render();else refreshSheet()},err=>console.error(err));
  /* correcao pedida em 24/10: RE-04 veio zerada de fabrica e ainda nao tem preventiva -> conta do zero */
  const fixRE04=()=>{try{if(localStorage.getItem('frota.fixRE04'))return}catch(e){}if(!(S.loaded.e&&S.loaded.t)){setTimeout(fixRE04,2000);return}
    const e=eqById('RE-04');if(e&&!e.novo0&&semPrev(e)){const upd={novo0:true,itens:(e.itens||[]).map(i=>i.reprog?i:{...i,ultimaH:'',ultimaData:'',proxH:''})};db.doc('equipamentos/RE-04').update(upd).catch(()=>{})}
    try{localStorage.setItem('frota.fixRE04','1')}catch(e){}};setTimeout(fixRE04,2500);
  db.doc('config/listas').onSnapshot(d=>{applyCfg(d.exists?d.data():null);S.loaded.c=1;if(!sheetOpen)render();else refreshSheet()},err=>{S.loaded.c=1;console.error(err)});
  $('#fab').hidden=false;
  sub('equipamentos','equips','e');sub('trocas','trocas','t');sub('estoque','estoque','s');sub('listas','listas','l');
  if(window.__frotaEu){$('#bell').hidden=false;fitTop();
    db.collection('avisos').onSnapshot(sn=>{S.avisos=sn.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()}));pintaSino();if(sheetOpen&&/^(avs|av):/.test((NAV[NAV.length-1]||{}).key||''))refreshSheet()},err=>console.error(err));
    db.doc('config/equipe').onSnapshot(d=>{S.equipe=(d.exists&&d.data().pessoas)||[]},err=>console.error(err))}
  db.doc('config/compras').onSnapshot(d=>{S.cart=(d.exists&&d.data().itens)||[];cartBtn();if(!sheetOpen)render();else refreshSheet()},err=>console.error(err));
})();
})();

/* AJUSTE AUTOMATICO DE CAIXAS (vale para todo o app): numero ou texto que nao cabe na caixa de tamanho fixo
   1) se cabe, nada muda; 2) se nao cabe, a fonte reduz (ate 72%) e a caixa fica igual; 3) so se ainda nao couber, a caixa alarga o minimo necessario.
   Nunca corta nem troca por reticencias. */
const FIT_SEL='.es-b,.dday,.lt-c,.ef-sum>div,.ct-b';
/* conteudo centralizado pode vazar para os dois lados (o scrollWidth nao ve o lado esquerdo): compara as caixas de cada parte */
const fitOver=el=>{if(el.scrollWidth>el.clientWidth+1||el.scrollHeight>el.clientHeight+1)return true;const c=el.getBoundingClientRect(),pd=2;
  return [...el.querySelectorAll('*')].some(x=>{if(x instanceof SVGElement)return false;const r=x.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&(r.left<c.left+pd||r.right>c.right-pd)})};
function fitBox(el){const els=[el,...el.querySelectorAll('*')].filter(x=>!(x instanceof SVGElement));
  const base=els.map(x=>parseFloat(getComputedStyle(x).fontSize)||14);el.dataset.fit='1';
  for(const k of [.92,.85,.78,.72]){els.forEach((x,i)=>{x.style.fontSize=(base[i]*k).toFixed(1)+'px'});if(!fitOver(el))return}
  const w=el.getBoundingClientRect().width;el.style.minWidth=Math.ceil(w)+'px';el.style.width='auto';el.style.flex='0 0 auto';el.style.paddingLeft=el.style.paddingRight='6px';el.style.textOverflow='clip';el.style.overflow='visible'}
let fitT=0;function fitAll(){fitT=0;const l=[...document.querySelectorAll(FIT_SEL)].filter(x=>!x.dataset.fit&&x.offsetParent);const ov=l.filter(fitOver);ov.forEach(fitBox)}
new MutationObserver(()=>{if(!fitT)fitT=requestAnimationFrame(fitAll)}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden','aria-expanded','class']});
addEventListener('resize',()=>{document.querySelectorAll('[data-fit]').forEach(x=>{delete x.dataset.fit;['fontSize','minWidth','width','flex','paddingLeft','paddingRight','textOverflow','overflow'].forEach(p=>x.style[p]='');x.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(c=>{if(!(c instanceof SVGElement))c.style.fontSize=''})});if(!fitT)fitT=requestAnimationFrame(fitAll)});
</script>
